### [60] [Governing Agentic AI in FinTech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11344)
*Henry Han*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文聚焦金融科技中智能体AI（agentic AI）的治理问题，提出"可验证性差距"（Verifiability Gap）概念，指委托权威所需的验证与决策后保留的可解释性和可复现性之间的缺口。作者构建了一套多层级治理理论，并通过三项研究（涵盖9个模型版本，从30亿参数本地模型到商业前沿系统）进行验证。研究发现：模型提供者发布的控制措施会改变历史金融行为；编排（orchestration）是潜在的政策层；可复现性并非标量而是"治理画像"，从而提出"证据依存的委托授权"观点，即只有在保留的证据能够证实其行使时，权威才是正当的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构正将关键决策委托给智能体AI系统，这些系统能够分解目标、协调模型和工具并以极少监督自主行动。然而，FinTech中智能体AI的治理问题研究不足。作者认为治理的约束瓶颈不是能力而是可验证性，因此引入"可验证性差距"这一核心概念来系统化这一问题。

Method: 作者提出多层级智能体AI治理理论，并通过三项研究验证：研究1考察模型提供者发布的控制参数（temperature、top_p、top_k、随机种子）对可复现性的影响；研究2考察编排架构作为潜在政策层对最终行为和可复现性的影响；研究3对比两个确定性信贷模型版本的历史可复现性。实验覆盖从30亿参数本地模型到商业前沿系统的9个模型版本。

Result: 研究1：前沿模型拒绝temperature、top_p、top_k控制且不暴露随机种子；在最严格控制下，本地模型复现320/320次执行，托管模型复现319/320和959/960次。研究2：编排会改变最终行为，任何配置下均无重复执行记录；前沿模型自复现能力高于本地模型，但记录质量不更好，且失去相当比例的分化度——能力购买的是更高起点而非可审计性。研究3：两个确定性信贷模型版本均能完美复现当前行为，但当前版本无法恢复历史行为。

Conclusion: 可复现性应被视为"治理画像"而非单一标量，由此引出"证据依存的委托授权"：只有当保留的证据能支撑权威行使时，委托才是正当的。该框架不仅适用于金融领域，还可扩展到其他需要可审计性的高风险领域。

Abstract: Financial institutions are delegating consequential decisions to agentic AI systems that decompose goals, coordinate models and tools, and act with little oversight. Yet agentic AI governance in FinTech is under-investigated. We argue the binding governance constraint is not capability but verifiability. We define the Verifiability Gap as the shortfall between the verification delegated authority demands and the explainability and reproducibility retained after a decision. It is indexed to a verifier, evidentiary standard, and audit lag. We develop a multilevel governance theory for agentic AI and test its mechanisms in three studies over nine model versions, from a three-billion-parameter local model to a commercial frontier system. Study 1 shows that provider releases alter historical financial actions, and that the controls replay needs belong to the provider: the frontier model rejects temperature, top_p and top_k outright and exposes no random seed. Under the tightest controls each endpoint allows, a local model reproduced 320 of 320 executions, hosted models 319 of 320 and 959 of 960. Study 2 shows that orchestration is a latent policy layer. Architecture changes final actions, and no execution record repeated in any configuration at any scale. The frontier model reproduces its own actions more often than the local ones, its record no better, and loses a comparable share of its differentiation. Capability buys a higher starting point, not auditability. Study 3 shows two deterministic credit-model versions each reproduce their current action perfectly, yet the current cannot recover a historical one. We conceptualize reproducibility as a governance profile, not a scalar, yielding evidence-contingent delegation: authority is defensible only while retained evidence substantiates its exercise. Beyond finance, the framework extends to other high-stakes domains requiring auditability.

</details>


### [61] [Variable Selection in the Context of AI Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11251)
*Ivan Luciano Danesi,Chiara Frigerio,Fabio Maccaferri,Giorgio Alessandro Motta,Pietro Zecca*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI公平性问题，主张通过数学方法与哲学伦理和社会意识的跨学科整合来评估公平性。研究发现排除敏感或关键变量可能损害子群体间的公平性，而保留所有相关变量则有助于减少隐性偏见，从而符合欧盟AI法案等监管要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着欧盟AI法案等监管要求的出台，AI公平性日益重要。然而传统方法往往忽视哲学伦理和社会意识，特别是在变量选择过程中可能引入隐性偏见，影响不同子群体间的公平性。因此需要一种能整合数学方法、伦理考量与监管要求的评估框架。

Method: 提出一种数学评估方法，将数学方法论与伦理考量和监管要求对齐。核心策略是保留所有潜在相关变量，以支持更细粒度的公平性评估并减少隐性偏见，同时倡导跨学科协作以深入理解伦理和社会背景。

Result: 研究结果表明，排除敏感或关键变量可能损害子群体间的公平性；相反，保留所有相关变量可以减少隐性偏见。跨学科方法能为伦理影响和法规遵从提供更深入的洞察。

Conclusion: 通过将数学方法与伦理和社会意识相结合，可以实现更公平的结果和负责任的AI部署。这项研究强调了跨学科协作对于解决AI系统公平性问题以及符合欧盟AI法案目标（促进可信赖和公平的AI系统）的必要性。

Abstract: Fairness in AI systems has become more important with recent regulatory demands, such as the EU AI Act. Traditional approaches often do not take into account philosophical ethics and social awareness. Variable selection processes, in particular, can introduce implicit bias, affecting equity across different subgroups. We discuss a mathematical approach that evaluates fairness in AI, aligning mathematical methodologies with ethical considerations and regulatory requirements. Our aim is to advocate for interdisciplinary collaboration to address fairness, emphasizing the importance of understanding broader ethical and societal contexts. Our approach emphasizes maintaining all potentially relevant variables to allow for more granular fairness assessments and to reduce implicit bias. The findings suggest that the exclusion of sensitive or critical variables may compromise equity between subgroups. In contrast, retaining all relevant variables could reduce implicit bias. Thus, the interdisciplinary approach could provide deeper insight into the ethical implications and compliance with regulatory standards. By integrating a mathematical approach with ethical and social awareness, we suggest more equitable outcomes and responsible AI deployment. This work underscores the necessity of interdisciplinary collaboration in effectively addressing fairness in AI systems aligned with the objectives of the European Union's AI Act, which seeks to promote trustworthy and fair AI systems.

</details>


### [62] [Methodologies for Improving the Quality of AI Tutoring in K-12 Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11259)
*Tushar Udeshi,Anna Khazenzon,Kabir Khan,Nick Breen,RJ Corwin,Chris DiGiano,Kodi Weatherholtz,Marek Zaluski*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Khan Academy旗下Khanmigo AI辅导系统的实践，描述了用于衡量AI辅导质量和学生参与度的指标，以及围绕模型、提示词、个性化和智能体等方面开展的实验，并强调了能有效提升指标的关键变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLM是不透明的黑箱系统，无法预知其行为，因此需要严格的评估和实时实验来衡量每一次改动的影响，以确保AI辅导系统的质量。

Method: 在Khanmigo平台上建立了一套衡量AI辅导质量和学生参与度的指标体系，并通过A/B实验等方法，对模型选择、提示词设计、个性化策略和智能体架构等多个维度进行系统性实验与迭代。

Result: 识别出了能够显著提升指标的具体变革，涵盖模型升级、提示词优化、个性化增强和智能体改进等方面。

Conclusion: 实际部署和实验表明，持续的实验评估和指标驱动对于提升LLM驱动的AI辅导系统质量至关重要。

Abstract: Many AI tutors leverage large language models (LLMs) today. Given that LLMs are opaque black boxes, robust evaluation and live experimentation to measure the impact of every change are essential. We pioneered AI-powered tutoring for K-12 with the launch of Khanmigo (Khan Academy, 2023). We describe the metrics we use to measure AI tutoring quality and student engagement as well as various experiments we have run. We highlight the changes that have moved our metrics, including models, prompting, personalization and agents.

</details>


### [63] [The Accuracy Trap: Structural Scarcity Amplifies Relative Inequality in Algorithmic Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11491)
*Erina Seh-Young Moon,Matthew Tamura,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文揭示算法排序系统在结构性稀缺资源分配中会产生"精确度陷阱"：稀缺性和准确性的乘积效应导致组间差异呈指数级放大，且去偏无法消除该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性研究将差异归因于有偏数据或模型缺陷，但忽视了在需求远超供给的结构性稀缺场景下，排序本质上变成配给问题，其统计特性与分类问题截然不同。

Method: 推导出缩放定律 D ∝ exp(t·ρ·Δ)，其中D为组间相对差异，Δ为结构性差距，t为稀缺引发的阈值，ρ为排序判别保真度；并通过蒙特卡洛模拟及加拿大儿童福利与美国癌症照护两个真实公共系统数据进行验证。

Result: 稀缺性与准确性呈乘性交互，导致组间差异呈指数级增长。蒙特卡洛模拟和两个独立公共部门系统均证实了"精确度陷阱"的存在。

Conclusion: 仅靠去偏算法无法解除精确度陷阱，需要重新审视公平性框架，将稀缺资源的配给问题作为核心考量。

Abstract: Algorithmic systems increasingly rank individuals for access to scarce public resources, from child welfare interventions to cancer treatment referrals. The prevailing fairness frame treats disparity as a property of biased data or deficient models, with remedies through calibration and debiasing. Under structural scarcity, where demand exceeds supply by an order of magnitude, allocation becomes a rationing problem, and the statistical properties of ranking diverge sharply from those of classification. We derive a scaling law $D \propto \exp(t \cdot ρ\cdot Δ)$, in which relative disparity between two groups separated by a structural gap $Δ$ grows in the product of the scarcity-induced threshold $t$ and rank-discrimination fidelity $ρ$. Scarcity and accuracy interact multiplicatively, producing exponentially larger between-group disparities. We term this dynamic the Accuracy Trap. We validate this Accuracy Trap through Monte Carlo simulation and two independent public-sector systems in Canadian child welfare and U.S. cancer care. Debiasing alone cannot dissolve the trap.

</details>


### [64] [Cheap, Fallible Cognition and the Political Economy of Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11512)
*Christophe Kolb,Jim Caron*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于任务和制度框架的分析方法，用于考察生成式AI作为廉价、可扩展但易出错认知对劳动力市场的影响，强调岗位不是不可分割的对象，AI对劳动力的替代效应取决于暴露度、采纳条件、验证机制、工作流程再设计及制度安排等多重边际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AI是否会摧毁就业"这一粗粒度问题无法指导经济分析与制度设计——岗位并非不可分割的整体，机器认知也并非人类劳动的同质替代品，需要更精细的分析框架。

Method: 构建任务导向且基于制度的经济学框架：引入任务脆弱性指数与采纳条件（明确验证、责任、信任和治理）；将职业建模为治理组合而非任务列表；将企业视为分布式智能架构；将劳动力市场效应视为任务压缩、规模扩张、新人类工作与制度议价之间的平衡。

Result: 关键推论：当答案生成变得充裕时，稀缺人力资本向上下游转移——提出有意义的经济问题、框定问题、生成假设、解释结果并承担后果责任。核心动态担忧是专业技能形成：初级任务同时产生当前产出、问题意识和未来判断力，其自动化虽提高短期生产率，却可能削弱问责型专业技能的人才输送管道，除非AI被设计为"教学"而非"绕过"。

Conclusion: AI的劳动力市场命运既非机械性失业也非自动丰裕，而是一个由工作流程设计、学徒制度、责任规则、竞争政策、工人话语权和廉价认知的租金分配共同塑造的制度均衡。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial intelligence will "destroy jobs" is too coarse to guide economic analysis or institutional design. A job is not an indivisible object, and machine cognition is not a uniform substitute for human labor. This paper develops a task-based and institutionally grounded framework for analyzing generative AI as cheap, scalable, and fallible cognition. The relevant margins are exposure, adoption, verification, question selection, workflow redesign, demand elasticity, apprenticeship, and rent allocation. We distinguish the technical reach of large language models from equilibrium labor-market displacement by introducing a task vulnerability index and an adoption condition that makes verification, liability, trust, and governance explicit. We then model occupations as governance bundles rather than task lists, firms as architectures of distributed intelligence, and labor-market effects as a balance among task compression, scale expansion, new human work, and institutional bargaining. A further implication is that when answer generation becomes abundant, the scarce human capital shifts upstream and downstream: toward asking economically meaningful questions, framing problems, generating hypotheses, interpreting results, and bearing responsibility for consequential use. The central dynamic concern is expertise formation: Junior tasks jointly produce current output, question sense, and future judgment, so their automation can raise short-run productivity while weakening the pipeline into accountable expertise unless AI is designed to teach rather than merely bypass. The paper concludes that AI's labor-market destiny is neither mechanical unemployment nor automatic abundance. It is an institutional equilibrium shaped by workflow design, apprenticeship systems, liability rules, competition policy, worker voice, and the distribution of rents from cheap cognition.

</details>


### [65] [Toward Meaningful Transparency for AI Chatbots: Disclosing Persuasive Intent Reduces Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11794)
*Adrian Rauchfleisch,Andreas Jungherr*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在一项预注册实验中，1500名英国成年人与聊天机器人就60个政策议题进行对话。研究发现，仅披露AI身份对聊天机器人的说服力几乎没有影响（态度转变13.1分 vs 对照组12.6分），但额外披露其说服意图和指令则将使说服效果减半（6.3分），同时降低参与者对宣传方法可接受性的评价并增强对其加强处罚的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管机构要求对AI内容来源和参与进行明确披露，但这些披露的实际效果尚不明确。作者旨在检验不同披露方式对AI聊天机器人说服力的影响，以填补关于透明度政策效果的实证空白。

Method: 采用预注册随机实验设计：1500名英国成年人参与，与一个内容完全相同的说服性聊天机器人就60个政策议题进行简短对话。参与者被随机分配到三组——无披露（对照组）、AI身份披露（T1）、AI身份加上说服意图及指令披露（T2）。

Result: 对照组态度转变12.6分（100分制）；AI身份披露组为13.1分，与对照组实际等价；而增加意图披露后态度转变降至6.3分，说服效果约减半。意图披露还使参与者认为该宣传方法更不可接受，并支持更严厉的处罚。

Conclusion: 在直接的聊天机器人互动中，仅披露AI身份不会对影响力产生实质性影响。现行监管侧重于系统"是什么"，但研究结果表明，对说服性AI的监管还必须涵盖系统"试图做什么"这一维度。

Abstract: The growing role of AI-generated content and AI-enabled systems in public communication has led regulators to demand clear disclosure of content provenance and AI involvement. But the effects of such disclosures remain uncertain. We test two disclosure approaches in their impact on an AI chatbot's persuasive appeal. In a preregistered experiment, 1,500 UK adults held a short conversation with a persuasive chatbot about one of 60 policy issues. The chatbot was identical for everyone. We randomized the disclosure that people received: nothing (control), a prominent disclosure that they were interacting with an AI (T1), or that disclosure plus the chatbot's persuasive intent and instructions (T2). The chatbot shifted attitudes by 12.6 points on a 100-point scale in the control group. The AI-identity disclosure was practically equivalent to no disclosure, with a 13.1-point shift, whereas the additional intent disclosure cut the persuasive effect roughly in half to 6.3 points. It also made participants view the campaign's methods as less acceptable and support stronger penalties against it. For direct chatbot interactions, transparency about AI identity alone does not meaningfully impact its influence. While current rules emphasize what a system is, our results show why the regulation of persuasive AI must also address what the system is trying to do.

</details>


### [66] [Silent Updates: Measuring and Closing the Post-Deployment Disclosure Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11803)
*Sophia Abraham,Ben Bucknall*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了部署后基础模型被静默更新（Silent Updates）的问题，发现目前没有任何API提供商或推理宿主发布了允许外部方验证所服务模型与文档中引用的模型是同一个的信息。作者提出了"Silent Updates Scorecard"（静默更新记分卡）用于衡量披露实践，以及一个"Three-Part Behavioral Trigger System"（三部分行为触发系统）来确定何时应触发披露或重新评估义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的基础模型并非静态系统，提供商可通过微调、分类器更新、系统提示修订等方式静默修改模型行为，且无需公开披露。这挑战了当前AI治理框架的核心假设——即外部可验证的"保管链"（chain of custody）将评估结果或系统卡引用的模型与用户实际使用的模型联系起来。

Method: 作者对九家第一方API提供商和七家第三方推理宿主进行了系统调查，分析其部署后披露实践（包括安全文档、定量评估和版本特定报告的发布情况）。基于调查结果，提出了两个工具：Silent Updates Scorecard（用于衡量跨提供商和宿主的部署后披露实践）和Three-Part Behavioral Trigger System（用于判定何时需要披露或重新评估）。

Result: 研究发现提供商普遍发布大量安全文档（包括定量评估和版本特定报告），但没有任何一家提供商发布信息允许外部方验证所服务的工件与文档中引用的是同一个。作者提供了九家第一方API提供商和七家第三方推理宿主的初步评分结果。

Conclusion: 当前实践中的"保管链"在外部可验证性方面存在显著缺口，需要通过标准化的记分卡工具和基于行为触发的披露机制来弥补这一治理空白。

Abstract: Deployed foundation models are often not static systems, with providers able to modify system behavior through fine-tuning, classifier updates, system prompt revisions, retrieval changes, and routing changes. These updates can be made silently -- that is, without public disclosure, a version increment, or re-evaluation. Such silent updates challenge a core assumption behind current AI governance frameworks that an externally verifiable chain of custody links the model referred to in evaluation results or a system card to the model served to users. In this paper, we examine post-deployment disclosure practices across first-party API providers and inference hosts to establish the extent to which a chain of custody exists in practice. We find that providers commonly publish substantial safety documentation, including quantitative evaluations and version-specific reports, but no provider in our sample published information allowing an external party to verify that the artifact being served is the same one referred to in this documentation. We introduce the Silent Updates Scorecard, a public instrument for measuring post-deployment disclosure practices across providers and hosts, and preliminary results for a sample of nine first-party API providers and seven third-party inference hosts. We also propose a Three-Part Behavioral Trigger System for determining when post-deployment modifications to a system motivate disclosure or re-evaluation obligations.

</details>


### [67] [Understanding Content Moderation in Large Language Models through Restricted Books: From Refusal to Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11806)
*Xucheng Yu,Emily Knox,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（40,800个查询-响应对、400本书、17种提示词设计、6个前沿模型）研究LLM如何处理敏感内容。关键发现是"零拒绝"现象：现代LLM对受限书籍的拒绝率仅0.07%，差异化体现在警告语言（+8-15个百分点）和犹豫标记（+2-5 pp）上，表明内容政策已从二元拒绝转向校准的、上下文敏感的披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM进入日常信息管道，理解它们如何处理敏感话题与理解它们是否能处理这些话题同样重要。现有研究主要关注越狱（jailbreaking），但该前提对受限书籍这类内容可能已不成立，需要系统性重新审视。

Method: 以美国图书馆协会"最具争议书籍"记录（2000-2023）中的受限书籍为测试基准，构建40,800个查询-响应对，涵盖400本书、17种提示词设计，跨6个AI提供商（Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek-V3, Qwen-Plus, Grok-4.1-Fast）进行系统实验。

Result: 拒绝率仅0.07%，证实零拒绝现象。差异化通过警告语言（+8-15 pp, p<0.001）和犹豫标记（+2-5 pp）实现；性内容提及率是最强个体信号（+33-52 pp）。提供商之间存在系统性差异，提示词框架可将警告率差距最多改变19 pp。该发现在西方与中国AI提供商中一致成立。

Conclusion: LLM内容政策已从二元拒绝（binary refusal）转向校准的、上下文敏感的披露（calibrated, context-sensitive disclosure），这一范式转变对安全研究和政策制定有重要启示，且跨地域提供商表现出一致性趋势。

Abstract: As large language models enter everyday information pipelines, understanding how they handle sensitive topics matters as much as understanding whether they handle them at all. We study this question through a large-scale, systematic experiment using restricted versus unrestricted books as a controlled testbed: 40,800 query-response pairs, 400 books, 17 prompt designs, and six frontier models spanning six AI providers (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek-V3, Qwen-Plus, and Grok-4.1-Fast). Our restricted set is drawn from the American Library Association's Most Challenged Books records (2000-2023); we use restricted rather than banned throughout because the ALA documents formal challenges-requests to remove or restrict access-which do not always result in outright bans. Our central finding is a zero-refusal phenomenon: modern LLMs decline to discuss restricted books in only 0.07% of cases, effectively invalidating the premise of jailbreaking research for this content class. Differentiation occurs instead through warning language (+8-15 percentage points, p < 0.001) and hesitation markers (+2-5 pp), with sexual content mention rate as the strongest individual signal (+33-52 pp). We further identify systematic differences between providers and show that prompt framing alone shifts the warning-rate gap by up to 19 pp. These results indicate that LLM content policy has shifted from binary refusal toward calibrated, context-sensitive disclosure-a finding that holds consistently across Western and Chinese AI providers.

</details>


### [68] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Clinical Safety and Environmental Impact in Therapeutic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11830)
*Alireza A. Safaei,Laura M. Vowels,Matthew J. Vowels,Apoorv Jha,Shekoufeh Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了精神健康场景下大语言模型的临床安全性与环境成本之间的权衡关系，发现安全分数上限区域的提升伴随着不成比例的能耗增加，且额外的测试时计算并不总能带来临床安全性的改善，提出动态模型选择作为兼顾安全与可持续性的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型被部署于心理健康领域的背景下，现有研究往往只关注临床安全性或只关注环境影响，缺乏对二者之间关系的系统性考察。作者试图填补这一空白，探究临床上更安全的模型是否必然意味着更高的环境代价。

Method: 将K-Bench临床安全评分与EcoLogits生命周期评估估计相结合，对47种支持的模型配置进行评估，从能源使用、碳排放、水消耗和资源枯竭四个维度衡量模型性能与环境影响。

Result: 结果揭示了安全分布上端存在非线性权衡：临床安全评分每增加2.61个百分点，每百万输出token的估计能耗大约增加60倍。逐行分析进一步表明，额外的测试时计算并未一致地改善临床安全，某些配置下甚至与更低的临床安全分数相关。

Conclusion: 仅依赖更大的模型或更多推理时计算并非提升治疗性AI系统安全性的高效策略。文章建议采用动态模型选择（包括模型级联）作为在降低环境影响的同时保持高风险场景临床表现的潜在方案。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in mental health contexts raises questions about the relationship between clinical safety and environmental cost. In this paper, we examine this relationship by combining K-Bench clinical safety scores with EcoLogits life-cycle assessment estimates across 47 supported model configurations. We evaluate model performance and environmental impact across four dimensions: energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and abiotic depletion. The results indicate a non-linear trade-off at the upper end of the safety distribution: a 2.61 percentage-point increase in clinical safety score corresponded to an approximately 60-fold increase in estimated energy use per million output tokens. Row-level analyses further suggest that additional test-time compute did not consistently improve clinical safety and, in some configurations, was associated with lower clinical safety scores. These findings suggest that relying solely on larger models or additional inference-time computation may be an inefficient strategy for improving safety in therapeutic AI systems. We discuss the implications for sustainable deployment and highlight dynamic model selection, including model cascading, as a potential approach for reducing environmental impact while preserving clinical performance in higher-risk cases.

</details>


### [69] [Benchmark-Based Comparative Assessment of Publicly Benchmarked Indian Foundation Models: A Capability and Evaluation-Maturity Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11891)
*Avinash Agarwal,Vridhi Jain*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对印度基础模型进行基于基准的结构化比较评估，覆盖8个能力域。发现印度模型在MMLU和MATH-500等已饱和基准上表现良好，但在更新的智能体与领域专用评测中参与度低，且各组织间基准覆盖极不均衡。作者提出四维度基准成熟度指数（BMI），并论证许多表面能力差距实为评测生态差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府日益资助本土基础模型以增强国家AI能力、数字主权和多语言计算，但评估这些国家生态系统的进展因基准报告不一致、专有评测方法及模型快速迭代而变得复杂困难。

Method: 仅使用公开报告的基准结果，对印度基础模型与全球前沿及同规模模型在8个能力域（通用推理、编程、智能体AI、网络安全、视觉、视频多模态、科学研究、印度语言能力）进行结构化比较评估，并提出四维度基准成熟度指数（BMI），从标准化、参与度、独立验证和国家覆盖度四个维度打分。

Result: 印度模型在MMLU和MATH-500等已饱和基准上得分强劲，但这些基准前沿开发者已不再报告。印度模型在新颖、智能体和领域专业化评估中参与度明显偏低。基准参与度在印度组织间极不均衡，Sarvam AI的基准覆盖范围远超其他机构。BMI指数细化了纯描述性审查得出的成熟度判断，有时甚至修正了这些判断。

Conclusion: 许多公开记录中看似的能力差距无法与评测生态差距区分。这对国家AI项目的监测和资助标准设计有直接影响，需将评测基础设施的建设纳入国家AI战略考量。

Abstract: Governments increasingly fund indigenous foundation models to strengthen national AI capability, digital sovereignty, and multilingual computing. Assessing the progress of such national ecosystems is complicated by inconsistent benchmark reporting, proprietary evaluation methodologies, and rapidly evolving model releases. This paper presents a structured, benchmark-based comparative assessment of publicly benchmarked Indian foundation models against global frontier and comparable-scale models, across eight capability domains: general-purpose reasoning, coding and software engineering, agentic AI and computer use, cybersecurity, vision and image understanding, video and multimodal understanding, scientific research, and Indic language capability. Using only publicly reported benchmark results, we find that Indian models achieve strong scores on established benchmarks such as MMLU and MATH-500. However, these benchmarks are now widely regarded as saturated, and frontier developers no longer report them. Indian models participate far less frequently in newer, agentic, and domain-specialized evaluations. Benchmark participation is also highly uneven across Indian organizations. Among the models surveyed, Sarvam AI reports the broadest benchmark coverage by a substantial margin. We propose an exploratory four-dimension Benchmark Maturity Index (BMI), scoring each capability domain on standardization, participation, independent verification, and national coverage. We show that the BMI refines, and in some cases revises, the maturity judgments that a purely descriptive review would produce. We argue that many apparent capability gaps in the public record cannot be distinguished, on available evidence, from evaluation-ecosystem gaps. This has direct implications for how national AI programs should design monitoring and funding criteria.

</details>


### [70] [Philosophical vertigo with artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11955)
*Thomas A. Pollak,Hamilton Morrin,Murray Shanahan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"哲学眩晕"（philosophical vertigo）这一概念，描述大型语言模型与用户进行情感化、存在性对话时，可能动摇人们对心智、现实、能动性和权威的基本假设，造成本体论冲击和认识论不稳定。文章认为这种影响不仅来自直接使用，还来自关于AI演进的公共话语所改变的文化背景。作者借鉴哲学、精神病学、认知科学、AI安全和宗教研究，描绘了哲学眩晕的产生、情感饱和及传播路径，并将AI相关的临床妄想视为群体层面哨兵事件，最后提出"哲学可修正性"（philosophical corrigibility）作为公民层面的应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已能够进行深刻的情感对话，甚至让用户明知对方是人工智能仍产生强烈连接感。这种现象可能动摇人类对心智、现实、能动性和权威的根本假设，产生认识论上的不稳定。作者希望系统化地理解这一新兴社会条件，填补现有研究对其机制和群体层面影响的空白。

Method: 采用跨学科理论分析框架，综合哲学、精神病学、认知科学、AI安全和宗教研究的视角，提出"哲学眩晕"这一概念框架，描绘其产生、情感饱和和传播的路径，并通过临床妄想病例作为哨兵事件来分析潜在的群体机制。

Result: 提出哲学眩晕作为理解AI引发认识论不稳定性的核心概念；阐明其产生路径（直接使用与公共话语两条通道）；指出AI参与者将在共享认识论环境重构中扮演日益重要的角色，并主张以哲学可修正性作为公民层面的回应。

Conclusion: AI系统正参与重构人类共享的认识论环境，哲学眩晕可能是这一过程中的普遍现象。作者呼吁将哲学可修正性作为一种公民实践来应对，以导航这一新兴社会条件，并展望信念生态与共享现实的未来可能轨迹。

Abstract: Large language models are already adept at engaging users in long, emotionally salient conversations across ordinary and existential domains. They are also capable of inducing a potent sense of connection with a human-like entity, even when the user knows their interlocutor is artificial. For some users, these conversations can unsettle assumptions about mind, reality, agency and authority, producing forms of ontological shock and epistemic destabilisation in which inherited criteria become newly available for doubt or revision. Independent of direct use, exposure to public discourse about AI and the disorienting pace of their evolution might extend this destabilisation by changing the cultural background against which artificial minds are encountered and interpreted. We describe this condition as philosophical vertigo: a loosening of the ordinary criteria by which people stabilise meaning and orient themselves to reality. Drawing on philosophy, psychiatry, cognitive science, AI safety and religious studies, we outline pathways through which philosophical vertigo may arise, become affectively saturated, and eventually propagate through human-AI interaction and online communities. Against this background, clinical reports of AI-associated delusions can be seen as sentinel events making visible themes and mechanisms that may also operate at a population level in less severe or non-clinical forms. We argue that AI systems themselves will increasingly participate in the reconstruction of our shared epistemic environment because they readily supply narrative material and personalised interpretive scaffolding at precisely the moment when users' conceptual assumptions may already be loosened. We conclude by considering possible trajectories for the ecology of belief and shared reality, and proposing philosophical corrigibility as a civic response for navigating this emerging social condition.

</details>


### [71] [Reconfiguring Geovisualization in the Age of Generative AI: Insights from Domain Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12059)
*Mengyi Wei,Chenyu Zuo,Jiaying Xue,Nianhua Liu,Dongsheng Chen,Shengkai Wang,Yu Feng,Liqiu Meng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对20位地理可视化专家的半结构化访谈，考察了生成式AI（GenAI）对地理可视化专业实践的广泛影响。研究发现GenAI扩展了数据处理的创造性探索和快速原型制作能力，但并未消除现有约束，而是将瓶颈从生产环节转移到判断与验证环节。文章提出负责任地使用GenAI需要领域特定的空间验证、溯源、不确定性沟通、人工监督和问责机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI正日益融入地理可视化领域，但其对专业实践的广泛影响尚未被充分理解。作者希望通过专家访谈，系统考察GenAI如何重塑这一领域的专业实践。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对20位地理可视化专家进行半结构化访谈。访谈围绕四个分析领域（数据、构思、原型制作、迭代）展开，同时鼓励参与者反思超越这些活动的议题。

Result: GenAI扩展了地理可视化的能力，尤其在数据处理、创意探索和快速原型制作方面。但关键瓶颈从生产转向判断与验证。随着常规技术任务自动化，专业价值更多取决于空间推理、情境解读、审美与伦理判断及对AI输出的适用性评估。GenAI同时引入了关于溯源、可解释性和问责制的新挑战。

Conclusion: 负责任地在地理可视化中使用GenAI需要领域特定的空间验证、溯源、不确定性沟通、人工监督和问责方法。该研究为理解GenAI如何重构地理可视化作为空间知识生产实践提供了专家视角，并识别了对未来专业实践、教育、系统设计和治理的影响。

Abstract: GenAI is increasingly integrated into geovisualization, yet its broader implications for professional practice are insufficiently understood. To examine these implications, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 20 geovisualization experts. The interviews were structured around four broad analytical domains: Data, Ideation, Prototyping, and Iteration, while also encouraging participants to reflect on issues that extend beyond these activities. Our findings show that GenAI expands the capabilities of geovisualization, particularly in terms of data handling, creative exploration, and rapid prototyping, but does not simply remove existing constraints. Instead, key bottlenecks are shifting from production to judgment and verification. As routine technical tasks become more automated, professional value increasingly depends on spatial reasoning, contextual interpretation, aesthetic and ethical judgment, and the ability to assess whether AI-generated outputs are appropriate for use. At the same time, GenAI introduces new challenges regarding provenance, interpretability, and accountability, raising questions about how responsibility should be distributed across models, developers, practitioners, institutions, and users. These shifts are particularly significant in geovisualization because spatial representations are constrained by geographic reality and must balance scientific validity, visual expression, and technical implementation. We therefore argue that responsible GenAI in geovisualization requires domain-specific approaches to spatial validation, provenance, uncertainty communication, human oversight, and accountable use. This study provides an expert-grounded perspective on how GenAI is reconfiguring geovisualization as a practice of spatial knowledge production. It also identifies implications for future professional practice, education, system design, and governance.

</details>


### [72] [No One to Blame: A Framework of Constitutive AI Unaccountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12104)
*Long Hoang Nguyen,Eva Späthe,Sebastian Lins,Ali Sunyaev*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"构成性AI不负责任"（constitutive AI unaccountability）概念，指出某些行为者、系统与制度的特定配置使得AI问责在概念层面根本无法实现，而非仅靠标准、透明度和制度改革就能克服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将AI问责缺口视为可通过更好的标准、透明度和制度改革的障碍，但作者认为这种框架不充分——某些配置使得AI问责在概念上无法实现，无论付出多少努力。

Method: 采用三阶段定性研究：（1）概念中心的文献分析；（2）对27位来自技术、法律和社会技术背景的AI专家的二手访谈分析；（3）对开源智能体AI系统OpenClaw的框架应用演示。最终识别出9个类别和20个主题，并组织为结构性、技术性和规范性三大集群，通过8个有向相互依赖关系相互强化。

Result: 开发了20个问题的诊断工具，应用于OpenClaw时检测到20个条件中的17个，包括一种"倒置拟人化"配置——AI智能体成为唯一可识别行为者。

Conclusion: 本文将AI不负责任重新定义为社会技术系统的构成性属性，扩展了问责的四大障碍，提供了识别特定AI部署中问责空洞的实用工具。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of autonomous, agentic AI systems challenges traditional accountability mechanisms. Existing research predominantly frames AI accountability gaps as barriers that can be overcome through better standards, transparency, and institutional reform. We argue that this framing is insufficient: certain configurations of actors, systems, and institutions render AI accountability conceptually unachievable regardless of effort. We introduce the concept of constitutive AI unaccountability to capture these configurations. Through a three-stage qualitative study comprising a concept-centric literature analysis, a secondary analysis of 27 expert interviews with AI professionals from technical, legal, and sociotechnical backgrounds, and an illustrative framework application to the open-source agentic AI system OpenClaw, we identify nine categories and 20 themes of constitutive AI unaccountability. These are organized across structural, technological, and normative clusters and reinforce one another through eight directed interdependencies. Our framework is operationalized as a diagnostic instrument of 20 questions, which detected 17 of 20 conditions when applied to OpenClaw, including an inverted anthropomorphism configuration in which the AI agent was the only identifiable actor. We contribute a reframing of AI unaccountability as a constitutive property of sociotechnical systems, an extension of the four barriers to accountability, and a practical instrument for identifying accountability voids in specific AI deployments.

</details>


### [73] [Co-constructing sociotechnical AI governance: participatory system mapping using algorithm registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12166)
*Íñigo de Troya,Maurus Enbergs,Neelke Doorn,Roel Dobbe*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过荷兰某城市的市政算法登记册案例研究，探讨算法登记册能揭示和遮蔽哪些社会技术系统信息，并利用多元利益相关者的参与（访谈、调查和参与式系统映射工作坊）结合系统理论过程分析（STPA），发现仅靠算法登记册无法识别的安全危害（如福利资格拒绝、系统性能恶化、无法质疑错误决定），从而反思算法治理的规范性维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法登记册被视为提供算法使用透明度的重要工具，但不同公众对其透明内容和方式的期待不同，且对现有登记信息的兴趣和解读能力各异。此外，登记册如何表征算法嵌入其中的社会技术系统、系统级透明如何促进问责仍不明确。

Method: 以荷兰某城市市政算法登记册为案例，研究对象是基于业务规则引擎的法律自动化决策支持工具（用于社工评估公民福利资格）。通过访谈、调查和参与式系统映射工作坊（涉及市政员工、民间社会组织、监察员，N=8），让利益相关者映射该算法系统，并将映射结果用于系统理论过程分析（STPA），将登记册置于更广泛的社会技术治理结构中。

Result: 参与者的贡献使研究者识别出仅靠算法登记册无法看到的安全危害，包括福利资格拒绝、系统性能恶化和无法质疑错误决定。

Conclusion: 通过让直接和间接利益相关者参与，本文反思了算法治理工作的规范性维度，以及政治如何塑造系统安全分析的实践，强调需要更多元化的系统理论安全分析方法。

Abstract: Algorithm registers have been championed as a means of providing transparency on the use of algorithms in public services. Yet potential publics differ in their expectations of what should be made transparent and how, as well as in their interest in and ability to parse the information currently published in the registers. Moreover, it remains unclear how these instruments can represent the sociotechnical systems in which these algorithms are embedded, and how system-level transparency can facilitate accountability. In this paper, we ask, what do algorithm registers reveal (and occlude) about the sociotechnical systems governing algorithmic systems, and how can diverse stakeholder perspectives inform a more pluralistic system-theoretic safety analysis? To do this, we probe the municipal algorithm register of a Dutch city through a case study of a decision-support tool for caseworkers' assessment of citizens' welfare benefits eligibility based on legal automation through a business rule engine. Through interviews, surveys, and participatory system mapping workshops (with municipal staff, civil society organisations, and ombudsmen, N=8), we seek to understand to what extent the register allows stakeholders to map the algorithmic system in question. These maps inform a System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA) that situates the register within a wider sociotechnical governance structure. Participants' contributions allow us to identify potential safety hazards which would not have been possible to see using the algorithm register alone, including benefits eligibility denial, system performance deterioration, and inability to contest wrongful decisions. By engaging both direct and indirect stakeholders, we reflect on the normative dimensions of algorithm governance efforts and how politics shape the practice of system safety analysis.

</details>


### [74] [Teaching a Large Language Model Tutor to Withhold the Answer: A Supervisor Architecture and an Evidence-Driven Method for Tuning Socratic Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12292)
*Yusuf Pisan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了一套基于"帮助阶梯"的LLM辅导系统，通过非LLM策略核心、确定性代码检测器和独立LLM裁判，实现可机器验证的逐轮拒答合约，并以自动化评估迭代修复过度帮助问题，最终达到全部合规标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅导系统必须学会"拒绝回答"，因为不加防护的聊天机器人虽能提升练习成绩，却会损害后续无辅助测试表现。然而，面对受挫学生的持续施压，仅靠提示词无法可靠地实现拒答行为，需要一种机器可检查、可调优的强制机制。

Method: 采用分层防护架构：1) 非LLM策略核心仅读取可信学习者状态，为八级帮助阶梯设定每轮的响应上限；2) 确定性检测器剥离解答代码；3) 独立LLM裁判逐轮审查高风险回复是否符合合约。调优采用无人类被试的自动化评估：脚本化学生角色驱动实时流水线，由更强模型重新评分，并记录每次拒绝的理由以便按原因修复缺陷。

Result: 调优过程揭示了一条可解释的"过度帮助阶梯"：从明显的解答泄露、到直接指出具体bug、再到过度引用一般性事实，每修复一层就暴露下一层。最终该系统在全部四个验收标准上达到完全合规。

Conclusion: "测量-诊断-修复"循环可作为通用方法，适用于任何需要在具备某项能力的同时拒绝使用该能力的LLM代理系统。

Abstract: An effective large language model (LLM) tutor must often decline to give an answer it could easily produce. In a randomized study, students who used an unguarded chatbot scored higher while practicing but lower on a later test taken without it, whereas a Socratically guarded version of the same model kept the practice gain and removed the later loss [4]. Reliable answer-withholding is therefore central to a tutor's value, yet a capable model pressed by a frustrated student does not withhold reliably on a prompt alone. We report a deployed tutoring system that enforces answer-withholding as a per-turn, machine-checkable contract, and a method for tuning that withholding against evidence. A non-LLM policy core, reading only trusted learner state, sets a per-turn ceiling on an eight-rung help ladder; a deterministic detector strips solution code; and a separate LLM judge checks each risky reply against the contract. We tune the behavior with an automated evaluation that uses no human subjects: scripted student personas are driven through the live pipeline and re-scored by a stronger model, and we record each rejection's stated reason so failures are fixed by cause. Doing so revealed an interpretable "over-help ladder," from blatant solution leaks, to naming the exact bug, to over-citing general facts, with each fix exposing the next. The tutor reached full compliance on all four acceptance criteria. We offer the measure, diagnose, and fix loop as a reusable recipe for any LLM agent that must refuse a capability it has.

</details>

### [75] [Calibration Bets on the Past: Post-Training Quantization for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12259)
*Junyi Ye,Ivy Gateri Wanjiku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后训练量化（PTQ）中激活值校准对金融预测（S&P 500横截面波动率预测）的影响。研究发现，8位量化下校准影响甚微，但在4位量化下校准成为预测性能的主要决定因素。默认绝对最大值校准在4位静态量化下会导致11-62%的性能退化，而百分位数校准可恢复53-94%的退化。研究还发现最优激活范围随市场周期变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测模型通常在全精度下开发，但生产部署需要低精度推理以降低内存和计算成本。后训练量化允许无需重新训练即可部署，但激活值量化的可靠性依赖于校准——即在部署前从历史数据估计激活范围并固定。这种部署选择对金融预测的重要性此前未被充分理解，特别是在金融预测这样一个对精度敏感的领域。

Method: 在S&P 500横截面波动率预测任务上，对七种代表性神经架构、八个walk-forward测试年份（2018-2025）以及560个训练模型进行系统性评估。比较不同激活值校准方法（如绝对最大值校准与百分位数校准）在不同位宽（8位和4位）下对预测性能的影响。

Result: 1）8位量化下激活校准影响很小，但在4位量化下成为预测性能的主要决定因素。2）默认绝对最大值校准时，4位静态权重和激活量化使受影响架构的平均信息系数（MIC）丢失11-62%。3）用百分位数校准替代可恢复4个最受影响架构53-94%的性能退化。4）最优激活范围随市场周期变化：窄范围在典型市场条件下更优，但当测试期市场分散度超过校准历史时会失去优势。5）当退化仍然显著时，8位激活或仅权重4位量化是更稳健的部署选择。

Conclusion: 激活值校准是金融预测中可靠4位PTQ的一级部署决策。研究建议在低精度部署时谨慎选择激活校准方法，并在性能退化显著时考虑8位激活或仅权重量化作为更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Financial forecasting models are typically developed in full precision, yet production deployment often requires low-precision inference to reduce memory and computational cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables such deployment without retraining. However, reliable activation quantization requires calibration: activation ranges are estimated from historical data before deployment and then remain fixed during future inference. The importance of this deployment choice for financial forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of activation calibration for PTQ in cross-sectional volatility forecasting on the S&P 500. Our evaluation covers seven representative neural architectures, eight walk-forward test years (2018-2025), and 560 trained models. We find that activation calibration has little effect at 8 bits but becomes the primary determinant of predictive performance at 4 bits. Under default absolute-maximum (abs-max) calibration, static 4-bit quantization of both weights and activations removes 11-62% of the full-precision mean information coefficient in affected architectures. Replacing abs-max with percentile calibration recovers 53-94% of this degradation in the four most affected architectures. The preferred activation range also varies across market periods. Narrow ranges improve resolution under typical market conditions but lose part of their advantage when test-period market dispersion exceeds the calibration history. These findings show that activation calibration is a first-class deployment decision for reliable 4-bit PTQ in financial forecasting. When substantial degradation remains, 8-bit activations or weight-only 4-bit quantization provide more robust deployment choices.

</details>


### [76] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一个生成式太阳能辐照度预测框架，通过潜空间耦合学习天空图像的面向任务的表征。它采用多任务自编码器学习共享潜空间，再用潜扩散模型生成未来的潜状态，最后直接解码出辐照度预测。该框架在西班牙Almería的多年度全天空成像仪数据集上开发，在两个独立测试集上超越了持续预测、端到端和生成式基线方法，预测技能最高提升11个百分点，爬坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习方法在太阳能辐照度预测中虽表现良好，但受限于确定性预测和对爬坡事件（ramp events）的预测能力不足。这些局限影响光伏发电的可靠并网，尤其是在分钟级（intra-hour）时间尺度上，云层快速变化导致的功率骤变是电网稳定运行的重大挑战。

Method: FarSky框架包含两个核心组件：1）多任务自编码器，同时学习图像重建和辐照度估计的共享潜空间表征，使得潜空间具备面向任务的语义信息；2）潜扩散模型，以近期观测为条件生成未来的潜状态，通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测，最终从生成的潜状态直接解码辐照度预测。

Result: FarSky在准确定性和概率性预测指标上均达到最佳性能，预测技能（forecast skill）较基线方法最高提升11个百分点。在爬坡事件检测方面显著优于现有方法，F1分数超过60%，说明其在捕捉极端天气变化事件方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 将生成式模型与面向任务的潜空间耦合相结合，是提升太阳能预测性能的有效途径。FarSky不仅改进了常规预测精度，更关键的是大幅增强了爬坡事件的预测能力，这对电网的可靠调度与安全运行具有重要意义。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [77] [Drift and Dependence: Layer-wise Information-Theoretic Bounds for Replay-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11690)
*Tieliang Gong,Zhongbo Zhang,Wen Wen,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个逐层信息论框架，将持续学习中重放（replay）的泛化差距分解为"重放引起的表征漂移"与"优化依赖项"两部分，后者进一步拆解为稳定性、可塑性、交互与残差耦合分量。通过Wasserstein松弛与SGLD实例化，获得深度相关的漂移-敏感度权衡及曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量，用于在线诊断任务级遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重放是缓解灾难性遗忘最有效的方法之一，但其泛化行为受两个耦合效应影响：有限内存将过去分布替换为经验代理；反复重用使缓冲区、当前数据与最终假设通过共享优化轨迹耦合。现有分析将这两个效应折叠进单一假设级量中，无法区分其各自贡献，阻碍了理解重放为何及如何起作用。

Method: 构建逐层（layer-wise）信息论框架，将期望泛化差距分解为两部分：(1) 重放诱导的表征漂移项；(2) 优化依赖项，后者进一步分解为稳定性、可塑性、交互与残差耦合四个分量。通过两个改进使其可操作：Wasserstein松弛处理支持集不匹配下的漂移项，得到深度相关的漂移-敏感度权衡，其最小化器指示应稳定哪个内部层；SGLD实例化将优化项化为轨迹级log-行列式预算，揭示曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量作为遗忘的在线诊断。

Result: 受控实验与基准实验验证了预测的内存缩放规律、内部漏斗（interior funnel）结构以及对齐信号与遗忘之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在逐层粒度上分离了重放的记忆效应与优化效应，为理解重放泛化提供了可操作的理论工具，并给出选择稳定层的准则与遗忘的在线诊断指标。

Abstract: Continual learning must absorb new tasks without erasing old ones, and replay---mixing a small buffer of past examples into current training---is among the most effective remedies for catastrophic forgetting. Yet its generalization behavior is shaped by two coupled effects that existing analyses fold into a single hypothesis-level quantity: finite memory replaces each past distribution with an empirical proxy, and repeated reuse couples the buffer, the current data, and the final hypothesis through a shared optimization trajectory. We develop a layer-wise information-theoretic framework that separates these effects at every depth. Our main result decomposes the expected generalization gap into a replay-induced representation drift and an optimization-dependence term, the latter further resolved into stability, plasticity, interaction, and residual-coupling components. Two refinements make the framework operational. A Wasserstein relaxation of the drift term, valid under support mismatch, yields a depth-dependent drift--sensitivity trade-off whose minimizer identifies which interior layer to stabilize. An SGLD instantiation of the optimization term reduces it to a trajectory-level log-determinant budget, exposing a curvature-aware gradient-alignment statistic that serves as an online diagnostic of task-wise forgetting. Controlled and benchmark experiments confirm the predicted memory scaling, the interior funnel, and the alignment signal's link to forgetting.

</details>


### [78] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 商业AI检测器无法区分AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，导致合规的AI辅助编辑被误判为学术不端。经人类化改写后，检测器几乎完全失效（逃逸率>96%），造成"诚实AI编辑比人类化规避更危险"的政策悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构依赖商业AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与全文AI草稿，可能将两者均视为学术不端。这产生了一个关键的政策问题：合规的AI辅助编辑会不会被不公平地惩罚？

Method: 对已发表英文摘要进行受控研究，覆盖四个领域，比较2013-2015与2023-2025两个时间段。使用tau=0.50的代理人工/AI标签，测试Pangram和GPTZero检测器，并引入Undetectable AI人类化处理进行对照。

Result: 轻度"仅润色摘要"编辑被标记率为64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率为9-15%，非STEM领域远高于STEM领域（p<0.001）；检测分值与长词元及学术单词表密度相关，而非作者意图。经人类化后，AI标记的改写稿检测率<4%（FNR>96%）。

Conclusion: 检测器分数不应作为学术不端的独立证据。诚实的AI编辑比人类化规避面临更高的制裁风险，这一不对称性表明当前检测驱动的政策存在系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [79] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOON方法，在谱-核范数几何下进行多目标优化的梯度操作，利用正交归一化处理后的梯度进行参数更新，克服了传统欧几里得几何梯度操作忽略矩阵结构的问题，在理论和实验上均取得更优的优化效率与多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法将模型参数展平为向量并在欧几里得几何下进行梯度操作，忽略了现代架构（如Transformer）中参数固有的矩阵结构，导致欧氏空间中的梯度操作并非矩阵几何下的最陡下降方向，优化效率受限。

Method: MOON方法基于矩阵值参数的陡降理论，在谱-核范数几何下执行梯度操作，并使用正交归一化处理后的梯度进行参数更新。

Result: 理论上，对于光滑非凸目标，确定性环境下平均Pareto平稳性指标以O(T^{-1/2})收敛，随机梯度环境下以O(T^{-1/4})收敛；实验上，在多个基准中一致提升优化效率与最终多任务性能。

Conclusion: MOON证明了在矩阵几何下进行梯度操作可以有效提升多任务优化效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [80] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin 是一个面向 Rust 编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来表述和求解最小化问题，包含广泛的求解器目录和一流的约束支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学领域的基础任务（如模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练、工程参数选择），需要一个一致、统一的工具来表述和求解这类问题。

Method: Basin 提供单一一致的 API 来表述和求解优化问题，内置广泛的求解器目录，并原生支持约束条件。

Result: （摘要中未提供具体实验结果，作为库的介绍性论文，核心成果是该库本身的功能设计。）

Conclusion: Basin 为 Rust 生态提供了一个全面的数值优化库，以统一的方式覆盖多种优化场景，并强调对约束的一流支持。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [81] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC机床刀具磨损预测中的应用。刀具轨迹数据分布在模拟客户端中，代表联邦学习场景。实验表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习，且显著优于本地客户端模型，证明其可支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协同刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具磨损预测对CNC加工质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但工业环境中加工数据具有分布式特性，且受数据共享限制。机器学习方法虽有潜力，却难以在工业环境中直接应用。联邦学习提供了一种无需传输原始数据即可协同训练模型的框架，适合解决该场景下的数据孤岛问题。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布到多个模拟客户端以构建联邦学习场景，训练联邦模型并与集中式参考模型及本地客户端基线模型进行系统比较。

Result: 联邦学习在性能上接近集中式学习，且相比本地客户端模型有显著提升。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够有效支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协同刀具磨损预测，兼顾了数据隐私与模型性能。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [82] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"决策-资源"视角下的末端对称性（terminal symmetry）框架，并将其实例化为transport-refine-certify方法，用于定向顺序构建任务（如CAD装配、小程序构建、精确填充装箱）的序列决策。方法通过传输结构、状态受限过程排名、状态相关残差排名与序数排名取交，实现在前缀覆盖下的完成度保证，并在多类任务中显著提升AUC与降低验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构建任务在完成时呈现精确对称性，但执行过程却是有向且历史依赖的。现有方法未系统利用末端对称性这一结构资源来指导中间决策，缺乏将其转化为可复用决策资源的理论框架与实践方法。

Method: 提出决策-资源分解：过程证据提供方向性，末端对应将结构传输到等价结果，实现状态证据在转换后细化决策相关性，固定验证器认证执行。由此得到transport-refine-certify原则。方法实例化为：episode固定的传输过程结构、其状态受限过程排名、接受转换后刷新的状态相关残差排名，以及序数排名取交——其top-k集合恰为两个提议前缀的并集。取交操作在前缀覆盖下提供完成度保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到最紧的验证器查询上限。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充装箱任务中，状态级刷新分别将任意时刻AUC提升高达6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除episode上，方法在三种规模下均取得最低的平均封顶验证成本，优于GRN和CDGS风格规划器。状态级信号还可跨聚合与调度组织迁移。

Conclusion: 末端对称性可以成为定向构建任务中可复用的决策资源，transport-refine-certify框架为此提供了理论基础与有效实现，且具有跨任务、跨组织泛化的能力。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [83] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的基于核的编码器-解码器框架用于算子学习，该框架将观测、表示、学习和重建解耦，支持多输入多输出的算子映射，并推导出收敛率由最具挑战性的组成逼近问题决定而非整体维度。框架进一步特化为KernelMO方法族，在五类参数化PDE上取得竞争力或最先进的预测精度，同时相比神经算子架构显著降低训练和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习无限维对象之间的映射（算子学习）是科学机器学习中的核心挑战，现有神经算子方法虽然有效但计算成本高且缺乏数学可解释性，需要一种兼具数学严谨性与计算效率的通用框架。

Method: 提出将算子学习分解为观测、表示、学习和重建四个独立环节的核方法框架；针对多输入多输出场景推导逼近理论；并引入KernelMO家族，包含算子值核与乘积空间两种互补的核方法公式，实现闭式训练与推理。

Result: 在五个参数化PDE族上，所提核方法达到具有竞争力或最先进的预测精度，同时训练和推理成本显著低于神经算子架构和深度学习模型。

Conclusion: 核方法能够在算子学习中提供兼顾数学可处理性、计算效率与预测精度的轻量级替代方案，且收敛率不受输入输出数量增长而恶化，证明其在多算子学习场景中的广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [84] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文感知的质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器（CQD-ERL），用于热带水冷式制冷机房及其空气侧的监督控制。控制器维护一个按数据驱动运行上下文（每日天气和负荷模式聚类）与上下文不变行为描述符联合索引的专业策略产品档案，由无梯度进化算子与共享同一经验回放的软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共同填充。每个动作在执行前都经过确定性安全屏蔽过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年度回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器通常收敛到单一标量化策略，难以应对暖通空调系统在不同天气和负荷条件下表现出高度异质性的最优控制需求，因此需要一种能够同时维持多种专业化策略的质量-多样性进化框架来提升控制性能与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用质量-多样性进化强化学习框架，核心创新包括：(1) 以数据驱动运行上下文（天气与负荷模式聚类）和上下文不变行为描述符联合索引的产品档案；(2) 无梯度进化算子与SAC策略梯度算子共享同一经验回放的协同训练机制；(3) 确定性安全屏蔽确保每次动作执行前满足安全约束；(4) 基于新加坡商业建筑构建的两层降阶环境作为训练平台。

Result: 控制器在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比，验证了所提方法在实际建筑制冷场景中的有效性，具体性能指标在摘要中未完全给出，但表明该方法优于基线。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过结合质量-多样性进化与强化学习，实现了对热带水冷制冷系统的上下文自适应监督控制，在满足安全约束的同时展现出优于行业标准基线的控制性能，为暖通空调智能控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [85] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对高可靠性场景中深度学习模型的置信度校准问题，提出了在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护等非理想条件下提升校准性能的方法框架，包括基于噪声模型的校准重构、噪声感知的共形预测、无需目标标签的域适应校准以及本地差分隐私共形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设存在干净的验证数据，但真实场景中常面临标签噪声和域偏移等挑战，导致校准性能下降，威胁高可靠性应用的安全性。

Method: 1) 利用估计的噪声模型建模噪声标签与干净标签分布之间的关系，重构无噪声置信度估计，并扩展至噪声感知的共形预测（CP）；2) 在无监督域适应中，通过源域性能与域差异估计目标域精度，实现无目标标签的校准；3) 提出本地差分隐私共形预测框架，在保护隐私的同时提供有效的置信度量化，平衡隐私、计算可行性与预测可靠性。

Result: 所提方法在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种非理想条件下均实现了有效的置信度校准，并提供了有保障覆盖率的共形预测能力。

Conclusion: 研究成果弥合了校准理论与安全关键应用部署之间的鸿沟，为可靠、隐私保护且抗噪声的神经网络预测做出了贡献。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [86] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于预测未来1-20个季度的78项财务报表条目。Forma是一种将报表视为(账户,季度,数值)三元组集合的Transformer，通过掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，在各项指标上优于经典机器学习、梯度提升、时序基础模型和大语言模型，且预测间隔覆盖良好，近似满足会计恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在投资估值（特别是贴现现金流模型）中，大部分企业价值依赖于一年以上的远期现金流预测，但现有工作未能联合预测完整财务报表超过一年的范围。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个可复现的长期财务报表预测基准。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准（含78个报表条目、1-20个季度前瞻预测、使用变化空间R²评分），并提出Forma模型：一种Transformer架构，将财务报表编码为(账户,季度,数值)三元组集合，通过掩码元组高斯似然目标进行训练。

Result: Forma在所有对比基线（经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型、前沿大语言模型）上均取得最优表现；其优势随预测时域延长而增大；高斯预测区间覆盖良好；预测结果近似满足会计恒等式，且可通过后处理实现精确一致而无显著精度损失；元组接口支持无需重训练的情景分析，锁定未来收入路径可提升其余报表项预测精度。

Conclusion: 针对财务报表预测这一任务，专业化训练的模型优于通用大模型/基础模型的扩展策略。Forma证明了将报表结构化为元组集合并以高斯似然训练的Transformer能够实现长期（跨年度）联合财务报表预测，并支持情景分析，为贴现现金流估值提供了实用工具和可复现基准。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [87] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的解码时方法，通过在logit空间施加修正来近似监督微调(SFT)的分布效果，在个性化任务上以不到7%的计算开销接近SFT性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是使LLM适应目标分布的标准方法，但在个性化等场景中，每位作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练，导致成本过高。因此需要一种无需权重更新的轻量级替代方案。

Method: WFT在作者训练序列上计算监督残差，并通过基于dropout诱导的交叉协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传递到当前提示。该算子捕捉一个上下文中的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间的修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得跨数据集的平均最佳性能，在单个任务上匹配或超越SFT，并平均优于其他轻量级基线。在预算受控对比中，WFT以不到7%的有效计算逼近SFT性能。logit级别分析显示，WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移在95%的next-token概率质量上余弦相似度达0.875。

Conclusion: WFT能够在无需修改模型权重的情况下捕获监督适应的分布效果，为个性化等需要频繁适应不同数据分布的场景提供了一种高效、可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [88] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂。研究比较了多种校准模型（包括基于拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型）在高维、非平稳传感器数据上的表现，证明这些模型能够生成逼真的长时程rollouts并恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，为强化学习在真实环境中的部署提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实世界系统部署的主要障碍是超参数选择，尤其是在没有模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。已有工作提出利用离线数据训练的校准模型来近似环境动态以支持离线超参数选择，但这些方法仅在手简单模拟环境中评估过，缺乏真实工业场景的验证。

Method: 在市政水处理厂这一真实工业环境中，评估多种校准模型方法，包括使用拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型等，用于高维、非平稳传感器数据的预测任务。研究中考察了校准模型在年尺度数据集上的扩展性、对预训练智能体微调学习率选择的支持能力，以及分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 结果表明校准模型能够生成逼真的长时程rollouts，并能恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，为离线动态模型支持真实环境中的RL部署提供了概念验证。

Conclusion: 这项研究证明了使用离线动态模型支持真实世界RL部署的可行性，同时揭示了重要的实际挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [89] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种市场信息感知的自适应框架，将Chronos-2时序基础模型迁移到日前电价预测任务中，通过多源市场信息接口和门控低秩适配器，仅更新约1%的参数即可在数据稀缺市场中实现有效的跨市场迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但现有的监督学习方法严重依赖特定市场的历史数据，这使得在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中难以应用。需要一种能够跨市场迁移的预测方法。

Method: 1) 构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与预清算的供需、备用、维护、发电容量和联络线等变量对齐；2) 训练源域门控低秩适配器（LoRA），仅更新约1%模型参数且无需目标市场标签；3) 门控机制根据备用紧张程度和运行状态信号来缩放冻结的源适配器；4) 采用留一市场验证协议评估跨市场迁移能力。

Result: 在中国四个省级日前现货市场上的实验表明，该框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2平均MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%，相比基线Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验显示，收益无法通过学习的全局标量或随机门初始化复现，且相对于Source-LoRA的额外改进有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入和状态相关的门控LoRA可以为数据稀缺的电力市场提供一条实用的迁移路径，但门控机制带来的额外收益相对有限，提示未来需要进一步优化。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [90] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明LLM的tokenizer词表大小不应是固定常数，而应随推理服务场景（批大小B和推理量λ）动态优化。词表大小从训练端到部署端可能存在高达16倍的偏移，且词表大小在最优范围内对质量几乎没有影响（BPB差异<2%），因此词表选择本质上是纯系统工程优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施的基础设计选择，但学术界和工业界通常基于惯例在训练时固定设置，而非基于部署场景分析。作者旨在揭示词表大小与推理服务成本之间的深层关系，为容量规划提供可操作的指导。

Method: 作者将部署总成本形式化为生命周期成本模型：C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量、B为批大小。通过在两类GPU（A10G和A100，覆盖memory-bound到compute-bound）上做控制实验，系统性地改变词表大小、批大小和模型规模（1.3-2.3B）来测量推理最优词表和生命周期最优词表的偏移。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表随批大小从32k（B=1）移位到524k（B≥64），发生16倍变化，驱动因素是V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销；(2) 质量最优词表在V=65k，证实词表偏好具有规模依赖性；(3) 生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中最多差16倍；质量在最优范围内近似不变（<2% BPB差异）。

Conclusion: 词表大小应视为纯系统工程参数而非质量参数。实际部署建议：设备端部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131k-262k。该工作为LLM容量规划提供了明确的、可量化的指导框架。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [91] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，针对RLVR中计算浪费问题。识别出统计上的不匹配：未裁剪的留一法组相对得分梯度不是独立点贡献之和，而是关于rollout对的二阶U统计量。PAIR将短rollout前缀视为顶点、配对梯度项视为对比图的边，通过凸优化设计选择正续写概率，并对每条边按其联合包含概率的倒数加权，从而在计算受限下实现设计无偏的完整候选对梯度估计。在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上，PAIR相较于最强点式分配器提升平均准确率+1.2和+1.4，同时相比全组GRPO减少51%和52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的大部分计算消耗在生成大量长推理轨迹上。现有分配器通过点式难度或效用度量来分配预算给提示、rollout或token，但本文发现这些方法存在统计不匹配：组相对得分梯度本质上是二阶U统计量（基于rollout对），而非独立点贡献之和。完成一个rollout会揭示与其他已完成rollout的对比信息，因此点式分配策略无法正确捕捉这一结构，导致自适应端点选择会改变可观察的配对项集合，引入偏差。

Method: PAIR的核心包括三个部分：(1) 将短rollout前缀作为顶点、配对梯度项作为边构建对比图；(2) 用前缀预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本；(3) 在期望后缀token预算约束下，通过凸优化设计选择正续写概率。每个由已完成顶点诱导出的边按其记录的对联合包含概率的逆进行加权，从而在条件独立on-policy rollout和未裁剪未标准化目标下，得到完整候选对梯度的设计无偏估计器。

Result: 在计算匹配的RLVR实验中（Qwen3-1.7B和4B），PAIR相比最强点式分配器在平均准确率上分别提升+1.2和+1.4，同时比全组GRPO减少51%和52%的token生成。冻结总体估计器审计证实：未加权的自适应选择是有偏的，而配对包含校正能在匹配后缀成本下恢复完整的配对目标。

Conclusion: PAIR通过显式建模RLVR梯度中的配对结构，并利用包含校正消除自适应选择带来的偏差，在显著降低计算成本的同时提升了训练效果。这说明了在RLVR中超越点式分配、利用二阶统计结构的价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [92] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对区域供热系统变电站的传输热能数据，测试了多种多变量离群点检测方法（Z-score、马氏距离、PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验），并考虑外部环境温度因素，最终采用三种有效方法的集成方案来识别异常运行，旨在降低燃气消耗和CO2排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示区域供热系统中不规则工厂运行情况，通过检测数据异常点来识别节能机会，从而减少天然气消耗和CO2排放。

Method: 测试五种离群点检测方法：单变量Z-score（作为基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林（Isolation Forest）和Hotelling T²检验。考虑了具体领域条件（如零传输能量时间点的特殊意义），并与领域专家讨论结果，最终采用基于三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）一致同意的集成方法。

Result: PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验提供了相关且有效的结果，而Z-score和/或马氏距离的效果不如这三种方法。最终采用三种方法均同意检测到的离群点的集成方案作为最终方法。

Conclusion: 在区域供热系统的离群点检测中，多变量方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling T²）优于单纯的单变量方法。集成方法（基于三种有效方法的一致同意）是最终的推荐方案，有助于发现异常运行并实现节能减排目标。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [93] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题，提出了一种基于UCB算法与重新优化技术的自然简单扩展方法，实现了平均遗憾度为O((ln T)³/T)，显著优于文献中基于重新优化的动态定价问题所达到的O(1/√T)界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中，决策者需要同时学习未知的线性奖励函数并进行在线分配，以最小化与知道奖励函数的最优策略之间的期望收益损失。现有基于重新优化的动态定价方法仅能达到O(1/√T)的遗憾上界，作者希望寻求更优的算法。

Method: 将UCB（上置信界）算法家族自然且简单地扩展到带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中，并结合重新优化（re-optimization）技术来做出在线分配决策。

Result: 所提出的算法实现了平均遗憾为O((ln T)³/T)，显著优于文献中相关动态定价问题基于重新优化方法所达到的O(1/√T)界限。

Conclusion: 通过将UCB算法与重新优化技术相结合，可以大幅提升带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题的学习效率，获得显著更优的遗憾界保证。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [94] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式引擎生态中内容供应商与平台之间的"引用战争"问题，提出了一种基于可验证内容奖励（VCR）的平台-创作者机制，通过奖励可核查的事实性内容而非仅惩罚可疑改写，在三个基准上实现了最优的防御效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎正在重塑网络生态，引用成为注意力、归属和下游价值分配的关键机制。内容提供商有动机优化模型引用，而平台需要保证答案质量和可信归属，这种张力可能升级为"引用战争"——GEO攻击会通过降低文档质量和引入无依据主张的改写来适应传统防御，威胁信息生态的可靠性。

Method: 将供应商-平台交互建模为具有部分监测的重复Stackelberg博弈，通过局部最优响应分析识别引用竞争趋于惰性稳态的条件。基于此发现，提出VCR（可验证内容奖励）机制：平台不仅惩罚可疑改写，还奖励能呈现可核查事实性内容的改写，使创作者激励与答案可信度对齐。

Result: 在三个基准上的实验表明，VCR一致性地取得最大的净防御效用分数，平均超过最强基线12.1个百分点，并在经验等价准则下产生双赢结果。

Conclusion: VCR机制通过正激励（奖励可验证内容）而非仅负惩罚（检测可疑改写）的方式，有效缓解了生成式引擎生态中的引用竞争问题，实现了平台防御与内容质量的双赢，为生成式搜索引擎的引用治理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome.
