Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [95] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，用于检测离线强化学习智能体在ICU治疗决策中的"毒性模仿"失败模式，并通过MIMIC-III数据库验证了该框架能有效区分统计拟合良好但临床危险的智能体与临床安全的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估指标（MSE和FQE）只能评估行为模仿程度，无法检测"毒性模仿"——即智能体复制有害治疗模式（如舒适护理过渡期间停止治疗）的失败模式，这暴露了统计拟合与临床安全之间的系统性错位。

Method: 提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，基于幸存脓毒症运动（SSC）指南，通过对生理扰动进行压力测试来审计RL智能体。对比审计了两个模型：Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT)和采用因果动作屏蔽、倾向性重要性加权及保守Q学习的Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL)，并使用MIMIC-III数据库。

Result: CCA揭示了MedDT在乳酸升高时反而减少血管加压药剂量，违背复苏指南；而HCT-RL能维持生理学上一致的反应。这证明统计拟合指标无法发现临床不安全行为。

Conclusion: 反事实审计应作为医疗RL的必要评估标准，以弥补传统统计指标在临床安全性评估上的不足。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [96] [XGBoost "is all you need": the case of forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11446)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对XGBoost和LSTM两种方法在区域供热系统（DHS）传输热能预测中的表现进行了对比研究，发现XGBoost在该特定时间序列预测任务中持续优于LSTM，且计算成本更低、碳足迹更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在时间序列预测中，传统机器学习算法相对于深度学习网络在何种场景下表现更优，以及由此带来的计算成本和环境影响方面的优势。

Method: 使用真实世界区域供热系统数据集，对XGBoost和LSTM两种方法进行对比实验，并通过误差分布分析来解释两者性能差异的原因。

Result: XGBoost在DHS传输热能预测任务中持续优于LSTM；误差分布显示LSTM在数据可用性较少的区间出现更显著的误差；传统ML方法计算需求更低，节省成本并减少碳足迹。

Conclusion: 在特定的能源系统时间序列预测任务中，传统机器学习方法（如XGBoost）可能比深度学习（如LSTM）更具优势，不仅预测精度更高，而且在计算成本和环境可持续性方面也更具吸引力。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of two distinct approaches, XGBoost and Long-Short Term Memory (LSTM), for forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems (DHS). The objective is to explore scenarios in which conventional ML algorithms demonstrate better performance over deep learning networks in time series forecasting and the associated benefits in terms of computational cost and environmental impact. The study focuses on a real-world DHS dataset. Through experimentation and analysis, it is demonstrated that XGBoost consistently outperforms LSTM in this specific forecasting task. The difference is explained by the error distribution illustrating that LSTM makes more significant errors in the intervals of less data availability. The reduced computational demands of conventional ML approaches not only result in cost savings but also minimize the carbon footprint associated with data analysis tasks in energy systems.

</details>


### [97] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于引导离散扩散的模型无关的商品组合优化框架，将商品组合表示为二进制向量，通过学习逆向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并引入基于预期收益估计的奖励引导机制来平衡探索与利用，实验证明该方法在高维场景下能稳定找到近似最优解且对模型误设具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的商品组合优化依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），虽然计算上易于处理，但对模型误设敏感，且难以捕捉复杂客户行为。现有方法在模型偏差和复杂行为建模方面存在固有局限。

Method: 提出模型无关的框架：1）将商品组合编码为二进制向量；2）利用学习到的逆向扩散过程进行随机搜索，避免显式组合枚举；3）引入奖励引导机制，通过估计预期收益来偏置局部转移，在生成过程中有效平衡探索与利用。

Result: 实验表明该方法能持续识别高质量商品组合，在模型误设下保持鲁棒性，在高维场景中常能恢复接近最优的解决方案；同时扩散的生成特性使其能产出多样化的高性能组合，超越单一确定性解。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了生成建模作为数据驱动决策中组合优化的一种可扩展且稳健的新范式的潜力，为商品组合优化提供了超越传统参数化模型的替代路径。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [98] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对硅双极光电晶体管工艺流程序列化、优化与稳定化需数月洁净室时间的问题，提出了一种基于真实制造历史（单器件13-14次工艺批次）的小样本分层结构模型，用于在运行前预测器件增益。研究发现约半数增益方差存在于批次间而非批次内，因此仅基于配方的预测天然受限。在此基础上，论文提供了具有相对不确定性感知信号的正向增益预测器、针对目标增益的逆向配方搜索，以及以批次-晶圆-芯片嵌套物理实体为核心的多层级数据质量评估框架（含跨层级关联评分）。数据集与代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅双极光电晶体管的工艺定制、优化与稳定化需要数月洁净室时间才能完成并测量器件，因此在运行前从工艺参数预测器件增益具有巨大价值。传统虚拟计量法适用于大语料库，而本问题只有13-14次工艺运行，是小样本、分层结构的独特场景，现有方法难以直接适用。

Method: 首先对方差进行分解，确认约一半增益方差存在于工艺批次间而非批次内。然后构建三个核心组件：(1)正向增益预测器，输出相对且具有不确定性感知的信号；(2)逆向搜索工具，可针对目标增益返回对应配方；(3)多层级数据质量评估框架，针对制造中嵌套物理实体（批次、晶圆、芯片）设计，并包含显式的跨层级关联评分。

Result: 方差分解显示批次间方差占比约一半，表明仅用配方的预测在结构上存在固有上限。论文提供了正向预测、逆向搜索和数据质量评估的完整工具链，并发布了标准化数据集与分析代码以实现完全可复现性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在极小样本、分层结构的制造场景中仍可建立有意义的预测模型，并强调了数据质量评估作为模型基础的不可或缺性。其方法论对小样本半导体制造预测具有参考价值，公开的数据与代码也促进了该领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [99] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦聚合方法进行了大规模系统化比较，重建了一个500单元评估矩阵，覆盖5种聚合方法、5个数据集、5种架构和4种攻击条件（干净、符号翻转、高斯、BadNets），并发现Trimmed Mean在干净条件下表现最佳，Krum在对抗条件下最优，同时对BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO框架进行了审计，发现了指标语义偏差和潜在的预测-聚合不一致路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习聚合方法的稳健比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行出处，但现有比较往往缺乏对这些维度的联合考量，且存在执行日志不完整、指标实现语义偏差等问题，因此需要对联邦聚合方法进行严格、可追溯的系统性比较。

Method: 研究者重建了一个500单元格的评估矩阵（seed-1），覆盖5种聚合方法（包括Trimmed Mean、Krum等）、5个数据集、5种架构和4种记录条件（干净、符号翻转、高斯、BadNets），识别并整理了454个原始运行日志和36个修复/重跑执行，对BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO框架进行了代码审计。

Result: Trimmed Mean在干净数据下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均任务内排名（1.70）；Krum在符号翻转和高斯配置下均取得最高记录准确率；这些排名在仅使用21个有完整原始日志的任务对时保持不变。审计发现BadNets指标实际度量的是触发目标标签率（TTLR）而非传统攻击成功率，FedPARETO框架存在预测摘要与实际聚合更新可能不一致的路径。

Conclusion: 该研究结果应被视为在记录配置内的描述性比较，而非统计估计或关于稳健性的普遍性声明。由于每个单元仅有单一种子且攻击和配置溯源的完全不完整，研究存在局限性，但其价值在于揭示了指标语义偏差框架问题，并为联邦聚合方法比较提供了可追溯的执行出处框架。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>


### [100] [Clustered Randomized Smoothing for Stochastic Prediction Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12037)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Frederik Mathiesen,Julian Schumann,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类α-平滑（clustered α-smoothing）框架，通过将噪声样本聚类后局部应用α-平滑，再将结果组合为混合分布，解决了随机平滑在随机多模态回归中因模式坍缩而失效的问题。在驾驶轨迹预测和四旋翼控制两个基准上，分别将Wasserstein距离降低27%、碰撞率降低81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代随机预测器能够建模丰富的多模态结果分布，但在安全关键领域需要确保鲁棒性。随机平滑虽是提升对抗鲁棒性的领先技术，但在随机多模态回归中常因模式坍缩而失败——平均化预测无法反映真实的多模态分布，这是现有方法的核心缺陷。

Method: 提出聚类α-平滑框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 使用任意聚类算法对噪声样本进行划分；(2) 在每个簇内局部应用α-平滑；(3) 将各簇的预测结果组合为混合分布。理论上将平滑分布解释为α-平滑器的混合，并推导平滑预测落于对应不同模态的并集紧致区域的概率下界。

Result: 在驾驶模拟器数据集上的随机轨迹预测中，平均Wasserstein距离比α-平滑降低27%；在四旋翼控制任务（模态对应到目标的可行路径）中，碰撞率比最先进的随机平滑方法降低81%。

Conclusion: 聚类α-平滑有效解决了随机多模态回归中随机平滑的模式坍缩问题，在保留鲁棒性的同时更好地保持多模态分布特性，为安全关键领域的随机预测提供了更可靠的鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [101] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一般拟阵约束下、以任意受控值谕示（controlled oracle）访问的非负子模函数最大化问题。核心结果是针对"恶意贪心交换泊松过程"（SGS-Poisson）的对抗鲁棒性定理：在完全不修改泊松强度、单元素交换规则及恶意丢弃步骤的前提下，算法对非单调目标仍保持1/e的极限近似比，对单调目标保持1-1/e的极限近似比。该结果进一步通过离线到在线归约，得到一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全bandit CMAB算法，具有精确的极限近似-遗憾因子1/e和1-1/e，以及Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模最大化在拟阵约束下是一个经典且重要的组合优化问题。然而，现实场景中值谕示（value oracle）往往存在误差或被对抗性操控，而传统算法对这类噪声缺乏鲁棒性保证。此外，离线算法的鲁棒性直接关系到在线学习（如CMAB）中能否实现有效的离线到在线归约。因此，作者希望证明一个原本高效的离线算法在任意受控噪声下仍能保持其近似比，从而为在线学习算法铺平道路。

Method: 作者证明SGS-Poisson（恶意贪心交换泊松过程）算法具有对抗鲁棒性。关键洞察是：即使谕示被任意偏差ξ所污染，算法不需要修改其核心机制（泊松强度、交换规则、恶意丢弃步骤），仅需在实现中调整，其极限近似因子保持不变，代价仅是目标值损失O(kξ)以及使用Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次谕示查询。随后利用该鲁棒性定理构建离线到在线的归约，用于全bandit设置下的CMAB算法。

Result: 在任意受控谕示满足|f̂(S)-f(S)|≤ξ的条件下，实现返回的可行集期望值至少为(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)（非单调）和(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)（单调），查询复杂度为Õ(nk²ε⁻²)。由此导出的CMAB算法达到精确极限近似-遗憾因子1/e和1-1/e，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: 本文确立了SGS-Poisson算法对受控谕示噪声的强鲁棒性，表明该算法的理论基础足够稳固以支撑在线学习场景。其推论——一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全bandit CMAB算法——在遗憾界和近似因子之间取得了良好的平衡，为实际应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [102] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Click2Poly是一个基于Florence-2视觉语言模型的人机协同AI助手，以QGIS插件形式实现，通过用户点击直接编辑建筑物和墙体矢量图层，加速地理空间应用中人工质量控制和修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物和墙体的矢量映射对地理空间应用至关重要，但人工标注成本高且耗时。虽然深度学习方法改进了自动提取，但为了达到制图标准，仍需人工质量控制和修正复杂案例。因此需要加速这一人工步骤。

Method: 扩展Florence-2视觉语言模型（VLM），使其能响应用户点击直接编辑建筑物或墙体矢量图层。以QGIS插件形式实现，集成到真实生产环境中。

Result: Click2Poly作为QGIS插件在实际生产环境中加速了建筑物和墙体矢量图层的手动编辑工作。

Conclusion: 通过人机协同的交互式AI助手，可以有效加速人工矢量编辑流程，提升地理空间数据生产效率。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [103] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析证明，在Min-IP布尔输入任务上，核注意力机制与全注意力机制之间存在指数级的表达能力差异。具体而言，单头归一化非负核注意力要在三词元序列上取得低于1/2的错误率，需要2^{Ω(m)}个特征，而稠密softmax注意力仅需m维分数和常数温度即可解决相同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全注意力（暴露所有词元对）与核注意力（压缩为固定维度草图）之间的根本性差异，并证明这种差异在竞争候选首次出现时呈指数级放大。

Method: 通过构造Min-IP布尔输入任务，分析rank-one归一化核注意力的表达能力；对单归一化非负核注意力头提出指数级特征数的下界证明，并考虑位置依赖词元映射、因果最终查询等扩展场景；同时对确定性多头多层草图模型证明基于转录本的下界。

Result: 单归一化非负核注意力头需2^{Ω(m)}个特征才能在三个词元序列上达到低于1/2的错误率；而稠密softmax只需m维分数即可；下界随上下文长度增长趋近精确的2^m特征实现。

Conclusion: 核注意力在表达能力上与全注意力存在本质的指数级差距，且该差距在上下文长度仅含两个竞争候选时即已显现，即使允许任意查询相关的仿射读出也无法弥补。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [104] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AutoGrable，一种无需训练GNN即可为表格和关系数据库自动构建图的方法。核心思想是：在最小表到图抽象中，图构建的质量可仅通过行标签与颜色细化类划分的一致性来评判，从而避免昂贵的图模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格和关系数据库本身没有图结构，将GNN应用于它们需要手动或启发式地决定如何构建图（哪些实体作为节点、如何连接等）。现有方法要么依赖人工设计，要么需要对每个候选图训练模型，成本高昂。作者希望找到一个无需训练图模型的图质量评判标准，从而高效地在候选图空间中搜索。

Method: 提出最小表到图抽象（每行是一个节点），利用1-WL界限下的消息传递GNN，将图构建质量归结为行标签与颜色细化类划分的对齐程度。对关联构建（incidence constructions），图结构由所选列决定，因此将建图问题简化为选列问题。通过标签对齐风险（label-alignment risk）对角子集进行评分：即在该划分块上最佳常数预测器的留出风险，加上衡量块填充稀疏度的占有率惩罚项。该评分不物化图也不训练GNN，因此可用贪心策略廉价搜索子集空间。

Result: 实验显示：该评分能在大量候选图中丢弃大部分低质量图同时保留最优图；在受控任务中AutoGrable能恢复产生标签的列；在真实任务中优于固定、随机和任务感知的构造器（在固定预测器下）；并且是唯一一种在无图有帮助时能拒绝建图的方法。

Conclusion: AutoGrable提供了一种无需训练GNN的图构建准则和高效搜索方法，既适用于单表也适用于外键模式，且能判断何时不需要建图，为表格数据的图学习提供了更经济且理论有据的路径。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model. In the minimal table-to-graph abstraction each row is a node, so a message-passing GNN, bounded by 1-WL, sees a construction only as a partition of the rows into colour-refinement classes: a construction is good for a task when that partition separates rows with different labels and does not split rows that share one. AutoGrable turns this criterion into a construction procedure. For incidence constructions the partition is fixed by the selected columns, so building a graph reduces to choosing them, and we score a candidate subset by a label-alignment risk: the held-out risk of the best predictor constant on its blocks, penalised by an occupancy term measuring how thinly the blocks are populated. The score materialises no graph and trains no GNN, so AutoGrable can search the space of subsets greedily and cheaply, and returns the resulting grable for single tables and for foreign-key schemas alike. Our experiments show that over a space of candidate graphs the score discards a large fraction while retaining the best; that AutoGrable recovers the columns that generate the label on controlled tasks and outperforms fixed, random, and task-aware constructors on real tasks under a fixed predictor; and that it is the only method compared that can decline to build a graph when none helps.

</details>


### [105] [Variational Parameter Calibration with Physics-Aware Latent-Space Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11435)
*Qiyao Zhou,Xujia Zhu,Pierre Joli,Yu Cong,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于物理感知神经网络的潜空间框架,用于参数动力学系统的降阶前向建模和变分参数估计。通过自编码器构建可微代理模型,将物理参数映射到预测流场(经潜空间),并利用可观测监督增强潜变量与系统参数的相关性,从而实现端到端可微的参数标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降阶代理模型在状态预测上是准确的,但缺乏系统性的端到端可微公式来将深度学习降阶代理与变分参数估计耦合,导致代理模型在参数标定任务中信息量不足。

Method: 1) 基于自编码器的潜空间框架,构建从物理参数到预测流场的可微代理模型;2) 离线训练时采用可观测监督,使潜变量保留与系统参数相关的信息;3) 在线逆问题在参数空间中通过代理诱导的观测算子求解。

Result: 在两个计算流体力学基准上评估:重建精度不足以支持逆建模;可观测监督改善潜表示的案例级可分性和时间组织性;在含噪声、低分辨率、随机遮蔽和块状部分观测的实测条件下,该框架相比标准代理模型普遍降低标定误差和变异性。

Conclusion: 可观测监督和端到端可微性对于变分参数标定至关重要,该框架在多种现实观测设置下展现出更强的鲁棒性和更低的标定误差。

Abstract: Forward and inverse modeling of parametric dynamical systems requires surrogate models that are not only accurate for state prediction, but also informative for parameter calibration. However, a systematic end-to-end differentiable formulation for coupling deep-learning-based reduced-order surrogates with variational parameter estimation remains underdeveloped. In this work, we introduce a physics-aware neural-network-based latent-space framework for reduced-order forward modeling and variational parameter estimation. The proposed autoencoder-based approach yields a differentiable surrogate that maps physical parameters to predicted flow fields through a latent representation. The observable supervision is used during offline training to encourage the latent variables to retain information correlated with system parameters, while the online inverse problem is solved in the parameter space through the surrogate-induced observation operator. The method is evaluated on two computational-fluid-dynamics benchmarks. The results show that reconstruction accuracy alone is insufficient for inverse modeling, owing to the lack of end-to-end differentiability or physics awareness for variational parameter calibration. Quantitative latent-space analysis further shows that observable supervision improves case-level separability and temporal organization of latent representations. Experiments with realistic measurement settings, including noisy, low-resolution, randomly masked, and block-wise partial observations, demonstrate the robustness of the proposed framework and show that it generally reduces calibration error and variability compared with the standard surrogate models.

</details>


### [106] [PAC-Bayes Beyond Parameter Space: Behavioral Equivalence, Z-Information, and Exact Complexity Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11465)
*Vasant G. Honavar,Satish Kumar Keshri,Neil Ashtekar,Zehao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从行为等价性的角度重新审视PAC-Bayes理论，指出经典KL散度混淆了预测行为不确定性与行为等价实现间的变异性，并通过测度分解证明KL散度可精确分解为行为选择项和实现层项，定义Z-信息为二者之差的负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC-Bayes理论用KL散度控制后验与先验分布间的差异，但在过参数化系统中，许多不同配置对应相同的预测行为，经典KL散度无法区分预测行为的不确定性与行为等价实现间的变异性，这导致泛化复杂度被高估或误解。

Method: 通过可测行为映射形式化行为等价性，利用测度分解（measure disintegration）将配置空间上的概率测度分解为预测行为上的分布与行为纤维上的条件分布，从而得到经典PAC-Bayes KL散度的精确分解（行为选择项 + 期望条件KL项），并定义Z-信息为负数化的实现层贡献，进一步证明行为选择项的变分刻画及其纤维对称化最优代表。

Result: KL散度可精确分解为行为选择项与实现层项；Z-信息刻画了行为等价实现间冗余的复杂度；行为选择项等于所有诱导相同行为分布的后验中KL散度的最小值，由纤维对称化后验达到；对称性、行为保持方向、纤维几何及纤维保持扰动下的不变性均从行为映射结构自然涌现。

Conclusion: 预测行为（而非内部实现配置）是PAC-Bayes复杂度的自然对象，该框架为理解和优化过参数化模型中的泛化复杂度提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: PAC-Bayes theory provides generalization guarantees by controlling the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between posterior and prior distributions over a chosen hypothesis representation. However, predictive risk depends only on the predictive behavior induced by a hypothesis, not on the particular internal realization that implements that behavior. In over-parameterized systems, many distinct configurations induce identical predictive behavior, yet the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence does not distinguish uncertainty over predictive behavior from variation among behaviorally equivalent realizations.
  We show that this distinction induces an exact structural decomposition of classical PAC-Bayes complexity. We formalize behavioral equivalence through a measurable behavior map and use measure disintegration to decompose probability measures on the configuration space into a distribution over predictive behaviors and conditional distributions over behavioral fibers. This yields an exact decomposition of the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence into a behavior-selection term and a realization-level term given by an expected conditional KL within fibers.
  We define Z-information as the negative of this realization-level contribution: the exact gap between the KL divergence and the complexity of uncertainty over predictive behavior alone. We further show that the behavior-selection term admits an exact variational characterization: it is the minimum KL divergence among all posteriors inducing the same distribution over predictive behaviors, attained by a canonical fiber-symmetrized representative.
  Finally, we show that symmetry, behavior-preserving directions, fiber geometry, and invariance under fiber-preserving perturbations arise naturally from the same behavior-map structure. Together, these results identify predictive behavior as the natural object of PAC-Bayes complexity.

</details>


### [107] [Dual-Primal Graph VAEs for Noisy Label Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11473)
*Patrick Stinson,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图VAE架构，用于从嘈杂的众包标注中推断真实标签。该模型利用GAT消息传递机制分别在众包数据集的邻接图及其对偶图上运行编码器和解码器，将真实标签作为潜变量进行无监督表示学习，无需训练单独的分类器，在众包基准上达到最先进性能，并能扩展融合神经网络分类器的侧信息以提升测试时分类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从嘈杂众包标签中推断真实标注是重要的理论与实践问题。神经网络方法虽可替代需要指定生成模型族的经典贝叶斯方法，但现有模型要么仍依赖简单生成模型进行推断，要么需要伪标签或合成数据来训练聚合分类器，缺乏一种既无需显式指定生成模型也无需额外训练数据的端到端方法。

Method: 提出图变分自编码器（Graph VAE）架构：解码器与编码器分别使用基于GAT（图注意力网络）的消息传递机制，作用于众包数据集的邻接图及其对偶图。真实标签被建模为潜变量，实现无监督表示学习，无需训练独立分类器。此外，原始众包图可扩展融合侧信息（如从噪声标签训练的神经网络分类器表征），以增强测试时分类性能。

Result: 在众包基准数据集上达到最先进的性能；通过图增强引入神经网络分类器表征作为侧信息后，显著提升了测试时的分类准确率，验证了方法的通用性和扩展性。

Conclusion: 图VAE架构为众包标签推断提供了一种统一的、无需指定生成模型族或伪标签/合成数据的无监督方案，且其图结构天然支持融合额外信息，展示了良好的泛化能力与实用价值。

Abstract: Inferring the ground-truth from noisy crowdsourced labels is an important theoretical and practical problem. Neural network-based methods offer an alternative to classical Bayesian models which require specifying a family of generative models used for inference. However, current models either still rely on fairly simple generative models for inference or require pseudo-labels or synthetic data to train the aggregate classifier. We propose a graph VAE architecture in which the decoder and encoder use GAT-based message passing on the adjacency graph of a crowdsourced dataset and its dual, respectively. The ground-truth labels are treated as latent variables, enabling unsupervised representation learning without needing to train a separate classifier. We show our model achieves state of the art performance on crowdsourcing benchmarks. We then demonstrate the generality of our approach by showing how the original crowdsourcing graph can be augmented to incorporate side information such as representations from neural network classifiers trained on the noisy labels to substantially boost their classification performance at test time.

</details>


### [108] [Convergence Guarantees of Gradient Descent for Neural Networks via Generalized Lipschitz Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11479)
*Siqiao Mu,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为任意宽度或深度的前馈神经网络建立了梯度下降的收敛性保证，仅需激活函数满足Lipschitz平滑、连续和有界线性条件，损失函数满足Lipschitz平滑（例如均方误差）。研究发现激活函数的Lipschitz性质在重复复合中部分保持，提出了一种广义Lipschitz平滑条件，使得下降引理成立，最终证明L层网络在T次迭代中最小平方梯度范数以O(1/T^{1/L})速率收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度下降收敛性分析通常对网络宽度、深度、初始化或数据集有特殊要求，缺乏对一般前馈神经网络在任意宽度或深度下的收敛保证。本文旨在移除这些限制条件，为更广泛的神经网络结构建立理论收敛性保障。

Method: 核心理论洞见是激活函数的Lipschitz性质在重复复合过程中被部分保留，由此提出一种广义Lipschitz平滑条件：梯度变化的上界由参数空间变化乘以两端参数范数的多项式项决定。该条件同时适用于模型函数和损失函数，从而建立下降引理——只要学习率相对参数范数足够小，损失就会下降。通过控制参数范数不会过快增长，证明收敛速率。

Result: 在L层神经网络中，最小平方梯度范数在T次迭代后以O(1/T^{1/L})的速率收敛到零。该结果适用于linear、tanh、softplus和sigmoid等常见激活函数，以及均方误差损失，无需对初始化或数据集做特殊假设。

Conclusion: 本文为一般前馈神经网络的梯度下降提供了普适的收敛性保证，填补了此前理论分析对初始化、数据集或网络结构限制的空白。该工作为理解深度学习优化过程提供了更一般的理论基础。

Abstract: We establish convergence guarantees of gradient descent for general feedforward neural networks of arbitrary width or depth, with no special requirements on the initialization or dataset. We only assume that the activation functions are Lipschitz smooth, Lipschitz continuous, and linearly bounded--- properties that hold for linear, tanh, softplus, and sigmoid activation functions. For the loss function, we require that it is Lipschitz smooth in the model outputs, which is true for mean-squared error. The key theoretical insight is that the Lipschitz properties of the activation functions are partially preserved even through repeated compositions, leading to a novel generalized Lipschitz smoothness condition where the change in gradient is upper bounded by the change in the parameter space, multiplied by polynomial terms of the parameter norms at both endpoints. This type of condition holds for both the model function and the loss function, enabling a descent lemma where the loss decreases as long as the learning rate is small enough with respect to the parameter norms. By ensuring that the parameter norms do not grow too quickly to infinity, we prove that the minimum squared gradient norm converges to zero in $T$ iterations at rate $O(1/T^{1/L})$ for an $L$-layer neural network.

</details>


### [109] [Defending against Model Extraction for GNNs with Model Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11495)
*Yan Wen,Zhenyi Wang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphRP是一个基于图重编程的主动防御框架，用于抵御针对图神经网络的黑盒模型提取攻击。它通过结构感知门控机制和可学习的拓扑原型创建动态"结构防火墙"，在保持良性查询效用的情况下显著降低攻击者的窃取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN模型提取攻击防御存在严重的"欧几里得偏见"：直接移植图像领域的防御策略（如随机噪声）到图上，忽略了节点之间复杂的拓扑依赖关系，导致严重的效用退化。被动防御方法（如水印）也无法实时阻止攻击者的窃取行为。

Method: GraphRP将模型重编程（Model Reprogramming）重新用于安全防御目的。核心创新是结构感知门控机制（Structure-Aware Gating Mechanism），由可学习的拓扑原型驱动，创造动态"结构防火墙"。该机制选择性调制模型决策边界：对训练流形上的良性查询保持保真度，同时沿扰动方向最大化Fisher信息以对抗恶意查询。理论上在有限损失、最优攻击者和局部二阶近似假设下证明了攻击者估计误差的下界随重编程噪声的结构敏感性增加。

Result: 在硬标签和软标签模型提取攻击的广泛实验上，GraphRP显著降低了攻击有效性，同时保持了良性查询的效用。

Conclusion: GraphRP首次将模型重编程引入图模型提取防御，通过结构感知的动态防御机制解决了现有方法忽略图拓扑依赖的问题，在理论与实践层面均验证了其有效性，为图神经网络的主动安全防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) serve as the backbone for high-stakes applications in Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS). Still, their black-box deployment exposes them to Model Extraction (ME) attacks, in which adversaries steal intellectual property by querying APIs. Existing defenses suffer from a critical ''Euclidean bias'': they transfer image-based strategies (e.g., random noise) to graphs, ignoring the complex topological dependencies between nodes, which often results in severe utility degradation. Passive methods like watermarking also fail to prevent theft in real time. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphRP (Graph Reprogramming Protection), a proactive defense framework that repurposes Model Reprogramming for security. Unlike static perturbations, GraphRP introduces a Structure-Aware Gating Mechanism driven by learnable topological prototypes. This creates a dynamic ''structural firewall'' that selectively modulates the model's decision boundary: it preserves fidelity for benign queries residing on the training manifold, while maximizing the Fisher Information along the perturbation direction for adversarial queries. Under standard assumptions (bounded loss, optimal attacker, and local second-order approximation), we prove a lower bound on the attacker's estimation error that increases with the structural sensitivity of the reprogramming noise. Extensive experiments on both hard-label and soft-label ME attacks demonstrate that GraphRP significantly degrades attack effectiveness while preserving benign utility.

</details>


### [110] [HyperFix: Combinatorial Nonlinear Correction for Task Vector Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11499)
*Hyo Seo Kim,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperFix，一个轻量级超网络，用于预测基于任务子集条件的权重空间非线性修正，从而解决跨不同任务子集的任务向量合并问题。HyperFix在单任务、双任务和三任务子集上训练一次，即可泛化到更大的子集，无需针对每个子集重复调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的任务向量合并方法大多针对特定任务子集使用标量调优，需要跨不同子集重复调优，且局限于线性缩放，无法很好地处理任务子集可能变化的情况。

Method: 将跨不同任务子集的合并问题形式化为组合修正问题，引入HyperFix轻量级超网络来预测基于子集条件的非线性权重空间修正。通过对局部扰动的分析，界定了超越线性合并的残差修正范围，并从小任务更新中学习该修正。HyperFix在单/双/三任务子集上训练，泛化到更大子集。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，HyperFix优于现有的任务向量合并方法，同时降低了调优成本。

Conclusion: HyperFix通过一次训练即可泛化到任意大小的任务子集合并，避免了逐子集优化，在性能提升的同时大幅降低调优开销，为任务向量合并提供了更高效的非线性解决方案。

Abstract: Task vectors enable model merging without joint retraining. In practice, the subset of task vectors to be merged may vary, but many existing methods use scalar tuning for a particular subset, requiring repeated tuning across subsets and restricting task vector merging to linear rescaling. We therefore formulate merging across varying task subsets as a combinatorial correction problem and introduce HyperFix, a lightweight hypernetwork that predicts subset-conditioned nonlinear corrections in weight space. Trained once on singleton, pair, and triple subsets from a task bank, HyperFix generalizes to larger subsets without per-subset optimization. Our local perturbation analysis bounds the residual correction beyond linear merging and motivates learning it from small task updates. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show that HyperFix outperforms existing task vector merging methods while reducing tuning cost.

</details>


### [111] [RelShap: Relationally Consistent Shapley Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11508)
*Seungeun Lee,Joao Fonseca,Julia Stoyanovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RelShap是一个将关系约束和数据溯源纳入Shapley值计算的框架，通过限制背景数据和联合评估为关系有效配置，生成更忠实于数据生成过程的解释，同时利用函数依赖加速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习流水线将关系数据扁平化为单表表示，丢弃了结构约束。基于Shapley值的特征归因方法依赖特征独立性假设，会评估在底层数据中不可能出现的组合，从而产生误导性解释。

Method: RelShap框架将关系约束和数据溯源融入Shapley值计算，限制背景数据和联合评估为关系有效配置。该框架与估计器无关，可与Kernel SHAP、Monte Carlo和Leverage SHAP组合而不改变其采样或加权性质。函数依赖在特征联合上诱导等价类，RelShap利用这一点在不改变Shapley值的情况下减少运行时间，并提供预期加速的组合学特征刻画。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型和估计器上的实验表明，RelShap生成的解释更忠实于数据生成过程，在受控设置中正确识别主导特征，而现有方法（包括Conditional SHAP和ManifoldShap）无法做到。

Conclusion: RelShap通过引入关系约束有效解决了传统Shapley值归因在关系数据上的失效问题，在保持与现有估计器兼容的同时提升了解释忠实度和计算效率。

Abstract: Machine learning pipelines commonly flatten relational data into single-table representations, discarding structural constraints. Widely used Shapley value-based feature attributions then rely on feature independence, evaluating the model on combinations that could never arise in the underlying data, producing misleading explanations. We propose RelShap, a framework that incorporates relational constraints and data provenance into Shapley value computation, restricting both background data and coalition evaluation to relationally valid configurations. The framework is estimator-agnostic and composes with Kernel SHAP, Monte Carlo, and Leverage SHAP without altering their sampling or weighting properties. Functional dependencies further induce equivalence classes over feature coalitions, which RelShap exploits to reduce runtime without changing Shapley values; we provide a combinatorial characterization of the expected speedup. Experiments across multiple datasets, models, and estimators show that RelShap produces explanations that are more faithful to the data-generating process, correctly identifying the dominant feature in controlled settings where existing methods, including Conditional SHAP and ManifoldShap, do not. Our code is available at: https://github.com/duneag2/relshap.

</details>


### [112] [Let it Cook: Learning to Wait in Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11511)
*Christopher Watson,Arjun Krishna,Dinesh Jayaraman,Rajeev Alur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"学习等待"（learning to wait）的概念，通过训练一个"等待策略"来决定何时何地暂停感知和行动，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下减少感知与决策的频率。方法采用具有字典序目标的强化学习，在4个离散状态家庭任务和3个连续状态环境中验证了有效性，部分任务中等待时间可超过任务总时长的50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多序贯决策任务中，智能体并不需要在每个时间步都主动感知和行动。例如煮咖啡这类任务中存在大量无需持续监控的时段，此时智能体可以选择等待以节省资源或将注意力转向其他任务。现有方法默认智能体全程主动参与，忽略了这种"可等待"的机会，造成了资源浪费。

Method: 将"学习等待"形式化为在不牺牲任务性能（如任务完成总时长）的前提下，最小化感知与决策频率的问题。提出采用字典序（lexicographically ordered）多目标强化学习方法训练等待策略，使策略能在保证任务性能优化的前提下，优先或同时学习何时何地进行等待。

Result: 在4个离散状态家庭任务和3个连续状态环境中进行实验，结果表明该方法成功学习到等待行为，并能将预训练策略适配为在适当时机等待。不同任务允许的等待量各异，但方法都能找到包含大量等待的解决方案，部分任务中等待时间超过任务总时长的50%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了"等待"是序贯决策中一种有效且可学习的资源节约策略，通过字典序强化学习可系统性地训练智能体在保持任务性能的同时大幅减少感知与决策频率，为多任务调度和资源受限环境下的智能体设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In sequential decision making, an agent typically observes its environment and acts at every timestep. However, such active participation may not always be necessary; tasks such as brewing coffee include periods that are served equally well by letting the environment evolve without constant monitoring and control. During such periods, the agent could simply wait to conserve its resources, or redirect its attention to another task. We capitalize on these opportunities by training a "waiting policy" that decides where and how long to wait. This involves forgoing sensing to commit to a wait action, representing a deliberate pause for a set number of timesteps. We formalize "learning to wait" as minimizing the frequency of sensing and decision making without sacrificing task performance (e.g., the total amount of time to complete a task). To train a waiting policy, we propose an approach that employs reinforcement learning with lexicographically ordered objectives. In experiments across 4 discrete-state household tasks and 3 continuous-state environments, we show that our approach successfully learns waiting behaviors, and can adapt pre-trained policies to wait where appropriate. While different tasks permit different amounts of waiting without sacrificing task performance, our approach consistently finds solutions with significant waiting, sometimes waiting for over 50 percent of the task duration.

</details>


### [113] [FLARE++: Low-rank attention with dynamic attention routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11519)
*Vedant Puri,Yongjie Jessica Zhang,Levent Burak Kara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLARE++是一种动态令牌路由的低秩注意力架构，通过复用FLARE的编码器构建输入条件化的路由查询，替代了FLARE中固定的学习查询参数。该方法保持了线性O(NM)复杂度，仅使用标准缩放点积注意力操作，并提供多GPU上下文并行实现。在PDE代理基准上比FLARE平均提升24%，在Long Range Arena上平均准确率提升2.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自注意力在非规则域上的PDE代理模型中表现优异，但二次方计算成本限制了高分辨率问题的应用。FLARE通过M<<N个学习潜查询实现低秩注意力，避免这一成本，但其查询是固定参数，训练后对所有输入使用相同的查询模板，缺乏输入适应性。

Method: FLARE++复用FLARE自身的编码器来构建路由查询：学习到的潜种子（latent seeds）驱动一次额外的编码调用，将N个输入令牌聚合成M个输入条件化的查询，这些查询再决定相同令牌如何被压缩和重分配。该方法保留FLARE的显式低秩分解和线性O(NM)复杂度，整个路由操作仅使用标准缩放点积注意力（SDPA）调用表达，并提供多GPU上下文并行实现，将输入令牌分片到各设备而无需在单个设备上聚合完整令牌序列。

Result: 在一系列标准PDE代理基准上，FLARE++优于固定查询的FLARE平均24%；在Long Range Arena上平均准确率提升2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过让路由查询动态适应每个输入，FLARE++成功打破了FLARE固定查询模板的限制，在保持线性复杂度优势的同时显著提升了性能，为高效低秩注意力模型在高分辨率PDE代理问题上的应用提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Full self-attention is a strong token mixer for PDE surrogates on irregular domains, but its quadratic cost limits its use on high-resolution problems. Efficient latent-attention models such as the Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE) avoid that cost by routing all N tokens through M << N learned latent queries, but those queries are parameters: once trained, the same learned query templates serve every input. We remove this restriction with FLARE++, a low-rank attention architecture with dynamic token routing. FLARE++ reuses FLARE's own encoder to build its routing queries: learned latent seeds drive one extra encode call that gathers the N input tokens into M input-conditioned queries, and those queries then determine how the same tokens are compressed and redistributed. This preserves FLARE's explicit low-rank factorization and linear O(NM) complexity, and expresses the complete routing operation with standard scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) calls alone. We also provide a multi-GPU context-parallel implementation that shards input tokens across devices without ever gathering the full token sequence on one of them. FLARE++ is competitive across a set of standard PDE surrogate benchmarks, improving on fixed-query FLARE by 24% on average, and it gains 2.3 points of average accuracy on Long Range Arena.

</details>


### [114] [Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning in Digital Twin-Based Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11532)
*Qasim Zia,Saide Zhu,Haoxin Wang,Zafar Iqbal,Yingshu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向数字孪生车载自组织网络（DT-VANET）的分层联邦迁移学习（HFTL）框架，通过车辆类型聚类和数据质量评分机制，解决联邦学习在数据异构性和数据稀疏性下的全局模型精度不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在DT-VANET中虽能提供数据隐私保护，但面对车辆间涌现的数据异构性和数据稀疏性问题时，难以充分训练全局模型，导致对不同类型车辆的预测精度不理想。

Method: 1) 将联邦迁移学习（FTL）与基于车辆类型的聚类相结合；2) 构建DT-VANET框架，设计云服务器模型更新和集群内联邦迁移学习两种算法；3) 提出基于数据质量评分的机制，防止恶意车辆影响全局模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上进行了详细实验，考虑多种性能指标，验证了所提算法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: HFTL框架结合车辆聚类和迁移学习，配合数据质量评分机制，能有效提升DT-VANET中全局模型的预测精度，并抵御恶意车辆的干扰。

Abstract: In recent research on the Digital Twin-based Vehicular Ad hoc Network(DT-VANET), Federated Learning (FL) has shown its ability to provide data privacy. However, Federated learning struggles to adequately train a global model when confronted with data heterogeneity and data sparsity among vehicles, which ensure suboptimal accuracy in making precise predictions for different vehicle types. To address these challenges, this paper combines Federated Transfer Learning (FTL) to conduct vehicle clustering related to types of vehicles and proposes a novel Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning (HFTL). We construct a framework for DT-VANET, along with two algorithms designed for cloud server model updates and intra-cluster federated transfer learning, to improve the accuracy of the global model. In addition, we developed a data quality score-based mechanism to prevent the global model from being affected by malicious vehicles. Lastly, detailed experiments on real-world datasets are conducted, considering different performance metrics that verify the effectiveness and efficiency of our algorithm.

</details>


### [115] [Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) From Model- and Feature-Space Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11541)
*Manya Singh,Mark T. Keane,Arjun Pakrashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了鲁棒模糊性检测（RAD）框架，通过模型空间一致性和特征空间一致性两个互补指标来量化机器学习模型预测的模糊性，并通过RAD图提供可解释的模糊性来源表征，最终在下游应用中通过RAD Pareto排序对最模糊样本进行弃权处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型应在允许的变化下保持预测一致性，但实际部署中很难识别预测的模糊性（如模型被功能等价替代或输入受轻微扰动时预测显著改变）。在高风险决策场景中，模型应弃权或标记此类模糊预测供人工检查，但现有方法难以在部署后有效识别这种模糊性。

Method: 提出RAD（鲁棒模糊性检测）框架，使用两个互补指标：模型空间一致性（Model-Space Consistency）和特征空间一致性（Feature-Space Consistency），两者构成RAD评分对（RAD Score-Pair），并通过RAD图可视化，提供对模糊性来源的可解释表征。

Result: 在具有系统性控制重叠的合成数据集以及多个无法直接检查模糊性水平的真实数据集上进行评估；下游应用中，通过RAD Pareto排序对样本排序并对最模糊样本弃权，性能与现有的基于拒绝的方法相当。

Conclusion: RAD框架提供了一种可解释的、部署后有效的模糊性量化方法，能够在高风险场景中帮助模型弃权或标记模糊预测，其下游弃权表现与现有方法相当，同时提供了更丰富的模糊性来源分析。

Abstract: Machine learning models should be robust, in the sense of remaining predictively consistent under permissible variations. A model's predictions should ideally remain unchanged when it is replaced by a functionally equivalent one, or when its inputs are subject to minor, admissible perturbations. If such changes alter a prediction significantly, then the prediction is "ambiguous" with respect to the model. Models should abstain from making such ambiguous predictions and/or should flag them for human inspection, especially in high-stakes decision-making scenarios. However, in practice, such ambiguity is not easy to identify once a model is deployed. Here, the Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) framework is advanced for quantifying predictive ambiguity using two complementary metrics: Model-Space Consistency and Feature-Space Consistency. These two scores, the RAD Score-Pair, visualised through the RAD Plot, provide an interpretable characterisation of the sources of ambiguity and the actions a user may consider in response. RAD is evaluated on synthetic datasets with systematically controlled overlap, as well as several real-world datasets where the level of ambiguity cannot be directly inspected. Finally, we demonstrate a downstream application of RAD where samples are ranked by their RAD Pareto-Rank and the most ambiguous are abstained from prediction, achieving performance comparable to existing rejection-based approaches.

</details>


### [116] [Certifying What Helps Customer-Return Timing: A Screen-and-Confirm Test for Conditioning Signals, and Why Decay Is Nearly Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11555)
*Sang Su Lee,Vineeth Loganathan,Shishir Dash,Vijay Raghavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于可靠评估时序点过程（TPP）模型条件特征是否有用的方法学工具，包括"筛选-确认"协议和模型无关的性能上限，并通过在多个真实基准上的实验证明：连续时间衰减本身几乎已足够，额外的条件特征要么冗余要么有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者不断向客户回归模型和时序点过程模型添加更多信号（如生命周期价值、品类、频次、日历、地理等），但缺乏可信的方法来判定这些条件特征是否真的提升了事件时间的预测能力。已有的"特征X无帮助"的零假设只有在模型本身具备发现信号能力时才有意义，否则可能只是方法乏力而非特征无用。

Method: 论文贡献了两项工具：(i) 筛选-确认（screen-and-confirm）协议：通过植入已知强度的耦合作为正对照，确认模型能恢复该信号，从而使真实数据上的零结果可以解读为"无信号"而非"方法弱"；(ii) 模型无关的性能上限：量化任何协变量对客户回归时序的可预测性上限（结果显示仅能解释个位数百分比的间隔方差，即回归几乎无记忆性）。

Result: 在三个公开基准（Amazon、Taobao、RetailRocket）和真实市场（Thumbtack）上，论文证明：连续时间衰减——早已被证明优于冻结强度模型——几乎已足够充分，而领域内不断添加的条件特征在其之上要么统计上无效（公开基准上为零结果，最多0.06 NLL），要么无效至轻度有害（市场上）。

Conclusion: 论文不声称发现了衰减有效这一事实，其真正贡献在于将"条件化无帮助"这一论断转化为可检验、可认证的声明，并提供了一套诚实评估的框架，同时披露了在读出/泄漏陷阱中遇到的坑及其修正过程。

Abstract: Practitioners enrich customer-return models with ever more signals (lifetime value, category, recency/frequency, calendar, geography), and the temporal-point-process (TPP) literature follows suit with covariate- and external-covariate-conditioned intensities. But does any of it improve the timing, and how would you know? A null ("feature X doesn't help") is only meaningful if the model could have found a signal. We make two contributions--a method and a measurement--to answer this credibly. (i) A screen-and-confirm protocol that certifies whether a candidate signal improves a TPP's event-timing likelihood: a positive control plants a coupling of known strength and confirms the model recovers it, so a real-data null can be read as "no signal" rather than "weak method." The control is validated for categorical and continuous encodings, and on a real clock-driven dataset (NYC taxi hour-of-day). (ii) A model-free ceiling quantifying how little of customer-return timing is point-predictable at all (a single-digit percentage of gap variance from any covariate; returns are near-memoryless). With these we certify a clean result on three public benchmarks (Amazon, Taobao, RetailRocket) and a real marketplace (Thumbtack): the inter-event clock--continuous-time decay, long known to beat frozen-intensity models--is nearly sufficient, and the conditioning the field keeps adding is redundant or harmful on top of it (statistically null on the public benchmarks, at most 0.06 NLL; null to mildly harmful on the marketplace). We do not claim to discover that decay helps; our contribution is the tools that turn "conditioning doesn't help" into a checkable, certified statement--plus an honest-evaluation account of the read-out/leakage pitfalls we hit and retracted.

</details>


### [117] [When Offline Evaluation Misleads: A Diagnostic Protocol for Reward and Policy Selection in Delayed-Feedback Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11560)
*Sang Su Lee,Vineeth Loganathan,Shishir Dash,Vijay Raghavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个有序诊断协议，用于在延迟反馈环境下评估上下文多臂老虎机（CMAB）的奖励代理和策略候选。该协议从两个轴筛选：对齐性（优化奖励是否推动北极星指标）和可学习性（老虎机能否识别奖励最优策略），并通过公开基准和合成数据验证，在真实部署系统上说明。揭示两个教训：单一离线指标可能误排奖励；无法预知最优单一消息时，个性化溢价容易被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化营销中使用CMAB能带来商业价值，但真正重要的下游转化指标观测延迟数周，无法驱动在线学习。团队通常用快速代理奖励训练老虎机，但需判断CMAB是否值得其复杂度。传统的离线检查方法（批量离线策略评估、边际臂区分检验、置信区间）在延迟反馈下会系统性误导，因此需要更可靠的诊断方法。

Method: 提出一个有序诊断协议，对奖励与策略候选在两个轴上进行筛查：1) 对齐性——优化该奖励是否真正推动北极星指标；2) 可学习性——老虎机能否从该奖励中识别出奖励最优策略。协议在信任任何报告提升前执行，并通过公开离线策略评估基准和可控合成数据生成器进行验证。

Result: 在公开离线策略评估基准和可控合成生成器上验证了协议（这些场景中真实情况已知），并在一个已部署的大型市场推送系统上进行了说明（五臂、一次分割，证据是方向性的而非充分检验的）。揭示两个反复出现的教训：N1）单一离线数值可能误排奖励，密集奖励信号提供更多学习空间，静态估计中看似持平的奖励在在线学习中会拉开差距；N2）若无法预先判断单一最优消息，按用户策略部分只是避免押错，这看起来像个性化实为鲁棒性，"个性化溢价"容易被夸大。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是方法论而非算法性的：提供有序协议、协议揭示的两个教训，以及在延迟反馈CMAB上应用的端到端经验。核心结论是传统离线检查在延迟反馈下可能误导，需要在对齐性和可学习性两个维度上进行系统诊断。

Abstract: Personalizing marketing messages with contextual multi-armed bandits (CMABs) drives real business value, yet the objective that ultimately matters - a downstream conversion - is observed only weeks later, too late to drive online learning. Teams therefore train the bandit on a fast proxy reward, and separately must judge whether a contextual bandit is worth its complexity over sending one best message. Settling both decisions with the usual offline checks - a batch off-policy estimate, a marginal arm-discrimination test, a confidence interval - can mislead systematically under delayed feedback. We give an ordered diagnostic protocol that screens a reward-and-policy candidate on two axes, alignment (does optimizing the reward move the north-star?) and learnability (can the bandit identify the reward-optimal policy?), before trusting any reported lift. We validate it where the truth is known - a public off-policy-evaluation benchmark and a controllable synthetic generator - and illustrate it on a deployed large-marketplace push system (where, with five arms and one split, the evidence is directional rather than powered). Two lessons recur. (N1) A single offline number can mis-rank rewards: a denser reward signal gives the bandit more to learn from, so rewards that look tied in a static estimate pull apart once learning happens online. (N2) If you cannot tell in advance which single message is best, a per-user policy partly just avoids betting on the wrong one - that looks like personalization but is really robustness, so a "personalization premium" is easily overstated. Our contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic: the ordered protocol, the two lessons it surfaces, and the end-to-end experience of applying it to a delayed-feedback CMAB.

</details>


### [118] [Sparse and robust geometric twin support vector machine via asymmetric RoBoSS loss function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11567)
*Kai Qi,Xinji Huang,Hongchun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非凸非光滑aR损失函数和l1范数惩罚的几何孪生支持向量机（aRSGTSVM），用于处理包含冗余特征、标签噪声和特征噪声的真实世界数据。该方法兼具特征选择、鲁棒性和平滑性，并通过近端梯度下降算法高效求解，在分类、回归及中国股市指数跟踪任务中均表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中训练数据常包含冗余特征、标签噪声和特征噪声，而标准SVM采用l2范数惩罚和hinge损失函数，缺乏特征选择能力且对噪声敏感，亟需一种既能稀疏化又能鲁棒应对噪声的新型SVM模型。

Method: 提出一种新颖的aR（非对称、鲁棒、有界、稀疏、平滑）损失函数，结合l1范数惩罚构建几何孪生SVM模型；利用影响函数进行鲁棒性的统计分析；针对非凸非光滑优化问题，设计快速稳定的近端梯度下降求解算法。

Result: 在合成数据集和UCI数据集上与多种先进方法对比，aRSGTSVM展现了优越性；应用于中国股市不同指数的跟踪任务中，也获得了令人满意的表现。

Conclusion: aRSGTSVM有效解决了标准SVM在特征选择和噪声鲁棒性方面的不足，在分类、回归及实际金融应用（指数跟踪）中均验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In real-world scenarios, the training data usually contains redundant features, label noise and feature noise, which provide severe challenges for the efficiency of machine learning methods. Since standard support vector machine (SVM) adopts $l_2$-norm penalty and hinge loss function, it lacks the ability of selecting significant features and is sensitive to noise. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel asymmetric, robust, bounded, sparse and smooth (aR) loss function for $l_1$-norm penalized geometric twin SVM (aRSGTSVM) to handle classification and regression tasks. The $l_1$-norm penalty can achieve the feature selection. The proposed aR loss function can not only effectively mitigate the impact of label noise, but also significantly enhance the stability to resampling noise, i.e., the zero-mean feature noise around the boundary hyperplanes. Furthermore, a statistical analysis of the robustness of aRSGTSVM was also conducted using the influence function. Since aRSGTSVM involves nonconvex and nonsmooth optimization, we develop a fast and stable proximal gradient descent based solving algorithm. Compared with related state-of-the-art methods, experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed aRSGTSVM on both synthetic and UCI datasets. Furthermore, we apply aRSGTSVM to index tracking tasks, where results for tracking the different indices in the China stock market show that it can achieve satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [119] [RECAST: A Machine-Learning Framework for Correction and Super-Resolution of Coarse-Grid PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11572)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAST是一个机器学习框架，通过在学习的时间步进循环中加入误差校正，并从校正后的粗网格历史重建细网格状态，从而在保持粗网格演化效率的同时恢复PDE模拟的精度。在六个一维PDE系统上，粗化8-16倍时，RECAST将时间平均相对误差降低了约50-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗网格数值求解器能显著降低时间相关PDE模拟的计算成本，但分辨率不足往往会同时降低解的轨迹精度和空间保真度。作者希望在不放弃粗网格演化效率的前提下恢复丢失的精度。

Method: RECAST结合了两个关键组件：(1) 在数值时间步进循环内学习误差校正（learned correction）；(2) 从校正后的粗网格历史重建对应的细网格状态（fine-grid state reconstruction）。

Result: 在六个1D PDE系统（涵盖输运、扩散、色散、反应和波动动力学）上，使用8-16倍空间粗化和1000步闭环rollout（未见初始条件），RECAST与细网格参考解保持紧密一致，将时间平均相对误差降低约50-92%。此外，RECAST能泛化到未见PDE参数值，并在5000步rollout中比当代粗校正架构取得更低误差和更好的长时程一致性。

Conclusion: RECAST的学习校正和重建能力使得可以在显著更粗的网格上进行PDE演化而不损失解保真度，为机器学习加速高维数值模拟提供了概念验证路径。

Abstract: Coarse-grid numerical solvers can substantially reduce the computational cost of time-dependent PDE simulation, but under-resolution often degrades both the trajectory and the spatial fidelity of the solution. We introduce RECAST (Recurrent Error Correction And Super-resolution of coarse-grid Trajectories), a machine-learning framework designed to restore this lost accuracy while retaining coarse-grid evolution. RECAST combines learned correction within the numerical time-stepping loop with reconstruction of the corresponding fine-grid state from the corrected coarse history. We evaluate the framework on six one-dimensional PDE systems spanning transport, diffusion, dispersion, reaction, and wave dynamics, using spatial grids coarsened by factors of 8-16 and 1000-step closed-loop rollouts from unseen initial conditions. Across the test cases, RECAST remains closely aligned with the fine-grid reference solutions and reduces time-averaged relative error by approximately 50-92% compared with the corresponding uncorrected coarse-grid solvers. Additional tests show generalization to unseen PDE parameter values, while comparison with a contemporary coarse-correction architecture shows that RECAST achieves lower error and better long-horizon agreement with the fine-grid reference over 5000-step rollouts. These results demonstrate that the learned correction and reconstruction capabilities of RECAST can enable substantially coarser PDE evolution without the corresponding loss of solution fidelity, providing a proof-of-concept route toward machine-learning acceleration of higher-dimensional numerical simulations across science and engineering.

</details>


### [120] [Dion3: Full-Stack Orthogonal Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11612)
*Noah Amsel,Jack Zhang,Kwangjun Ahn,Ali Naeimi,Austin Feng,Berlin Chen,Tri Dao,John Langford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dion3是Muon优化器的一个修订版本，通过Gram Newton-Schulz算法、CuteDSL内核和megabatching策略分别降低计算FLOP、加速内核和减少通信开销，并提出只正交化动量矩阵部分行的更新规则，在匹配或改善Muon损失性能的同时将优化器步进时间最多缩短6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器因其三次时间的Newton-Schulz正交化步骤而具有显著的计算开销，且当权重分片时通信开销进一步叠加，削弱了Muon在许多场景下的优势。

Method: 1) 提出Gram Newton-Schulz算法，降低正交化步骤的FLOP成本；2) 使用CuteDSL内核，利用对称性加速正交化；3) 提出megabatching策略减少通信开销；4) 提出简化更新规则——每步只选择动量矩阵的一部分行进行正交化，相比Dion（另一个压缩版Muon）在速度和性能上均有提升。

Result: Dion3在匹配或改善Muon损失性能的前提下，将优化器步进时间最多减少6倍，并在速度和性能上均优于Dion。

Conclusion: Dion3作为Muon的即插即用替代品，通过多层次的优化（算法、内核、通信和更新规则）显著降低了Muon的计算与通信开销，同时保持或提升训练性能，并已通过dion包开源发布。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer incurs a significant overhead cost due to its cubic-time Newton-Schulz orthogonalization step. When weights are sharded, communication overhead compounds this computational cost, eroding the benefits of Muon in many settings. We present Dion3, a revision of Muon that targets this overhead at every level of the stack. Our Gram Newton-Schulz algorithm reduces the FLOP cost of orthogonalization, our CuteDSL kernels accelerate it by exploiting symmetry, and our megabatching strategy reduces communication overhead. Moreover, we propose a simple change to the update rule that cuts costs even further: selecting only a fraction of the momentum matrix's rows to orthogonalize at each step. This update rule improves on Dion (another "compressed" version of Muon), in both speed and performance. Overall, Dion3 matches or improves on the loss achieved by Muon but reduces optimizer step time by up to 6x. Dion3 is available via the dion package (https://github.com/microsoft/dion) as a drop-in replacement for Muon.

</details>


### [121] [A Local Sinkhorn Framework for Conditional Distribution Reconstruction of Multidimensional Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11613)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部Sinkhorn散度框架，用于多维随机场的条件分布重构。该方法利用去偏Sinkhorn散度构建可微且计算高效的局部分布匹配目标来训练随机神经网络（SNNs），并建立了理论泛化误差估计，揭示了正则化参数对近似偏差与统计效率之间权衡的影响。该框架在几何保真度、统计效率和计算可扩展性之间提供了实用的折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确局部最优传输方法在条件分布重构中计算成本高昂，难以扩展到多维随机场模型。作者希望开发一种可扩展的替代方案，既保留几何保真度又能兼顾计算效率和统计效率，用于不确定性量化和概率科学机器学习。

Method: 1) 提出基于去偏Sinkhorn散度的局部分布匹配目标函数；2) 利用该目标训练随机神经网络（SNNs），实现可微且计算高效的优化；3) 建立理论泛化误差估计，刻画近似偏差与统计效率之间的权衡关系；4) 通过多个数值实验与其他损失函数及机器学习不确定性量化框架进行对比验证。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的局部Sinkhorn散度框架在重构准确性和计算效率之间取得了有效平衡，并对多维随机系统保持良好的可扩展性，优于其他损失函数和现有机器学习不确定性量化框架。

Conclusion: 局部Sinkhorn散度框架为多维随机场的条件分布重构提供了一种可扩展且实用的替代方案，在几何保真度、统计效率与计算可扩展性之间实现了良好权衡，适用于不确定量化和概率科学机器学习场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a local Sinkhorn divergence framework for conditional distribution reconstruction of multidimensional random fields. By utilizing the debiased Sinkhorn divergence, our proposed approach develops a differentiable and computationally efficient local distribution matching objective to train stochastic neural networks (SNNs). Furthermore, we establish theoretical generalization error estimates for our local Sinkhorn divergence framework, which explicitly characterizes the trade-off between approximation bias and statistical efficiency controlled by the regularization parameter and reveals how our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence loss function can be efficiently applied to learning multidimensional random field models. The proposed framework provides a scalable alternative to exact local optimal transport for conditional distribution reconstruction, offering a practical compromise between geometric fidelity, statistical efficiency, and computational scalability for uncertainty quantification and probabilistic scientific machine learning. Through various numerical examples, we compare our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework with other loss functions to train SNNs and with other machine-learning-based uncertainty quantification frameworks, demonstrating that the proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework achieves an effective balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency while maintaining good scalability for multidimensional stochastic systems.

</details>


### [122] [FM-LLM: A frequency-enhanced mixture-of-experts framework for adapting LLMs to time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11623)
*Rentao Gu,Yihang Ding,Junjie Li,Yi Ding,Weijing Sang,Xiaoli Huo,Xin Qin,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FM-LLM是一个无需文本提示、基于频率感知的冻结大语言模型时间序列预测框架，通过傅里叶分析网络注入谐波表征，使用非对称混合专家解码器分离全局周期性与非周期残差建模，在11个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的跨模态时间序列预测方法过度依赖文本提示进行模态对齐，不仅引入大量计算开销，还未能充分利用时间序列数据中丰富的频谱动态信息。

Method: 提出FM-LLM框架：1）基于FAN的频谱token对齐器将结构化的谐波表征直接注入冻结LLM；2）非对称MoE解码器实现角色分离——共享专家配合轻量FAN层重建全局周期骨干，路由专家仅用标准FFN建模非周期残差动态；3）时间-频率混合损失函数联合优化时间精度与频谱一致性，缓解长序列自回归滚动中的误差累积。

Result: 在11个公共基准的78项评估指标中59项达到SOTA；与最强自回归LLM基线相比，MSE平均提升5.3%，MAE平均提升5.6%，最大分别提升8.0%和8.4%；在10%少样本和零样本场景下保持优越性能，展示强迁移能力。

Conclusion: FM-LLM验证了无需提示的频谱感知策略能有效释放冻结LLM在时间序列预测中的潜力，其非对称MoE设计在计算效率与建模精度间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have spurred cross-modal solutions for time-series forecasting. However, existing methods rely heavily on textual prompts for modality alignment-introducing nontrivial computational overhead and failing to leverage the rich spectral dynamics inherent in time-series data. To enable prompt-free, frequency-aware adaptation of frozen LLMs, we propose FM-LLM (Frequency-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for adapting LLMs to Time Series Forecasting), an autoregressive framework grounded in constrained asymmetric coupling. A Fourier Analysis Network (FAN)-based spectral token aligner injects structured harmonic representations directly into the frozen LLM with numerical compatibility. An asymmetric Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder enforces role separation: shared experts with lightweight FAN layers reconstruct the global periodic backbone, while routed experts-restricted to standard FFNs-specialize in modeling non-periodic residual dynamics. A time-frequency hybrid loss function jointly optimizes temporal accuracy and spectral consistency, mitigating error accumulation during long-horizon autoregressive rollouts. Evaluated across eleven public benchmarks, FM-LLM achieves state-of-the-art performance on 59 out of 78 evaluation metrics. Compared to the strongest autoregressive LLM-based baseline, it delivers average improvements of 5.3% in MSE and 5.6% in MAE, with maximum gains reaching 8.0% for MSE and 8.4% for MAE. FM-LLM also demonstrates robust transferability, maintaining superior performance in 10% few-shot and zero-shot forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [Transferable Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) Estimation Model from Multi-Sensor Data with Sparse Field Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11638)
*Pann Thinzar Seint,Bryan Atwood,Subas Chhatkuli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个操作化框架，基于单一全球训练的CNN模型，结合GEDI激光雷达参考数据与多种遥感数据（Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2、DEM），通过轻量级实地样地标定（scale-and-bias校正及随机森林微调）实现森林地上生物量的空间连续估算，验证中R²约0.82、RMSE约15 Mg/ha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林AGB的空间连续量化对碳核算至关重要。传统实地清查精度高但空间稀疏，GEDI虽提供广泛生物量样本但缺乏空间连续性且对高生物量森林存在系统性低估。现有方法难以同时满足全局泛化与局地精度。

Method: ①单一全局CNN模型，融合Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2及DEM数据；②训练数据来自GEDI Level-4A多区域、多季节参考数据；③10m共享网格数据规范化，提取植被指数和极化比值；④混合log域SmoothL1与RMSE损失函数应对偏态分布；⑤轻量级现场标定流程：scale-and-bias校正+随机森林微调（10折交叉验证）适配局地场景。

Result: 全局GEDI模型验证R²≈0.78、RMSE≈22 Mg/ha；经现场标定后R²≈0.82、RMSE≈15 Mg/ha，优于未标定的全局模型及ESA CCI Biomass产品。

Conclusion: 该框架证明全球训练模型配合轻量级现场标定可有效平衡空间连续性与局地精度，避免针对每个景观重新训练，具有操作化部署的可行性。

Abstract: Spatially continuous quantification of forest above-ground biomass (AGB) is what makes carbon accounting credible and mitigation strategies actionable. While field inventories provide high localized accuracy, they are spatially sparse; conversely, spaceborne LiDAR from the Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) offers broad biomass samples but lacks spatial continuity and systematic underestimation of high-biomass forests. This paper presents an operational framework centered on a single globally trained convolutional neural network (CNN) that is seamlessly adapted to each new landscape through a lightweight empirical field-calibration workflow. The global model combines optical (Sentinel-2), C-band SAR (Sentinel-1), L-band SAR (ALOS-2 PALSAR-2), and terrain (DEM) data. It is trained once against GEDI Level-4A biomass reference data spanning multiple regions and both wet and dry seasons so that it learns the persistent woody-structure rather than a single-date appearance. To avoid retraining for every landscape, the framework applies a small number of local field plots to fit a scale-and-bias correction that aligns the global prediction with ground truth in each region. The pipeline harmonizes sensor data onto a shared 10 m grid, derives vegetation indices and polarimetric ratios, computes per-band normalization stats, and trains the CNN with a hybrid log-domain SmoothL1 with RMSE loss for skewed biomass distribution. On held-out validation the global GEDI-based model achieved R^2 approximately 0.78 and RMSE approximately 22 Mg/ha. A subsequent field calibration combining Random Forest fine-tuning under a 10-fold cross-validation eliminates localized regional biases. This improves local validation performance to R^2 approximately 0.82 and reduces RMSE to approximately 15 Mg/ha, outperforming both the uncalibrated global model and the ESA CCI Biomass product against field plots.

</details>


### [124] [Towards a Formal Definition of Agent Memory: Basis, Span, Optimality, and the Sequential Memory Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11654)
*Hongyao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于大模型智能体记忆的统一形式化框架：记忆是基（basis），知识是其张成空间（span），可回答性是一个覆盖问题。最优记忆是容量约束下期望覆盖的最大化，其价值形成效用-容量前沿。文章进一步考虑了噪声下的覆盖与精度权衡，并将持续智能体记忆问题形式化为多层级顺序MDP，最后在《奥德赛》上进行了实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管记忆在大模型智能体中被广泛部署，但缺乏对"记忆是什么"以及"何时最优"的统一形式化描述。这种缺失使得不同记忆系统之间难以比较，开放问题也难以被明确表述。

Method: 1) 将记忆定义为基、知识定义为张成空间、可回答性定义为覆盖问题；2) 通过生成算子将事件集转化为蕴含的知识；3) 将最优记忆定义为容量约束下期望覆盖的最大化，并构建效用-容量前沿；4) 引入噪声讨论覆盖与精度的权衡；5) 将持续记忆问题形式化为多层级顺序MDP（记忆为状态、写作为动作、查询时的效用为延迟奖励）；6) 在《奥德赛》上进行实例化。

Result: 该框架将前沿、压缩区和覆盖与精度的分歧在《奥德赛》实例上转化为具体数值，并成功将现有记忆系统纳入该框架，使"记忆有多好"可量化度量。

Conclusion: 本文为智能体记忆提供了统一的形式化基础，使记忆系统之间可以基于效用-容量前沿进行公平比较，并将构建和学习智能体记忆的开放问题重新表述为具体的研究问题。

Abstract: Despite the wide deployment of memory in large-model agents, there is no unified formal account of what a memory is or when it is optimal. This paper takes a first step toward this account. The central idea is that memory is a basis, knowledge is its span, and answerability is a coverage problem: an agent stores events extracted from a material; a generation operator turns any event set into the knowledge it entails; and a query is answerable exactly when some single item in the span covers it. The optimal memory is then the capacity-constrained maximizer of expected coverage, and its value traces a utility--capacity frontier, the common yardstick on which memory systems can be compared. Next, we consider noise in the memory and discuss coverage versus precision under it: a memory may store false claims, so the write policy must infer the truth of what it stores. Drawing an analogy with biological memory, which is formed continuously through ongoing experience, we formalize the continual agent-memory problem in a sequential MDP that covers multiple levels, where memory is the state, writing is the action, and the utility settled at query time is the delayed reward that drives learning. To make the framework concrete, we instantiate it on Homer's \emph{Odyssey}, turning the frontier, the compression zone, and the divergence of coverage from precision into concrete numbers. Finally, we position existing systems within the framework, making ``how good is a memory'' measurable and recasting the open problems of constructing and learning agent memory as concrete research questions.

</details>


### [125] [Continuous-Latent Predictive Modeling with Semantic Alignment for EEG-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11656)
*Myeong-Ju Cho,Hye-Bin Shin,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM)，一种EEG-语言基础模型，通过连续语义嵌入预测来统一多种EEG解码任务，利用CELP编码器学习可迁移表示，并通过MQSD模块将连续EEG表示与文本语义对齐。实验表明该方法在多个基准上具有一致的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的预训练范式存在关键缺陷：掩码自编码倾向于优先重建低层信号而忽略任务相关语义；自回归建模则在连续神经动力学与离散token空间之间存在不匹配。因此需要新的策略来有效对齐连续EEG表示与自然语言语义，并实现与大语言模型的集成。

Method: 提出BLPM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP)编码器，通过潜在目标预测学习可迁移表示；(2) Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD)模块，提取任务相关信息并在共享潜在空间中将连续EEG表示与文本语义按语义关系对齐。整体将异构EEG解码任务重构为连续语义嵌入预测问题。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，BLPM在不同任务上展现出一致的泛化性能，验证了连续潜在语义预测作为EEG-语言基础模型有效范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 连续潜在语义预测被确立为EEG-语言基础模型的有效范式，能够克服传统预训练方法在语义对齐和模态集成方面的局限性，实现跨任务、跨环境的通用神经解码。

Abstract: Recent advances in EEG foundation models have demonstrated the potential of large-scale pretraining to enable generalizable neural decoding across subjects, recording environments, and datasets. However, dominant pretraining paradigms face key challenges: masked autoencoding tends to prioritize low-level signal reconstruction over task-relevant semantics, while autoregressive modeling creates a mismatch between continuous neural dynamics and discrete token spaces. To address these challenges, new strategies are needed to effectively align continuous EEG representations with natural-language semantics and enable their integration with large language models. Accordingly, we propose Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM), an EEG-language foundation model that reformulates heterogeneous EEG decoding tasks as a continuous semantic embedding prediction problem. BLPM introduces a Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP) encoder that learns transferable representations through latent target prediction. Building on these representations, a Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD) module extracts task-relevant information and aligns continuous EEG representations with textual semantics within a shared latent space according to their semantic relationships. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent generalization performance across diverse tasks, establishing continuous latent semantic prediction as an effective paradigm for EEG-language foundation models.

</details>


### [126] [Is Per-Agent Policy Composition Safe? Rethinking Successor-Feature Transfer in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11658)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体强化学习中的动态目标迁移问题。作者证明独立组合每个智能体的策略库可能产生比库中所有策略都更差的行为，唯一无条件安全的固定规则是同步组合（但无法服务不同智能体不同目标）。为此提出MA-USFA分层方法：下层用通用后继特征逼近器预测各智能体后继特征（条件于队友目标），上层组合器跨智能体选择库条目并提供交叉智能体修正。训练一次即可部署，无需逐任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多智能体强化学习系统（如车队管理、交通信号控制）需要在部署后动态改变目标，而针对每个新目标重训策略代价过高。单智能体场景已有成熟方案（后继特征+广义策略提升），但多智能体迁移研究不足。常见做法是让各智能体独立重组自己的策略库，这继承了配方却无法保证性能，可能导致整体行为比库中任何策略都差。

Method: MA-USFA（多智能体通用后继特征逼近器）采用两层架构：下层由通用后继特征逼近器组成，预测每个智能体的后继特征时以其队友目标为条件；上层组合器跨智能体选择每个智能体应遵循的库条目，并提供单个智能体价值函数无法表示的交叉智能体修正。训练在目标分布上一次完成，部署时无需逐任务适应。

Result: 理论证明独立组合可产生严格劣于库中所有策略的联合行为（因重组队友改变各智能体所处环境并使价值失效），这是单智能体场景不存在的失败模式。证明同步组合是唯一无条件安全的固定规则，但其无法服务不同智能体不同目标。MA-USFA兼顾安全性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 多智能体动态目标迁移不能简单将单智能体方法独立推广。MA-USFA通过分层设计（条件化后继特征预测+跨智能体组合与修正）同时实现了安全性保证和逐智能体目标灵活性，为多智能体零样本目标适应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many reinforcement learning systems, from fleet management to traffic signal control, must serve an objective that changes dynamically after deployment, and retraining a policy for each new objective is prohibitively expensive. For a single agent, this problem is well understood: successor features with generalized policy improvement, together with their universal extension, recombine a library of learned policies into a policy for any new objective, with a guarantee that the result is never worse than any policy in the library. However, multi-agent transfer has received far less attention, and the common practice of letting each agent recombine its own library independently inherits the recipe but not the guarantee. We prove that this independent composition can produce joint behavior strictly worse than every policy in the library, because recombining teammates changes the environment each agent faces and invalidates the values it relies on, a failure with no single-agent counterpart. We further show that the only unconditionally safe fixed rule is synchronized composition, which moves the whole team to one jointly trained policy but cannot serve objectives that assign different goals to different agents. To attain safety and flexibility at once, we propose MA-USFA, a hierarchical method with two layers: a lower layer of universal successor feature approximators that predicts each agent's successor features while conditioned on its teammates' objectives, and an upper composer that selects, across agents, which library entry each agent should follow and supplies the cross-agent correction a per-agent value cannot represent. Trained once over the distribution of objectives, it is applied at deployment with no per-task adaptation.

</details>


### [127] [Low-Interaction-Rank Learning: Unifying Multiplicative Dual-Encoder Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11661)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"低交互秩函数"这一统一理论框架，用以指导乘法双编码器网络（multiplicative dual-encoder network）的设计决策（如交互模式数量、编码器归一化方式、何时应避免该架构），并揭示了该架构的规范对称性（gauge symmetry）问题，证明归一化是规范固定、白化可固定交互模式至置换与符号，从而解释了对比学习维度不可解释性的根源并提供了建设性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法双编码器网络在算子学习、二分匹配、对比视觉-语言模型、检索等多个领域被独立发展，但缺乏统一理论来指导基本设计决策：应表示多少交互模式、如何归一化编码器、何时应避免该架构。此外，该架构存在编码器仅定义到线性规范对称性的可辨识性问题，导致学习到的坐标任意且不可解释。

Method: 作者引入"低交互秩函数"类，以内积形式刻画该架构可表示的函数族，并用交互谱（interaction spectrum）度量其内在复杂度。在此框架下：(1) 近似误差分解为谱截断项与编码器实现项；(2) 样本复杂度由两个编码器复杂度之和而非乘积决定；(3) 基于谱衰减的可判据性标准决定架构是否可用。作者还从规范理论视角分析可辨识性：归一化即规范固定，白化（whitening）可将交互模式固定至置换和符号。

Result: 在合成核、算子学习和CLIP模型上的实验验证了理论预测：谱衰减率与预测的标度一致；白化能恢复真实交互模式；独立训练的CLIP模型之间存在一个单一旋转关系，经白化去除该旋转后，可暴露可解释的概念轴。

Conclusion: 本文为乘法双编码器网络提供了统一的理论基础，覆盖了架构能力刻画、误差与样本复杂度分析、可判据性判断以及可辨识性修复，并通过白化解决了对比学习中维度不可解释的问题。代码已开源。

Abstract: A multiplicative dual-encoder network computes a real-valued output for a pair of inputs as the inner product of their separate encodings. This architecture has been developed independently in operator learning, bipartite matching, contrastive vision-language models, retrieval, and other areas, yet no unified theory guides the basic design decisions: how many interaction modes to represent, how to normalize the encoders, and when the architecture should be avoided. We provide such a foundation by introducing the class of functions of low interaction rank, a class whose intrinsic complexity is measured by its interaction spectrum. Within this framework, approximation error decomposes into a spectral truncation term and an encoder-realization term; sample complexity is governed by the sum of the two encoder complexities rather than their product; and a usability criterion based on spectral decay determines when the architecture can succeed. The same framework exposes a central identifiability problem: the encoders are defined only up to a linear gauge symmetry that leaves the learned coordinates arbitrary. We show that normalization is gauge fixing and that whitening pins the interaction modes up to permutation and sign, thereby explaining the uninterpretability of contrastive dimensions and providing a constructive remedy. Experiments on synthetic kernels, operator learning, and CLIP models validate the theoretical predictions: spectral decay rates match the predicted scaling, whitening recovers the true modes, and independently trained CLIP models are related by a single rotation which, after removal by whitening, exposes interpretable concept axes. The code of this paper is provided at https://github.com/RS2002/Mul-Net .

</details>


### [128] [Rubric Dropout: A Simple Way to Mitigate Reward Hacking in Rubric-as-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11669)
*Minglai Yang,Xinyu Guo,Utkarsh Tyagi,Mian Zhang,Razvan Dumitru,Sunjie Hou,Yunzhong He,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLHF中基于评分标准(rubric)的强化学习存在的奖励欺骗(reward hacking)问题，提出了一种简单有效的方法——Rubric Dropout。该方法在每步训练中随机丢弃部分评分标准，防止策略过度优化固定代理指标。实验表明在医学和科学基准上，该方法能显著提高OOD gold judge得分，同时不牺牲域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM judge评分的rubric是质量的不完美代理，策略训练过度会利用rubric与实际质量之间的差异进行奖励欺骗。现有文献缺少对此问题的直接量化测量，也缺少有效的缓解方法。

Method: 1) 直接测量奖励欺骗：用GRPO训练Qwen3-8B，对比训练judge与更强gold judge的评分分歧。2) 提出Rubric Dropout：每步随机丢弃部分criteria再计算奖励，丢弃子集在rollout组内共享以保持GRPO的组间相对优势可比性，评估时始终使用完整rubric。

Result: 训练judge分数持续上升而gold judge分数先升后降（HealthBench-Hard下降3分，ResearchQA下降22分），证实奖励欺骗。Rubric Dropout在30%和50%丢弃率下，OOD gold分数每个匹配检查点均提升（+1到+2分在HealthBench-Hard，+6到+7分在ResearchQA），降低两种hacking指标，域内性能无损。30-50%为最优区间，而reweighting方法反而比不干预更差。

Conclusion: Rubric Dropout是一个零成本、一行代码的修复方案，有效缓解基于rubric强化学习中的奖励欺骗问题，通过防止策略重复优化同一rubric来保持OOD泛化能力。这一发现为RLHF后训练中代理指标的使用提供了一个实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning against rubrics, lists of criteria graded by an LLM judge, has become a standard way to post-train language models on tasks with no deterministic answer. The rubric, however, is a fixed proxy for quality, never a complete description of it, and a policy trained against it long enough will learn to exploit the difference. We measure this directly. Training Qwen3-8B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on medical and science rubrics and grading out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks with both the training judge and a stronger gold judge, we find that the two scores diverge during training. The training judge's score keeps climbing while the gold judge's score peaks and then falls, by 3 points on HealthBench-Hard and by 22 points on ResearchQA. A judge with a fixed bias would shift the gold curve by a constant, not send it down while the training score rises, so the divergence is reward hacking, not judge noise. We propose Rubric Dropout, a one-line fix borrowed from neuron dropout. At every step, we randomly drop a subset of the rubric's criteria before computing the reward, so the policy never optimizes the same rubric twice. The dropped subset is shared across each rollout group, so GRPO's group-relative advantages stay comparable, and evaluation always uses the full rubric.
