By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个联邦学习框架，在函数空间（而非参数空间）进行客户端更新选择。它利用NTK（神经正切核）一致性评分来刻画预测行为，并通过P2P拓扑让每个客户端在私有验证数据上评估其他客户端的更新，从而为每个客户端确定个性化的聚合集。在五个数据集和九个基线方法上始终优于集中式FL基线和现有P2P方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的核心挑战是如何判断哪些客户端更新对每个客户端的特定目标域是有益的。现有方法通常在参数空间比较模型参数或梯度来解决问题，但参数空间相似性在异构、非IID数据下难以很好地反映预测行为，导致与目标域不一致的更新（由异构数据或故障客户端引起）会降低本地模型性能。

Method: LIGHTYEAR采用函数空间进行更新选择：1) 使用基于NTK的一致性评分来刻画预测行为，相比参数空间相似性提供更具表达力的选择标准；2) 采用P2P拓扑，客户端直接交换更新并在私有验证数据上评估传入模型；3) 每个客户端仅为自身目标域选择有益的更新，并使用正则化聚合规则提高异构环境下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集和九个基线方法的实验中，LIGHTYEAR始终优于集中式联邦学习基线和现有的P2P方法。

Conclusion: 通过将更新选择从参数空间迁移到函数空间，并利用NTK刻画预测行为，LIGHTYEAR有效解决了异构环境下联邦学习更新选择的问题，为个性化联邦聚合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [164] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用对称投影吸引子重建（SPAR）方法将光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力计脉冲波时间序列转化为图像，使用卷积神经网络将健康受试者分类为两个年龄相近的组别（35-40岁和50-55岁），在内部和外部测试集上均达到70%以上的F1分数，证明SPAR图像包含可区分健康成年人年龄的形态学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形态会随年龄演变，反映心血管系统的结构和功能变化，因此血管年龄是心血管健康的有价值替代标志物。早期血管老化可预示疾病风险增加，对无症状成年人进行脉冲波分析有助于风险分层，因此需要一种能从脉冲波信号中提取判别性特征的方法来支持早期风险检测。

Method: 首先使用SPAR方法将PPG和动脉张力计采集的脉冲波时间序列数据转换为图像；然后利用这些SPAR图像训练卷积神经网络（CNN）模型；模型任务是将健康受试者分类为两个年龄相近的组别（35-40岁和50-55岁）；最后在内部和外部测试集上评估模型性能。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均表现出一致的分类性能，PPG和动脉张力计信号均获得了70%以上的F1分数。

Conclusion: SPAR衍生的脉冲波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成年人中也包含可判别的形态学特征，该概念验证研究为未来利用SPAR结合智能可穿戴设备进行早期风险检测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [165] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYDRA（HYperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture），一种参数高效的KAN双曲扩展方法。HYDRA将输入映射到Poincaré球的有界双曲潜空间，在切空间中进行KAN风格更新，并通过低秩原型块在隐藏维度间共享函数变换，从而减少参数冗余。实验表明HYDRA在八个基准数据集上取得竞争性或更优的性能，同时提升参数效率和表示可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN虽然通过用可学习的单变量函数替代标量权重来增强非线性逼近能力，但每个连接分配独立函数导致大量参数冗余，限制了其可扩展性和效率。

Method: HYDRA将向量值输入映射到Poincaré球的有界双曲潜空间，在切空间中执行KAN风格更新，并采用低秩原型块在不同隐藏维度之间共享函数变换。双曲表示提供结构化的径向坐标用于解释，半径控制通过防止边界饱和来提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HYDRA持续获得具有竞争力或更优的预测性能，同时改善了参数效率和表示可解释性。

Conclusion: HYDRA通过双曲几何与低秩共享机制有效解决了KAN的参数冗余问题，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升参数效率和表示可解释性，为KAN架构的扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [166] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScreenShot是一个层级Transformer模型，预训练于40个药物筛选数据集（覆盖3700种药物和6000个生物样本），通过上下文学习（in-context learning）直接基于功能测量预测新患者对联合用药的反应，无需微调或分子谱分析。在四个留出数据集上优于所有基线，且其内部表征支持主动学习策略，以三分之一的预算实现与全量筛选相同的命中检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合用药可降低耐药风险，但有效组合的搜索空间巨大，传统组合筛选成本高、耗时且技术上常常不可行。现有预测模型通常需要分子谱分析和逐个队列训练，在时间与组织样本稀缺的场景下应用受限，亟需一种无需分子谱分析、无需微调的预测方法。

Method: ScreenShot采用层级Transformer架构，其设计刻意镜像筛选数据的嵌套结构（药物×样本）。模型在大规模多数据集上预训练，通过上下文学习的方式，仅提供新患者少量观测样本作为few-shot上下文，即可直接预测该样本对联合疗法的反应，无需微调也无需分子谱分析。

Result: 在四个留出数据集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗方案识别两方面均优于所有基线方法。此外，其内部表征可用于驱动加权k-means++主动学习策略来选择实验，以三分之一的预算即可达到与全量筛选相同的命中检测效果。

Conclusion: ScreenShot展示了一种无需分子谱和微调的可泛化药物组合响应预测方法，其内部表征对实验设计具有直接实用价值，可显著降低组合筛选的成本，并提供了开源代码和交互式仪表板。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>


### [167] [An Efficient Near-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial $m$-Set Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12231)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性组合赌博机问题，其中学习者在每轮从d个物品中选择m个，并仅观察到所选物品的总损失。作者提出了一种计算高效且多项式时间的算法，在不需要枚举指数级动作集的情况下，实现了与有限动作EXP3-KW算法相同的高概率遗憾界O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性组合赌博机问题中，m-set动作集的规模K=binom(d,m)可能呈指数级增长，直接应用传统的有限动作算法（如EXP3-KW）需要枚举所有动作，导致空间和时间复杂度爆炸。因此，需要设计一种能够利用问题内在结构（损失由d维物品损失向量决定）的高效算法。

Method: 作者提出了一种计算高效的算法，其关键思想是利用m-set组合动作的线性结构，将每个采样分布仅用d个参数表示。该算法不需要显式枚举动作集，而是通过巧妙地表示和更新采样分布，在多项式时间内运行，同时维持与真实分布等价的采样行为。

Result: 算法保证了以至少1-δ的概率，对自适应非预期对手达到R_T = O(√(dT log(K/δ)))的遗憾界，这与有限动作的EXP3-KW算法的高概率边界相匹配，但实现仅需多项式时间和空间。

Conclusion: 该算法成功解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题，表明对抗性m-set组合赌博机问题可以在不牺牲遗憾性能的前提下实现计算高效性，通过利用问题的组合线性结构避免了对指数级动作集的直接枚举。

Abstract: We study adversarial combinatorial bandits with $m$-set actions, where at each round the learner selects $m$ out of $d$ items and observes only the aggregate loss of the selected items. The resulting action set contains $K=\binom{d}{m}$ elements and can therefore be exponentially large. Nevertheless, the loss of every action is determined by the same $d$-dimensional vector of item losses. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits this structure without explicitly enumerating the action set. Against adaptive non-anticipating adversaries, it guarantees, with probability at least $1-δ$, regret against the best fixed action of \[
  R_T =
  O\left(\sqrt{dT\log(K/δ)}\right). \] This matches the high-probability regret bound of the finite-action EXP3-KW algorithm of Zimmert and Lattimore, whose direct implementation may require exponential space. Our algorithm instead represents each sampling distribution with $d$ parameters and runs in polynomial time without enumerating the action set. Thus, it resolves the open problem posed by Maiti et al.

</details>


### [168] [Earth observation embeddings are effective sub-grid descriptors for probabilistic weather downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12271)
*Pedro Sousa,Will Tebbutt,Sadiq Jaffer,Robin Young,Anil Madhavapeddy,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用地球观测基础模型（TESSERA）的嵌入向量作为子网格地表描述符，增强卷积条件神经过程用于概率性天气降尺度。在五个气候区，该方法将2米温度和10米风速的CRPS技能分别提升11.5%和6.2%，且在更换粗网格输入或预测新站点时改进依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气再分析产品和预报在粗网格上描述大气状态，但站点级应用需要任意位置的预测，而近地表条件受地形和地表属性的未解析细节影响。现有概率性降尺度方法依赖手工设计的 topographic 描述符，本文质疑能否用地球观测基础模型提供可迁移的子网格地表表示。

Method: 在卷积条件神经过程（convolutional conditional neural process）中引入可学习的局部地表描述符：将10米分辨率的TESSERA嵌入块压缩后与粗网格ERA5再分析场（~25km）结合，共同作为降尺度模型的输入。该地表描述符编码站点的持久性地表属性，捕捉其相对于粗网格大气状态的偏差。

Result: 在五个气候多样区域、时空双重留出测试中：整体CRPS技能对2米温度提升11.5%，对10米风速提升6.2%。分析表明地形对温度的亚网格结构解释更多，而TESSERA为风速提供额外地表信息。改进在输入换为Aurora AI预报模型以及预测无区域历史的新部署站点时仍然保持。

Conclusion: 这是首次证明长时间尺度的地球观测嵌入可支持短时间尺度的天气降尺度任务，因为子网格偏差系统地由持久性地表属性所结构化。该方法为站点级天气预测提供了可迁移、免手工特征的地表表征方案。

Abstract: Global weather reanalyses and forecasts resolve the evolving atmospheric state on coarse grids, but site-specific applications require predictions at arbitrary locations where near-surface conditions also depend on unresolved terrain and land-surface properties. Existing probabilistic downscalers address this gap using hand-crafted topographic descriptors. We ask instead whether Earth observation foundation models can provide transferable sub-grid surface representations for probabilistic weather downscaling.
  We augment a convolutional conditional neural process that downscales coarse ERA5 reanalysis fields at ~25 km resolution with a learned local surface descriptor, obtained by compressing a patch of TESSERA embeddings at 10 m resolution. Although these embeddings summarise surface conditions over annual timescales, they improve downscaling of instantaneous 2 m temperature and 10 m wind speed by encoding persistent surface properties that capture a location's departure from the coarse-grid atmospheric state. Across five climatically diverse regions, the embedding improves point and probabilistic skill at stations held out in both space and time, overall improving CRPS skill by 11.5% for 2 m temperature and 6.2% for 10 m wind speed. We further analyse how its contribution differs by variable, finding that topography explains more of temperature's sub-grid structure, while TESSERA provides additional surface information for wind speed.
  These improvements persist when the coarse input is changed from ERA5 to forecasts from the Aurora AI forecasting model, and when predicting at newly deployed stations with no regional history. To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [169] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化流程，使非专家用户能够为给定任务实例化和迭代人类对齐的奖励函数。该流程通过三个步骤将自然语言描述的任务转化为线性奖励函数：将任务目标分解为基本目标并推导可测量的结果变量、通过最小成本部分覆盖在因果DAG上选择奖励项、通过偏好询问以凸可行性问题的几何框架拟合权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励函数设计通常需要专家知识和大量人工调整，非专家用户难以设计出符合人类偏好顺序的奖励函数。本文旨在提供一种系统化、可操作的形式化流程，使非专业人士也能为强化学习任务设计人类对齐的奖励函数。

Method: 该流程包含三个核心步骤：(1) 设计引导式工作流，从自然语言任务描述中推导出可测量的结果变量；(2) 将奖励项选择问题约简为因果DAG上的最小成本部分覆盖问题，通过最大流算法在多项式时间内求解；(3) 将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题，通过分离预言机方法迭代缩小可行权重区域，使用O(n log κ)次偏好查询达到期望容差。

Result: 该方法据作者所知是首个能够维持确定性无冲突可行权重区域的奖励设计方法，通过分离预言机将可行区域缩小到期望容差，仅需O(n log κ)次偏好查询。三步骤均具有形式化保证，且后两步具有多项式时间复杂性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可访问的、形式化保证的奖励函数设计流程，使得非专家也能够构建符合人类偏好的奖励函数，并具有计算效率和理论保证。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [170] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCI（基于重分配的代价推断）框架，将机器人/驾驶场景中仅有的轨迹级"停止反馈"（稀疏二进制信号）转化为稠密的逐步骤代价标签，并基于此训练安全的离线强化学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全离线强化学习通常假设可以获得稠密的逐步骤代价标注，但实际场景中监督者通常只能提供轨迹级别的停止反馈（即仅在被判定为不安全的第一个时间步给出二元信号），缺乏逐步骤的归因信息。这种稀疏反馈无法直接用于现有的安全离线RL算法。

Method: 将问题建模为时间信用分配问题，提出RCI框架：通过回报分解（return decomposition）将稀疏的停止反馈转化为稠密的逐步骤代价，构建增强数据集，然后在此数据集上训练带约束的离线策略。理论部分证明了回报等价的重分配（return-equivalent redistribution）保持可行策略集和最优拉格朗日乘子不变，从而保证变换无损；实践上则改善了代价批评器学习的条件。通过在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作任务上的实验验证。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作实验中，RCI相比稀疏基线（直接使用稀疏反馈）和基于分类器的基线，实现了显著更低的违规率，并且对异构数据集组成和标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RCI框架首次从理论上证明了稀疏停止反馈到稠密代价的转换在保持策略可行集和最优Lagrangian不变的意义上是无损的，同时在实际中带来了更好的代价批评器学习条件和更低的违规率，为实用场景下的安全离线RL提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Time-Optimal Control of One-Mode Flexible Structures: Analytical Solution and the Cost of Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11360)
*Manuel Keppler*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了双积分器耦合谐振子的时间最优控制解析解，推导出闭式时间-位移定律，并发现毕达哥拉斯恒等式 T² = T_r² + 2T_s²，将机动时间分解为刚体最小时间与柔性代价（同步时间）。该模型涵盖两质量弹簧系统、单弯曲模态柔性结构和线性化桥式起重机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双积分器的时间最优解是基础性成果，其时间-位移定律 T_r = 2√L 是数值方法无法给出的结构。对于耦合了谐振子的更复杂系统（柔性结构、起重机等），缺乏解析解，需要首次推导出来以理解柔性的根本代价。

Method: 推导双积分器耦合谐振子的时间最优控制解析解，将综合问题简化为单个标量求逆。通过毕达哥拉斯恒等式分解机动时间为刚体最小时间与同步时间，并推导闭式灵敏度公式分析刚度对机动时间的影响。

Result: 得到闭式时间-位移定律和尖锐包络 2√L ≤ T ≤ 2√(L + π²/2)。小机动时 T ∝ L^{1/4}（一个振荡器模态等价于两个额外积分器），大机动时柔性渐近自由。机动时间关于刚度非单调：软化已有柔性结构可以缩短时间，但给刚体添加柔性绝不会缩短。自然运动处一阶刚度灵敏度为零，是鲁棒设计目标。

Conclusion: 柔性的代价是尺度相关的：小机动下代价显著，大机动下渐近消失。添加柔性到刚体永远不能缩短机动，但软化已有柔性结构可能更快，"越硬越快"并非普遍成立。自然运动因刚度一阶灵敏度为零而成为鲁棒设计目标。

Abstract: The time-optimal solution for a double integrator is a foundational result: its time-displacement law $T_r = 2\sqrt{L}$ for a normalized step $L$ is structure that numerical methods cannot provide. We solve the time-optimal control for rest-to-rest maneuvers of a double integrator coupled to a harmonic oscillator, deriving the first analytical solution and closed-form time-displacement law. Synthesis reduces to a single scalar inversion. This is the model to which the two-mass-spring system, the one-bending-mode flexible structure, and the linearized overhead crane reduce. A Pythagorean identity $T^2 = T_r^2 + 2T_s^2$ decomposes the optimal maneuver time into the rigid-body minimum and a synchronization time $T_s \leq π$, the cost of flexibility, giving the sharp envelope $2\sqrt{L} \leq T \leq 2\sqrt{L + π^2/2}$. The penalty is scale-dependent. For small maneuvers $T \propto L^{1/4}$: one oscillator mode costs as much as two additional integrators. For large ones, flexibility is asymptotically free. It vanishes at the natural motions, where the oscillator completes whole cycles. A closed-form sensitivity formula proves the maneuver time non-monotone in stiffness. Adding flexibility to a rigid body never shortens a maneuver, yet softening an already flexible structure can: stiffer is not always faster. The natural motions are robust design targets since first-order sensitivity to stiffness vanishes there.

</details>


### [172] [Forward Trajectory Steering for Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11480)
*Sungje Park,Stephen Tu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: S2R是一种基于PINN的Hamilton-Jacobi可达性求解器，通过轻量级的自适应配点采样分布（利用当前值函数诱导的最优控制和扰动信号前向演轨迹并注入随机探索噪声）来改进训练，无需多阶段训练或MPC监督即可在安全指标和L2误差上达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HJ可达性分析为动态系统安全控制提供严谨的数学框架，但其实际应用受限于高维HJ-Isaacs变分不等式PDE的求解复杂度。虽然PINN已成为传统网格求解器的有前景替代方案，但其性能对配点采样方式高度敏感，现有PINN-based HJ可达性求解器不得不依赖复杂的训练流程和辅助监督才能学习精确的安全值函数。

Method: 提出STEER2REACH (S2R)，一个只需对标准PINN训练进行最小改动的求解器。核心创新是构造轻量级、低开销的自适应配点采样分布：利用当前值函数诱导的最优控制和扰动信号前向演轨迹，并注入随机探索噪声来引导采样点分布。

Result: 尽管方法简单，S2R在安全指标上达到竞争性甚至更优的性能，并在多个可达性基准测试中相比SoTA MPC引导的HJ可达性求解器降低了相对L2误差，且无需多阶段训练或MPC监督。

Conclusion: S2R证明了通过精心设计的自适应采样分布，简单的PINN训练框架即可在高维HJ可达性问题中取得优异的安全性能，避免了现有方法对复杂训练流程和外部监督的依赖。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides a mathematically rigorous framework for safe control of dynamical systems, but its practical application is bottlenecked by the computational complexity of solving Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs variational inequality PDEs in high dimensions. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to classical mesh-based solvers, yet their performance is highly sensitive to the choice of collocation sampling. In order to learn accurate safety value functions, existing PINNs-based HJ reachability solvers must rely on complex training pipelines and auxiliary supervision. In this work, we propose STEER2REACH (S2R), a PINNs-based HJ reachability solver that requires minimal modification on top of standard PINNs training. S2R's key contribution is a lightweight, low-overhead adaptive collocation sampling distribution constructed by steering forward trajectories using a combination of the optimal control and disturbance signals induced by the current value function, with injected stochastic exploration noise. We demonstrate that despite its simplicity, S2R achieves competitive--and in some cases improved--performance on safety metrics while reducing relative L2 error across a range of reachability benchmarks compared with SoTA MPC-guided HJ reachability solvers, all without requiring multi-stage training or MPC-based supervision.

</details>


### [173] [Matched Disturbance Rejection for Port-Hamiltonian Systems with Coupled Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11490)
*M. Reza J. Harandi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究在已稳定的端口-哈密顿（PH）系统中，如何抑制由耦合PH动力学产生的匹配扰动。通过将扰动动力学纳入统一的PH表示，并允许扰动通过匹配输入通道和互连结构同时影响被控对象，提出了基线扰动抑制方案及基于在线参数估计的扩展方案，保证系统状态渐近收敛到期望平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扰动抑制方法对扰动动力学施加了严格的结构限制（如要求特殊的互连或阻尼结构），难以处理更一般的耦合PH扰动，包括具有非零互连和阻尼项的扰动。因此需要提出一个更通用的框架来抑制这类扰动。

Method: 1) 将扰动动力学与已稳定的PH系统耦合成统一的PH表示；2) 针对已知扰动存储参数的情形，建立基线扰动抑制方案；3) 针对未知对称存储矩阵，通过在线参数估计扩展框架；4) 提出两种控制设计，分别在不同结构条件下适用，第二种设计放宽了第一种的维数限制。

Result: 所提出的方法保证了被控对象状态渐近收敛到期望平衡点，且在相应条件下保持了闭环动力学的PH表示结构。

Conclusion: 该框架推广了现有的扰动抑制方法，扩展了其对耦合PH扰动模型的适用性，能处理更一般类别的耦合PH扰动。

Abstract: This paper investigates the rejection of matched disturbances generated by coupled port-Hamiltonian (PH) dynamics in previously stabilized PH systems. The disturbance dynamics are incorporated into a unified PH representation, allowing the disturbance to affect the plant through both the matched input channel and an interconnection structure. Unlike existing results that typically impose restrictive structures on the disturbance dynamics, the proposed framework accommodates a more general class of coupled PH disturbances, including nonzero interconnection and damping terms. A baseline disturbance rejection scheme is first established for known disturbance storage parameters. The framework is then extended to the case of an unknown symmetric storage matrix through online parameter estimation. Two control designs are developed under different structural conditions, with the latter relaxing the dimensional restriction imposed by the first design. The proposed methods guarantee asymptotic convergence of the plant state to the desired equilibrium, while preserving a port-Hamiltonian representation of the closed-loop dynamics under the corresponding conditions. The results generalize existing disturbance rejection approaches and broaden their applicability to coupled PH disturbance models.

</details>


### [174] [A Conceptual Framework for Enhancing Workforce Readiness for Smart Manufacturing in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11540)
*Dalton Ross Smith,Wilburn Whittington,Alejandro Martinez,Aidan Duncan,Gang Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出劳动力就绪水平框架，将技术就绪水平量表改编为九个递进能力阶段和四支柱评分标准(数字与AI素养、网络物理系统流畅度、人机协作、数据驱动决策)，通过复合阶段评分和队列级劳动力就绪指数聚合，并在大学智能制造教学实验室的89个顶点项目中进行实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI、工业物联网、网络物理系统和先进机器人技术的融合使制造业变革速度快于工程课程更新速度，导致车间所需能力与传统工程教育所提供能力之间的差距不断扩大，缺乏统一的评估和诊断工具。

Method: 提出WRL框架，改编TRL量表为九个递进能力阶段，建立四支柱评分标准，采用"无薄弱支柱"规则通过复合阶段评分和队列级劳动力就绪指数进行聚合，并在大学智能制造教学实验室中对89个顶点项目(深入分析其中4个)进行实例化应用。

Result: 劳动力就绪指数在5.2至6.4之间；"无薄弱支柱"规则在四例中三例具有诊断信息价值，一例成为约束性认证条件；框架揭示网络安全和数据处理决策缺口隐藏在强大的分析画像背后；最高阶段的前进受行业嵌入经验限制而非额外课程。

Conclusion: WRL为教育工作者、认证机构和区域劳动力系统提供了一种基于证据的共同诊断和推进工具；未来工作将校准支柱权重并测试信度和预测效度。

Abstract: The convergence of artificial intelligence (AI), Industrial Internet of Things, cyber-physical systems, and advanced robotics is reshaping manufacturing faster than engineering curricula can adapt, widening the gap between the competencies required on the shop floor and those delivered by traditional engineering and technology education. This paper proposes a Workforce Readiness Level (WRL) framework, which adapts the Technology Readiness Level scale into nine progressive competency stages and a four-pillar rubric, digital and AI literacy, cyber-physical systems fluency, human-machine collaboration, and data-driven decision making, aggregated through a composite stage score and a cohort-level workforce-readiness index under a ``no-thin-pillar'' rule. The framework is instantiated at a university smart-manufacturing teaching laboratory and draws on 89 sponsored capstone projects delivered over four semesters, four of which are analyzed in depth. Four pillars jointly span the relevant ABET student outcomes. Across the highlighted cohorts the workforce-readiness index ranged from 5.2 to 6.4, and the no-thin-pillar rule was diagnostically informative in three of the four cases and the binding certification constraint in one, repeatedly surfacing cyber-physical and data-driven-decision gaps concealed behind strong analytics profiles; advancement to the highest stages was gated by industry-embedded experience rather than additional coursework. WRL offers educators, accreditation bodies, and regional workforce systems a common, evidence-based instrument for diagnosing and advancing workforce readiness; future work will calibrate pillar weights and test reliability and predictive validity.

</details>


### [175] [Poisson Tangent Limits and Critical Policy Switching for Sampled Bellman Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11549)
*Ming-Zhe Dai,Chengxi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续动作空间中的折扣马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中控制器从N个随机候选动作中选择最优动作。当最优动作集在采样分布下测度为零时，随机候选模型的值随N增大收敛到最优值，但收敛速率和渐近选择规则由最优集的几何形状和近优候选的转移核共同决定。文章建立了精确的一阶理论：重标度的近优候选收敛到标记泊松点过程，值间隙的首项是非线性切向Bellman算子的不动点，渐近选择规则是动态的，可通过Mecke积分公式显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续动作空间的MDP中，当最优动作无法被采样分布直接覆盖时（最优动作集测度为零），随机候选模型仅能以正的收敛速率逼近最优值。理解该收敛速率和渐近行为对于实际应用中基于随机搜索的动作选择策略至关重要，但现有的理论框架无法同时刻画收敛速率和渐近选择规则，特别是当多个最优动作竞争时存在复杂的几何与动力学交互。

Method: 本文通过以下步骤建立一阶理论：（1）将重标度的近优候选动作建模为收敛到标记泊松点过程；（2）证明值间隙的领先项是某个非线性切向Bellman算子的不动点，这是经典预解算子的随机推广，用于刻画多个最优动作竞争的情形；（3）通过Mecke积分公式在极限泊松层面显式给出渐近选择规则；（4）分析临界速率下的扰动切换层（switching layer），并研究唯一最优异质情形下临界类通过限策略的折扣可达性以最慢全局尺度传播的机制。

Result: 数值实验验证了所预测的收敛速率、常数和选择概率。理论结果揭示了渐近选择规则本质上是动态的，由转移核通过不动点方程决定，而非简单的静态优化。此外，临界扰动会产生连续的插值切换层，连接不同分支之间的值间隙和选择行为。

Conclusion: 本文为随机候选MDP模型提供了完整的精确一阶理论，阐明了最优集几何、转移核动力学与渐近选择规则之间的深层联系，为连续动作空间中的随机优化策略提供了理论基础和可验证的定量预测。

Abstract: Consider a discounted Markov decision process with continuous action space in which, at each state visit, the controller draws a random pool of $N$ candidate actions and selects among them. When the optimal action set has zero mass under the sampling distribution, the value of this random-candidate model converges to the optimal value as $N$ grows, but the rate of convergence and the asymptotic selection rule are governed jointly by the geometry of the optimal set and by the transition kernels of the near-optimal candidates. This paper develops an exact first-order theory of both. The rescaled near-optimal candidates converge to a marked Poisson point process, and the leading term of the value gap is the fixed point of a nonlinear tangential Bellman operator, a stochastic generalization of the classical resolvent that emerges when several optimal actions compete. The asymptotic selection at exact ties is genuinely dynamic, driven by the transition kernels through the fixed point, and admits an explicit Mecke integral formula at the limiting Poisson level; perturbing the tie at the critical rate yields a switching layer in which the value gap and the selection interpolate continuously between the branch regimes, and in the unique-optimum heterogeneous case the critical class is propagated at the slowest global scale through the discounted reachability of the limit policy. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates, constants, and selection probabilities.

</details>


### [176] [Energy-Aware Wind-Resilient Routing for Truck-Assisted Multi-UAV Delivery under Wind Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11641)
*Tianshun Li,Yanggang Sheng,Hongliang Lu,Zhongzhen Wang,Haoang Li,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出EWR（Energy-Aware Wind-Resilient Routing）框架，一种在线风险敏感规划方法，针对卡车-UAV系统中的风能不确定性和回程可行性问题，通过时间依赖有向能量图和保守不确定性边界实现风感知的能源安全无人机路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机路由方法依赖静态或确定性能源模型，可能低估逆风、侧风、电池电压及回程可行性风险。在卡车-UAV系统中，风力引发推进成本在线变化且仅部分可观测，能源可行性成为低空空地配送的关键安全问题。

Method: EWR将配送环境建模为时间依赖的有向能量图，边缘成本通过延迟噪声风估计、载荷状态和保守不确定性裕度进行更新，实现在线风险敏感的能源感知路径规划。

Result: 在合成配送图（使用公共卡车-UAV配送数据集重放风日志）上的实验表明，EWR提高了任务成功率并减少了风力引发的回程失败。

Conclusion: EWR框架有效解决了风不确定性下的能源可行性问题，通过保守不确定性建模提升了无人机配送系统的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Energy feasibility under wind uncertainty is a critical safety issue for low-altitude air-ground delivery. In truck-UAV systems, UAVs complete assigned deliveries and safely return to a mobile truck or depot, while wind-induced propulsion costs vary online and are only partially observable. Existing routing methods often rely on static or deterministic energy models, which may underestimate headwind, crosswind, battery-voltage, and return-feasibility risks. This paper proposes Energy-Aware Wind-Resilient Routing (EWR), an online risk-sensitive planning framework for wind-aware and energy-safe UAV routing. The delivery environment is represented as a time-dependent directed energy graph whose edge costs are updated using delayed noisy wind estimates, payload states, and conservative uncertainty margins. Experiments using synthetic delivery graphs with replayed wind logs from a public truck-UAV delivery dataset show that EWR improves mission success rates and reduces wind-induced return failures.

</details>


### [177] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Output Feedback LQR for Continuous-Time MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11750)
*Qihua Chen,Mingxiang Liu,Lili Wang,Zhiyun Lin,Minyue Fu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间线性系统的无模型输出反馈线性二次调节（LQR）问题，利用滤波后的输入-输出数据来设计控制器。关键发现是传统滤波向量中存在确定性冗余——SIMO系统仅含2n个独立分量，一般MIMO系统含n(m+1)个独立分量。基于此，作者直接从数据中提取精简滤波向量，开发了精简的无模型输出反馈策略迭代和值迭代算法，消除了冗余方向并减少了未知参数数量，同时保持了纯输入-输出数据驱动的实现方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在输出反馈LQR中，由于系统状态不可观测，现有方法依赖动态滤波器（如延迟滤波）来参数化隐藏状态。然而，基于滤波器的参数化内在维度可能小于完整滤波向量的维度，这种确定性冗余会导致Bellman回归出现秩亏缺（rank deficiency），影响算法的可解性和数值稳定性。作者希望通过刻画这一内在维度并消除冗余，来解决回归秩亏缺问题并降低参数数量。

Method: 本文首先从理论上刻画了滤波向量参数化的内在维度：对于SIMO系统，传统滤波向量仅包含2n个独立分量；对于一般MIMO系统，包含n(m+1)个独立分量。基于这一特性分析，作者直接从输入-输出数据中提取精简滤波向量，并在此基础上推导出精简的无模型输出反馈策略迭代（policy iteration）和值迭代（value iteration）方程，去除了冗余方向，减少了待估计的未知参数数量，同时保持了完全基于输入-输出数据的实现方式。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了秩降低的效果（即消除了Bellman回归的秩亏缺问题）以及所学习控制器的有效性。结果表明精简后的算法在减少参数数量的同时仍能有效学习出合适的控制器。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了输出反馈LQR中滤波向量参数化的内在维度结构，指出传统方法中存在确定性冗余，并提出了精简化的无模型强化学习算法来消除这种冗余，在降低计算复杂度的同时保证了控制性能。

Abstract: This article studies model-free output feedback linear quadratic regulation (LQR) for continuous-time linear systems with an $n$-dimensional state, an $m$-dimensional input, and a $p$-dimensional output, using filtered input--output data. Since the system state is unavailable, existing methods rely on dynamic filters to parameterize the hidden state using measurable input--output signals. However, the intrinsic dimension of the resulting filter-based parametrization can be smaller than the dimension of the complete filtered vector, and this deterministic redundancy can make the Bellman regressions rank deficient. We characterize this intrinsic dimension and show that the conventional filtered vector contains only $2n$ independent components for single-input multi-output (SIMO) systems and $n(m+1)$ independent components for general multi-input multi-output (MIMO) systems. Based on this characterization, a reduced filtered vector is extracted directly from data and used to develop reduced model-free output feedback policy iteration and value iteration equations, eliminating the redundant directions and decreasing the number of unknown parameters while retaining a fully input--output data-based implementation. A numerical example illustrates the rank reduction and the effectiveness of the learned controller.

</details>


### [178] [Disturbance-Observer-Based Grid-Forming Control for Unbalanced Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11857)
*Juho Määttä,Marko Hinkkanen,Tuure Nurminen,Orcun Karaca,Rayane Mourouvin,Jarno Kukkola,Lennart Harnefors*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于不平衡电网电压条件下的构网型控制方法，通过调节正序有功功率并主动抑制负序变换器电压，在不平衡故障期间保持电压幅值恒定。该方法仅需测量交流侧变换器电流，利用扰动观测器实现同步并提供积分和谐振作用，同时集成电流限制方案，并通过12.5-kVA变换器实验验证了其在严重平衡和不平衡故障下均能正常工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构网型变换器在不平衡电网电压条件下运行时面临挑战，传统控制方法难以在不平衡故障期间同时实现稳定同步、电压幅值恒定以及电流限制，需要一种更鲁棒的控制策略。

Method: 提出构网型控制方法，调节正序有功功率并主动抑制负序变换器电压；仅测量交流侧变换器电流，使用扰动观测器进行同步并提供积分与谐振作用；从扰动观测器获得正负序电网电压估计；集成适用于平衡与不平衡故障的电流限制方案。

Result: 提供了全面的稳定性分析和整定指南；12.5-kVA变换器实验结果表明该方法能够在严重平衡和不平衡故障下正常运行。

Conclusion: 所提出的构网型控制方法通过扰动观测器实现了无需外部电压测量即可在不平衡电网条件下稳定运行，并在变换器物理极限内保持电压幅值恒定，具备故障电流限制能力，实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This article proposes a grid-forming control method for operation under unbalanced grid-voltage conditions. The method regulates the positive-sequence active power delivered to the grid and actively suppresses the negative-sequence converter voltage, controlling the voltage magnitude to be constant also during unbalanced faults when within the physical limits of the converter. Only the converter current is measured on the AC side, and a disturbance observer is used for synchronization as well as providing integral and resonant action. Estimates for the positive- and negative-sequence grid voltage are obtained from the disturbance observer. A current-limitation scheme for both balanced and unbalanced faults is integrated. Comprehensive stability analysis and tuning guidelines are provided. Experimental results using a 12.5-kVA converter demonstrate that the proposed method can operate during severe balanced and unbalanced faults.

</details>


### [179] [Coordinated Dynamic Operation of Integrated Electrolyzer-Compressor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11929)
*Amin Salehi,Janne Seppanen,Mahdi Pourakbari-Kasmaei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对电解槽与电动压缩机站（EDCS）集成系统的动态协调控制问题，提出了动态模型与PID控制方案。通过线性化模型设计两种PID控制器（保守型和快速跟踪型），在四种不同扰动工况下验证了系统性能，证明该框架能保障集成系统的暂态稳定性和运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力与氢能部门的耦合日益紧密，电解槽与EDCS的协调运行在暂态扰动下尤为重要，但两者耦合子系统间的动态交互作用尚未被充分研究。

Method: 首先推导电解槽和EDCS的线性化模型以支持系统化控制器设计；随后设计两种PID控制器（分别代表保守型和快速跟踪型设计）来协调系统响应；最后在四种不同案例下对协调模型进行验证。

Result: 在EDCS扰动下，控制电解槽流量可协调系统动态并抑制不良暂态波动；在电解槽扰动下，调节EDCS转矩可消除压力、流量和转速的不一致响应，并防止危险的暂态欠冲和超冲。四种案例均验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够保证集成电解槽-EDCS系统的暂态稳定性和运行可靠性，填补了该类耦合系统动态协调控制研究的空白。

Abstract: The increasing interaction between power and hydrogen sectors highlights the importance of coordinated operation of electrolyzers and electric-driven compressor stations (EDCSs). This becomes particularly of higher importance under transient disturbances. However, coordinated dynamic interactions of these coupled subsystems remain largely unexplored. This article addresses such gap by developing a dynamic model for an integrated electrolyzer-EDCS system and designing appropriate PID control schemes to address the potential disturbances affecting either component. To this end, linearized models of the electrolyzer and EDCS are first derived to enable systematic controller design. Then, two PID controllers, representing conservative and fast-tracking designs, are developed to coordinate the system response. The developed coordinated model is examined and verified under four different cases. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed model under disturbances from the compressor driver or the electrolyzer. Controlling the electrolyzer flow in response to EDCS disturbances coordinates system dynamics and mitigates undesirable transient fluctuations. Conversely, under electrolyzer disturbances, regulating the EDCS torque eliminates inconsistent responses in pressure, flow, and rotational speed, while preventing hazardous transient undershoots and overshoots. Overall, the proposed framework guarantees transient stability and operational reliability of the integrated electrolyzer-EDCS system.

</details>


### [180] [Distributed Nash Equilibrium Seeking with Logarithmic Bit Rates over Digital Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12022)
*Zihao Ren,Chengyang Jiang,Lei Wang,Yang Liu,Kemi Ding*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式纳什均衡求解问题，引入量化技术以指数级降低数字信道上的通信比特率。基于终极有界性量化器的通用框架，作者提出了带时变缩放误差状态量化的无源性纳什均衡求解算法（PBA-TEQ），在充分条件下实现线性收敛，并建立了通信复杂度的下界为每个传输log₂(O(nd))比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式纳什均衡求解算法需要代理间通过通信网络进行迭代消息交换，其计算复杂度严重依赖数字信道上的通信开销。为降低通信负担，需要开发通信缩减机制，使算法在更低的比特率下仍能保持收敛性能。

Method: 提出基于稀疏化和均匀量化的终极有界性量化器通用框架，并在此基础上设计了PBA-TEQ（无源性纳什均衡求解算法结合时变缩放误差状态量化）。对于标量化量化器和贪婪量化器两种具体实例，在PBA-TEQ框架下建立通信复杂度下界分析。

Result: 在充分条件下，PBA-TEQ算法可实现线性收敛。当采用标量化量化器或贪婪量化器时，每个传输的通信复杂度下界为log₂(O(nd))比特，其中n为代理数，d为决策状态维度，可实现无偏线性收敛。数值仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 通过所提出的终极有界性量化器框架和PBA-TEQ算法，分布式纳什均衡求解能在指数级降低通信比特率的同时保持线性收敛，为网络博弈中的高效分布式求解提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: This paper introduces quantization techniques to reduce the communication complexity in the distributed Nash equilibrium (NE) seeking problem, achieving an exponential reduction in bit rates over digital channels. The goal of distributed NE seeking algorithms is to coordinate agents in a network game toward equilibrium through iterative message exchanges among them via a communication network. The computational complexity of this distributed algorithm critically depends on network communication overhead in the digital channel, motivating the development of communication reduction mechanism. Regarding this, we proposed some quantizers based on sparsification and uniform quantization through a general class of ultimate-boundedness-based quantizers. Based on this, we propose a Passivity-Based NE seeking Algorithm with Time-varying scaling Error state Quantization (PBA-TEQ), and show that the linear convergence can be achieved under a sufficient condition. Moreover, when employing either the scalarization quantizer or the greedy quantizer, both belonging to the ultimate-boundedness-based quantizers, within the PBA-TEQ framework, we establish a lower bound on communication complexity of $\log_2(\mathcal{O}(nd))$ bit rates per transmission to achieve unbiased linear convergence, with $n$ being the number of agents and $d$ being the dimension of the decision state of the network game. Numerical simulation examples are provided to validate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [181] [Network Topology Reconfiguration: Optimal Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12047)
*Basel Morsy,Jochen Stiasny,Adolfo Anta,Jochen Cremer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出最优过渡规划（OTP）问题，用于解决网络拓扑重构（NTR）中从当前运行点到目标运行点的可行过渡路径规划问题。通过滚动时域框架结合DC规划器和AC可行性滤波器，生成满足AC潮流方程和热限制的过渡序列，在PGLib-OPF系统上实现最高18.4%的运行成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NTR方法只识别目标运行点（快照层面），但未解决如何安全地从当前状态过渡到目标状态的问题。由于每个拓扑结构有各自的可行调度区域，简单的切换或重新调度可能使中间运行点超出热限，因此需要规划一条每一步都满足AC潮流可行性约束的过渡路径。

Method: 提出OTP问题以联合优化开关序列和调度轨迹，要求每个中间点满足AC可行性。采用滚动时域（receding-horizon）框架求解：DC规划器提出轨迹，通过AC可行性滤波器进行认证；对不可行拓扑使用可复用的组合割（combinatorial cuts）进行排除。

Result: 在高达1354节点的拥挤PGLib-OPF测试系统上进行案例研究，该方法能够生成AC可行的过渡路径，与无开关的ACOPF解相比，运行成本降低最高达18.4%，且可在普通商用硬件上求解。

Conclusion: 本文首次形式化了NTR中的过渡路径规划问题，填补了快照NTR与可行执行之间的空白。所提出的滚动时域方法能在实际规模系统上高效生成AC可行的低成本过渡方案，为NTR的实际工程应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Network topology reconfiguration (NTR) can reduce power system operating costs by co-optimizing generation dispatch and substation switching, but the reported savings describe a target operating point rather than a way to reach it. Reaching this operating point requires a sequence of intermediate operating points, each satisfying the AC power flow equations and thermal limits; because each topology admits its own feasible dispatch region, a naive transition that switches or redispatches first can drive intermediate flows past their thermal limits. Existing methods leave this gap open: snapshot NTR identifies a target but not a route. We formulate the Optimal Transition Planning (OTP) problem, co-optimizing the switching sequence and dispatch trajectory subject to AC feasibility at every intermediate point. We solve this problem with a receding-horizon framework: a DC planner proposes a trajectory that is certified against an AC feasibility filter, and infeasible topologies are excluded using reusable combinatorial cuts. Case studies on congested PGLib-OPF systems up to 1354-bus show that the method produces AC-feasible transitions that reduce operating cost by up to 18.4% compared to the no-switching ACOPF solution on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [182] [NetlistBench: Evaluating LLM Reliability in SPICE Netlist Recognition and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12197)
*Jiarui Ma,Jianghan Wang,Yuheng Ma,Ziyi Zhuang,Xiaoguang Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了NetlistBench，一个面向SPICE网表识别与操作的、结构验证的基准测试集。包含2,342个案例、24个任务族，覆盖参数/连接性识别、编辑、层次化操作、等价性判断及长程复合编辑。通过确定性结构感知oracle评估模型输出，发现六个非思考型LLM在简单局部编辑上表现优异（96%-100%），但在器件添加（41%-83%）和等价性判断（49%-90%）上显著下降；启用推理虽提升弱模型表现，但无法消除结构保持失败，且随编辑长度增加性能急剧退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在电路设计工作流中应用日益广泛，但其对模拟器面向的SPICE网表识别与操作的可靠性缺乏系统研究，且很少与高层设计推理分离。网表虽为文本形式，却通过拓扑和参数编码了结构化电路对象，现有基准未能针对性评估这一结构化文本理解能力。

Method: 构建NetlistBench基准，包含2,342个案例、24个任务族，涵盖参数与连接性识别与编辑、层次化操作、等价性判断和长程复合编辑。采用确定性结构感知oracle进行输出评估，并在六个非思考型LLM上进行系统测试，同时对比启用推理前后的表现。

Result: 不同操作的结构复杂度导致模型性能差异显著：简单局部编辑准确率达96%-100%，器件添加降至41%-83%，等价性判断为49%-90%。启用推理能显著提升较弱模型，但无法消除结构保持失败，且随编辑步数增加性能急剧退化。

Conclusion: NetlistBench揭示了网表可靠性是可信LLM电路设计自动化中的独立瓶颈，说明现有LLM在SPICE网表的结构化操作上仍存在系统性短板，值得专门研究与改进。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in circuit design workflows, yet their reliability on simulator-facing SPICE netlist recognition and manipulation remains poorly understood and is rarely separated from high-level design reasoning. Although netlists are textual, they encode structured circuit objects through topology and parameters. We present \textbf{NetlistBench}, a structure-verified benchmark for SPICE netlist recognition and manipulation. NetlistBench contains 2,342 cases across 24 task families, covering parameter and connectivity recognition and edits, hierarchical operations, equivalence judgment, and long-horizon compound editing. Model outputs are evaluated by a deterministic structure-aware oracle. Across six non-thinking LLMs, performance varies substantially with operation-level structural complexity. Simple local edits reach $96\%$--$100\%$ accuracy, while device addition drops to $41\%$--$83\%$ and equivalence judgment to $49\%$--$90\%$. Enabling reasoning substantially improves weaker models but does not eliminate structure-preservation failures, with performance still degrading sharply as the edit horizon increases. NetlistBench identifies netlist reliability as a distinct bottleneck for trustworthy LLM-based circuit design automation.

</details>


### [183] [SelectLight: Learning to Select Signal Plans Generated by Distributed Model Predictive Control for Urban Traffic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12256)
*Lyuzhou Luo,Chaopeng Tan,Zhengyong Gao,Hong Zhu,Andrea D'Ariano,Keshuang Tang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出SelectLight框架，将多目标分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）生成的候选信号方案与多智能体强化学习（MARL）的选择策略相结合，在满足交通信号控制约束的前提下，通过在线学习实现更优的方案选择。在两个28交叉口SUMO网络上验证了其卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）虽然能在线构造满足操作约束的可行信号方案，但其在选择折中解时依赖预设规则，无法从实际闭环运行结果中学习优化。这限制了信号控制系统的自适应能力和性能提升空间。

Method: SelectLight将后优化选择机制引入DMPC：1）使用状态剪枝多目标动态规划（SP-MODP）结合Newellian点-空间排队模型，生成有界且互不支配的候选信号方案集（针对总排队延迟、峰值排队累积和总停车次数三个目标）；2）采用拓扑感知注意力策略，通过独立近端策略优化（IPPO）训练，从每个可变大小的候选集中选择未修改的方案。学习仅局限于候选选择，保留了预设的信号时序约束，选中的方案及其预测目标折中可审计。

Result: 在两个28交叉口SUMO网络上的实验表明：SelectLight实现了最佳延迟相关性能，且优势随需求增加而扩大。在2倍基线需求下，排队延迟和等待时间分别比最强基线降低5.57%和6.44%。在所有需求偏移测试下，其迁移损失最低。在120秒预测时域下，每个交叉口的SP-MODP第99百分位求解时间为5.408毫秒，远低于5秒的控制间隔。

Conclusion: 将MARL学习能力嵌入DMPC的方案选择环节，而非替代整个控制过程，可以在保持可行性和可解释性的同时获得学习带来的性能提升。该方法既保持了信号时序约束的完整性，又通过强化学习提升了方案选择的适应性，且计算效率满足实时控制要求。

Abstract: Coordinated traffic signal control across urban networks must adapt to changing demand while satisfying operational constraints. Multi-objective distributed model predictive control (DMPC) can construct feasible signal plans online, but prescribed rules for selecting among trade-off solutions cannot learn from realized closed-loop outcomes. We propose SelectLight, which implements post-optimization selection by allowing a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) policy to choose directly from plans generated online by DMPC. At each control update, state-pruned multi-objective dynamic programming (SP-MODP) evaluates plans with a Newellian point--spatial queue model and returns a bounded set of mutually nondominated candidate signal plans for total queueing delay, peak queue accumulation, and total number of stops. A topology-aware attention policy trained with independent proximal policy optimization (IPPO) selects one unmodified plan from each variable-size set. This confines learning to candidate selection, preserves the prescribed signal timing constraints, and leaves the selected plan and its predicted objective trade-offs available for inspection. Experiments on two 28-intersection SUMO networks show that SelectLight achieves the best delay-related performance and that its advantage widens with demand. At twice the baseline demand, it reduces queueing delay and waiting time by 5.57% and 6.44%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline. SelectLight also incurs the lowest transfer loss under every tested demand shift. With a 120 s prediction horizon, the per-intersection 99th-percentile SP-MODP solution time is 5.408 ms, well below the 5 s control interval.

</details>


### [184] [Digital Twin Networks for 6G Wireless Systems: Architecture, Enabling Technologies, Intelligent Control, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12267)
*Charalampos Oikonomidis,Emmanouel T. Michailidis,Nikolaos I. Miridakis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对第六代（6G）移动网络中的数字孪生网络（DTN）架构进行了系统性的综述，将现有DTN架构正式分类为被动监控孪生和主动控制孪生两大类，深入评估了射线追踪、可重构智能表面、人工智能和移动边缘计算等使能技术，并首次对现有解决方案进行了详细的数学和计算复杂度分析以评估硬件可扩展性和推理瓶颈。文章将各架构与智慧城市、工业5.0、医疗和智能电网等6G用例相关联，最后总结了GPU硬件限制、网络物理执行延迟和零信任安全等关键未解决挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络的转型需要主动且确定性的编排来满足未来服务的严格性能指标（如超可靠低延迟通信、增强移动宽带和大规模机器类通信）。尽管数字孪生网络已被认为是满足这些需求的基础技术，但现有文献仅停留在概念层面的探讨，缺乏对DTN架构的技术分类和计算可行性评估，这一研究空白正是本文的出发点。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，首先对最先进的DTN架构进行正式分类（被动监控孪生和主动控制孪生）；其次对使能技术（射线追踪、可重构智能表面、AI、移动边缘计算）进行深入评估；然后对现有解决方案进行详细的数学和计算复杂度分析，以评估硬件可扩展性和推理瓶颈；最后将架构映射到6G用例并综合未解决的挑战。

Result: 本文成功建立了DTN架构的正式分类体系，揭示了被动监控孪生与主动控制孪生之间的本质差异及其适配场景；通过数学和复杂度分析识别了硬件扩展和推理瓶颈；并将各类架构与智慧城市、工业5.0、医疗、智能电网等具体6G用例建立了对应关系。

Conclusion: 文章指出实现万物互联愿景仍面临三大关键挑战：GPU硬件限制、网络物理执行延迟以及零信任安全范式的缺失，并据此提出了针对性的战略研究方向，为未来6G数字孪生网络的发展提供了指引。

Abstract: The transition to the Sixth Generation (6G) of mobile networks requires proactive and deterministic orchestration to satisfy the stringent key performance indicators of future services, including ultra-reliable low-latency communications, enhanced mobile broadband, and massive machine-type communications. Digital Twin Networks (DTN) have recently emerged as a foundational technology to meet these demands, offering real-time and high-fidelity virtual replicas of the physical network. Although the current literature explores DTNs conceptually, a gap exists in the coverage of technical classification and computational feasibility evaluations. This survey addresses this gap by formally categorizing state-of-the-art DTN architectures into passive monitoring twins and active control twins. We provide an in-depth evaluation of their underlying enabling technologies, specifically ray-tracing, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces, artificial intelligence, and mobile edge computing. Importantly, this paper conducts a detailed mathematical and computational complexity analysis of state-of-the-art solutions to assess hardware scalability and inference bottlenecks. These architectures are then linked to various forthcoming 6G use cases, including smart cities, Industry 5.0, healthcare, and smart grids. Finally, we synthesize crucial unresolved challenges, highlighting graphics processing unit hardware limitations, cyber-physical actuation latency, and the need for a zero-trust security paradigm, offering strategic research directions to realize the unified internet of everything.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出经验编排者（EO）作为控制理论治理层，解决LLM代理在目标对立时对话崩溃的问题。在金融服务的模拟环境中，EO通过上下文土匪（CB）、PID控制器和POMDP信念追踪三种机制共同作用，将高意图顾问联系率从46.1%提升至78.1%（+32个百分点），其中CB变体选择贡献了97%的因素间结果方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个目标结构对立的LLM代理进行多轮交互时，缺乏共享目标函数会导致对话崩溃而非竞争：访客妥协、站点代理停止调整方法、对话以双方均未达成目标告终。研究希望探索是否可以用控制理论治理层来替代缺失的目标函数。

Method: EO通过三种机制治理对话轨迹：1）上下文土匪（CB）根据真实网页分析数据校准和选择内容变体；2）PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制行为一致性；3）POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。方法在模拟金融服务环境中进行了60,000次仿真验证。

Result: 60,000次仿真中，EO实现高意图顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1%对46.1%），CB变体选择解释了97%的因素间结果方差。人物层面分析揭示两个不同机制：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是功能性系统与非功能性系统的分界；对已接近一致的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认行为已基本足够。

Conclusion: 治理策略而非环境初始条件决定了轨迹终点。但所有发现均基于LLM对LLM模拟，PID控制器未针对真实人类不可预测性进行校准，在实时流量上验证EO是关键的下一步。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [186] [AgonAlpha: Autonomous Alpha Discovery via Prompt Economy and Scalable Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11250)
*Weicheng Ye,Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Shunyao Yu,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgonAlpha 是一个面向量化交易因子挖掘（alpha mining）的自主研究系统，它不直接搜索公式，而是搜索"冻结的研究工件"（假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、理由和评审状态）。系统结合了验证工件搜索、具备重新执行和否决权的新鲜上下文对抗评审器，以及考虑待处理任务的并行预算分配，并提供完整的公开证据链。在 WorldQuant BRAIN 上的独立部署中，该系统在五个用户和六个模型后端上成功产出 SPECTACULAR 级别的 alpha，Fitness 达到 9.50，Sharpe 达到 3.48，且每个提交都保留了从提示到表达式的完整溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型可以提出大量看似合理的交易因子，但一个自主研究系统还需要解决三个关键问题：(1) 如何合理分配评估预算；(2) 如何验证自身产出的证据；(3) 如何保留每个候选因子的产生过程。作者认为仅搜索公式是不够的，需要搜索包含元信息的完整研究工件，以确保研究的可追溯性和可靠性。

Method: AgonAlpha 的核心架构包含四个要素：(1) 在冻结的研究工件空间中进行搜索，而非仅搜索公式；(2) 使用新鲜上下文的对抗性评审器，该评审器具有重新执行实验和否决候选的权限；(3) 采用待处理感知（pending-aware）的并行预算分配策略；(4) 维护完整的公开证据轨迹，确保每个提交都有从提示到表达式的完整溯源（provenance）。

Result: 系统在 WorldQuant BRAIN 平台上经过五个独立用户和六个不同模型后端的部署验证，成功产生了 SPECTACULAR 等级的 alpha 信号，其中 Fitness 得分达到 9.50，Sharpe 比率达到 3.48，并且所有提交都保留了完整的 prompt-to-expression 溯源记录。

Conclusion: AgonAlpha 证明了"搜索研究工件而非仅搜索公式"这一架构思路的有效性。通过结合对抗性评审机制和预算感知的并行分配，自主交易因子研究系统不仅能产出高质量的结果，还能确保产出的可验证性和可追溯性，为可信赖的自动化量化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Language models can propose many plausible trading factors, but an autonomous research system must also allocate its evaluation budget, verify its own evidence, and preserve how each candidate was produced. We present AgonAlpha, an architecture that searches over frozen research artifacts---hypotheses, executable expressions, platform evidence, rationales, and review status---rather than formulas alone. To our knowledge, AgonAlpha is the first alpha-mining system to combine verified artifact search, a fresh-context adversarial reviewer with re-execution and veto authority, and pending-aware parallel budget allocation, together with a complete public evidence trail. Independent deployments on WorldQuant BRAIN produced SPECTACULAR-grade alphas across five users and six model backends, with Fitness reaching 9.50 and Sharpe reaching 3.48, while retaining prompt-to-expression provenance for every submission.

</details>


### [187] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distribird是一个智能体网络应用，用于自动化从科学文献中构建贝叶斯模型参数的信息性先验分布。它通过多智能体pipeline搜索文献、提取并加权报告值、进行AIC模型选择来拟合概率分布，在无文献时回退到非信息性替代方案，并记录每个先验的证据来源和置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管贝叶斯校准需要每个模型参数有先验分布，研究者几乎总是使用均匀先验，因为从科学文献构建信息性先验既缓慢又需要同时具备领域和统计专业知识。作者旨在自动化这一过程，降低使用信息性先验的门槛。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体pipeline：(1)根据参数名、物理描述和领域上下文搜索文献；(2)提取并按照领域相关性加权报告值；(3)通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。当无文献可用时回退到非信息性替代方案，并明确报告证据来源和置信水平。内置有效性层拒绝超出范围的请求，所有语言模型调用均在本地运行。

Result: 在10个科学领域的24个参数上评估了三个开源模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B），与单提示LLM基线相比，完整pipeline在先验质量上与之持平。但pipeline优势在于：每个先验可追溯到具体论文、内置有效性层在30个模型-参数案例中有11个拒绝无根据的越界请求（而基线会自信地返回无依据先验），且所有语言模型调用本地运行保护隐私。

Conclusion: 作者认为对于科学研究而言，可追溯性、有效性和隐私保护比边际性的点估计精度提升更重要。Distribird的价值不仅在于匹配基线质量的先验生成，更在于其提供了可靠、透明且安全的先验构建途径。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [188] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一个自主AI研究代理系统性地攻击Conway的99图问题（即判定参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在），在部分学分指标下取得可验证的贡献，包括循环图约束上限证明、强制结构约简、自同构轨道存在性框架，以及最优验证工件69.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway的99图问题是组合数学和代数图论中的著名开放问题，该问题询问参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在。由于暴力搜索不可行，需要系统性、可验证的推理方法。该论文试图通过自主AI研究代理来推进这一问题，同时探索部分证明/近似证明在严格验证下的价值。

Method: 论文采用四种互补策略：(1)穷举证明Z/99上的循环图最多满足68.0%的约束（33/49个差分类），并对另一个99阶阿贝尔群得到相同上限；(2)利用λ=1使每个邻域为完美匹配、μ=2使外部顶点与未匹配邻对双射的强制结构约简，将问题转化为84顶点上的12-正则图，并使用CP-SAT编码，通过恢复唯一srg(9,4,1,2)验证正确性；(3)建立并验证了预设自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上检验；(4)获得最优验证工件69.43%，并收集十四个不同方法均未超过此边界的证据。

Result: 主要结果：(1)对两个99阶阿贝尔群上的循环图，穷举证明不能超过68.0%（33/49差分类）的约束满足率；(2)完成了从原问题到84顶点12-正则图的约简并验证；(3)建立了自同构轨道存在性框架并通过已知图验证；(4)获得69.43%的最佳验证工件，且14种方法均未超越此边界。由于任何可证明的低于4950的界都是非存在性证明，这些结果与开放问题紧密相关。

Conclusion: 该论文展示了自主AI研究代理在推进经典数学难题方面的能力，提供了多个可验证的部分结果，并确立了69.43%作为鲁棒的前沿边界。虽然未最终解决Conway的99图问题，但其系统性的部分证明框架为未来工作提供了坚实基础，且任何突破此边界的可证明结果可能直接导向非存在性证明。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [189] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)路由不连续性问题，具体考察4-bit KV-cache量化对专家选择决策的影响。通过四重运行装置量化了路由介导的损坏比例(RMF约0.31)，发现部署路由器裕度可以检测专家翻转发生(AUC 0.772)但无法区分有害与有益翻转，存在经验性的收益检测障碍。跨模型验证了符号翻转代价和符号不可分离性，而门控归一化约定的作用被重新界定为损坏幅度调节器而非路由可恢复性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由函数不连续，部署中常见的数值扰动（如4-bit KV-cache量化）会使token跨越决策边界，导致专家选择翻转。理解这种路由介导的量化损坏比例及其可检测性是关键动机。

Method: 采用四重运行装置：(1)发现、(2)过程复制、(3)预注册重执行、(4)预注册留出验证。使用token级归因分解路由介导分数，预注册探针跨三个架构验证。设计了受控同检查点flag交换实验，并对比模拟量化与真实int4 KV内核。

Result: 在OLMoE-1B-7B上RMF约0.31（多个运行间一致）；路由裕度检测翻转AUC 0.772但无法区分有害/有益翻转（机会水平）；跨模型验证符号翻转代价和符号不可分离性；真实int4内核产生兼容但统计功效不足的分数(95% CI [-0.111, 0.394])。

Conclusion: 路由介导的量化损坏约占三分之一且不可忽略；在局部可观测路由器统计特征族内存在经验性收益检测障碍，限制了选择性修复；门控归一化约定是损坏幅度调节器而非路由可恢复性机制。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [190] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LLM多智能体社会模拟加速的方法：用从少量廉价查询中拟合出的低参数模型替代每个LLM智能体，从而在任何智能体数量N下都能在笔记本上运行模拟。作者引入[交互阶数×记忆]分类法，将感知和记忆映射到有效理论和代理误差的N趋势预测，并在EconAgent及七个LLM模拟中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟大量LLM智能体组成的社会成本高昂，但通常提出的问题都是宏观层面的（相行为、程式化事实、随N的标度），而非单个智能体的认知。作者希望利用统计物理的观察——用低参数模型替代每个LLM智能体——来大幅降低模拟成本。

Method: 每个LLM智能体被替换为从几百到几千次廉价查询拟合出的低参数模型；是否奏效在模拟运行前就由每个智能体的感知决定。引入[交互阶数×记忆]分类法，将感知和记忆映射到有效理论及代理误差的N趋势预测，并在真实LLM采集的决策（主要来自DeepSeek，成本仅几美元）上验证。

Result: 在EconAgent（LLM宏观经济）的忠实复现及另外七个LLM模拟上，预测的误差趋势逐格成立；两个被反驳的预测都出现在强饱和响应上并追溯到其曲率，但理论仍以无自由参数的方式定量匹配这些结果。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，通过统计物理视角可以将昂贵的大规模LLM社会模拟转化为廉价的低参数代理模拟，且误差趋势可预测、可验证，为规模化LLM社会研究提供了一种经济高效的途径。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [191] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试，用于评估AI编码智能体在开放式研究中自主改进世界模型的能力。该基准涵盖8个游戏环境，使用统一的结构化状态表示，在固定计算预算下让前沿编码智能体改进提供的世界模型起始代码。在64个会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63个会话中取得了改进，其中91%的获胜修改是非平凡的科研型改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模是一个尚未定型的领域，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间交互复杂，没有统一方案适用于所有环境。这使其成为AI编码智能体作为自主研究者的理想测试平台，因为改进方向事先并不明确，与现有智能体基准中占主导地位的工程到规格任务不同。

Method: 构建AutoWorldModel-Bench基准：包含8个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示（从每个游戏中提取地真实体状态，通过共享张量格式消费），从而隔离动力学建模与感知问题，实现分钟级迭代。基准为闭环设置，前沿编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进提供的世界模型起始代码。

Result: 在64个会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63个会话中改进了起始模型；在91%的会话中，获胜修改是非平凡的科研型改动（新目标函数、新表示、新滚动程序或架构变化），而非超参数调整。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench提供了一个新的评估场景，使前沿编码智能体能够在开放式研究任务上进行评估，而非仅限工程到规格的问题，填补了现有智能体基准的空白。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [192] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead 是一种针对复制型潜在状态存储的寻址与读取机制，通过基于词片段的键控标签集合进行路由，并在硬注意力掩码下独立解码所选片段，使得后置查询能够选择性读取已合并的缓存片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立的潜在空间推理智能体可以通过共享键值缓存片段来协作，但合并后的缓存存在一个关键问题：片段共存导致相互干扰，共存并不等于可寻址性。编码时未知的后续查询无法可靠地从合并缓存中读取所需内容。

Method: MaSRead 将读取操作定位到内容本身：通过从片段词汇派生的不透明键控标签集合进行路由，并在硬注意力掩码下解码每个选中的片段以隐藏其余内容。在词汇连通性条件下，通过图遍历可到达多跳查询所需的片段。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、枢纽和自然语言存储等各类场景中，MaSRead 能隔离地恢复已访问片段，在无关片段累积时仍保持有效，并可迁移到另一模型家族。路由后的物化解码复杂度取决于片段长度而非存储总大小；端到端工作量包含与存储相关的路由和每个访问片段的单次读取。

Conclusion: MaSRead 证明了当所需片段与查询通过内容连接时，复制型潜在存储可通过内容实现选择性可读。其局限性明确：词汇路由可能遗漏不连通的证据，答案组合仍受制于冻结的阅读器。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [193] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAPO，一种段级自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式等段，基于top-5和bottom-5样例进行针对性改进，在多个基准和模型上优于现有强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化（APO）方法通常整体性地重写提示，导致改善一个行为的同时可能损害其他行为，缺乏对提示段级细粒度的诊断和优化。

Method: SAPO将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个部分，利用单个LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出进行分段、弱点分析和候选生成。优化流程包含训练/验证协议和两阶段生成过程：(1)段级诊断与推荐提取；(2)基于强弱段信号约束的候选合成。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K五个基准上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini进行评估，SAPO取得了最佳平均得分，超越了Zero-shot及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA和StraGO等强APO基线。

Conclusion: 段级提示优化比整体重写提示更有效，通过识别具体段落的弱点并进行针对性改进，能够避免整体重写带来的行为退化，实现更优且更稳定的提示优化效果。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [194] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了P&ID Pilot——一个端到端的AI管线，用于自动生成工艺流程图（PFD）并将其转换为管道仪表图（P&ID）。该管线结合了遗传算法（GA）和大语言模型（LLM）进行PFD合成，并使用LLM代理通过受限工程软件开发工具包将PFD转换为可执行的P&ID，实现了100%的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前PFD的创建及其向P&ID的转化主要依赖人工操作，耗时费力且成本高昂。引入人工智能有望实现流程自动化、节省时间，并通过探索多种拓扑方案和减少人工劳动来带来经济效益。

Method: 采用两阶段管线：第一阶段通过对比四种方法（包含遗传算法GA和大语言模型LLM的混合方法），选出最优的PFD合成方法；第二阶段利用基于LLM的代理，通过受限的工程软件开发工具包，对生成的PFD执行验证过的修改，将其转化为P&ID。

Result: GA+LLM混合方法在四种方法中取得了最低的损失值，生成的PFD拓扑满足出口流量参数且无工程规则违规。第二阶段的LLM代理实现了100%的执行成功率，同时保持了与领域特定规则和参考图结构的一致性。

Conclusion: GA/LLM驱动的合成与LLM转换代理相结合的统一管线，为端到端的工艺设计自动化提供了可行路径，能够生成经过验证、可部署的输出，并大幅减少人工工程工作量。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [195] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于"影响因素"（Influences）新概念的概念框架，用于支持仿真活动（simulation campaigns）的迭代和增量式优化，从而加深对信息物理系统（CPS）行为在环境中介交互下的理解，并在Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真的移动机器人案例中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统（CPS）由多个利益相关者开发，其行为由各领域制品与环境交互产生。仿真/联合仿真是分析CPS行为的关键手段，但一些环境中介交互（超出直接传感与驱动的部分）因复杂性、时间或领域经验缺乏而未被建模，导致仿真结果难以被正确理解和利用。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，引入"影响因素"（Influences）这一新概念，以支持仿真活动的迭代和增量式精化（refinement），帮助开发者逐步深化对系统行为的理解。

Result: 通过一个基于Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真的移动机器人案例研究，展示了所提方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Influences的概念框架能够有效弥补未建模的环境中介交互带来的理解缺口，支持仿真活动的渐进式完善，有助于更深入理解和利用CPS仿真结果。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [196] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向材料科学的终身AI伙伴框架，其核心是持久记忆而非特定智能体实现。该框架以可检查的事实和可执行的技能存储科学经验，支持检索、修订和跨模型迁移。在49个真实材料工具使用问题中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率近乎翻倍；在元素固态状态方程计算中，将失败转化为预防性护栏，错误率大幅降低；在13个实际材料仿真工作流中，记忆使token消耗减半、工具调用减少一半以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究的科学经验（脚本、协议、失败警告、判断力）对可复现性和知识传递至关重要，但这些经验通常分散在笔记本、仓库、任务日志和个人记忆中，难以跨AI智能体移植。作者旨在解决经验碎片化和不可移植的问题。

Method: 提出一个自我演进的记忆框架，将科学经验存储为可检查的事实和可执行的技能两类形式，使观察结果、失败边界、协议和验证检查能够在不同模型间检索、修订和迁移，而非绑定于特定智能体实现。

Result: 三个计算场景的评估：(1) 49个真实材料工具使用问题（138个可执行子任务）中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率几乎翻倍；(2) 元素固态状态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前护栏，结果从22/1/4提升到25/2/0（正确/部分/错误），避免92%的重复错误；(3) 13个实际材料仿真工作流中，记忆使总token负担减半，第三轮时工具调用减少超过2倍，同时保持带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析的物理意义。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆可以作为耐用的科学资产，是一种可移植、自我改进的材料研究经验记录，超越了任何单一模型或智能体栈的生命周期。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors.
